' vaḻakkiyalāṇi' yeṉṟataṉāṉ nītinūl viti piṟaḻakkūṟiṉ aḵtu irākkatam pōṉṟu kāṭṭumeṉpatu. ummiyāṟ kiḻattiyoṭu vaḻakkiyalāṇi yāṉaṉṟi maruṭṭik kūṟavum peṟumeṉpatu.
"aḻivilar muyalu mārva mākkaḷ
vaḻipaṭu teyvaṅ kaṭkaṇ ṭāaṅ
kalamaral varuttan tīra yāḻaniṉ
nalameṉ paṇittō ḷeytiṉa mākaliṟ
porippūm puṉki ṉeḻiṟṟaki yoṇmuṟi
cuṇaṅkaṇi vaṉamuli yaṇaṅkukoḷat timiri
niḻalkāṇ ṭōṟu neṭiya viki
maṇalkāṇ ṭōṟum vaṇṭa ṟii
varuntā tēkumati vāleyiṟ ṟōyē
mānaṉi koḻuti makiḻkuyi lālum
naṟuntaṇ poḻila kāṉaṅ
kuṟumpal lūrayāñ cellu māṟē"	(naṟ . 9)
eṉpataṉuḷ, 'varuntā tēkumati' yeṉavē, 'vaḻipaṭu teyvaṅ kaṭkaṇṭāl' viṭuvār illātatu pōla, niṉṉi viṭutal eṉakku aṟaṉaṉṟeṉak kūṟi melleṉac celkaveṉa maruṭṭik kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka.	(194)

kaṇṭōrum vaḻakkiyal āṇiyiṉ kiḷattal
507.	oḻintōr kiḷavi kiḻavaṉ kiḻattiyoṭu
	moḻintāṅku uriyar muṉṉatti ṉeṭuttē.
ituvuṅ kaṇṭōr kūṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mēliccūttirattuk kūṟiya talimakaṉiyun talimakaḷi yum oḻittu oḻintōreṉappaṭuvār kaṇṭōreṉappaṭupa. avar kūṟṟuṅ kiḻavaṉ kiḻattiyoṭu moḻintāṅku vaḻakkiyalāṇi (506)yiṟ kiḷattaṟ kum uriyar eṉpāṉ muṉṉatti ṉeṭuttuk kūṟupa eṉṟāṉeṉpatu.
muṉṉattiṉeṭutta leṉpatu atikārattāṉ aṟanūṟkut taka ōttiṉā ṉeṭutturippa veṉṟavāṟu ; avi,
" palavuṟu naṟuñcāntam paṭuppavark kallati
maliyuḷē piṟappiṉu malikkavitā meṉceyyum
niṉiyuṅkā ṉummaka ṇumakkumāṅ kaṉiyaṉē"	(kali . 9)
eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa.
ivvāṟu vaḻakkiyalāṇiyāṟ kiḷavātu vāḷātē avarik kaṇṭaṉameṉavum avariṉṉuḻip pukuvareṉavuñ colluvaṉa,
" kaṇṭōr moḻital kaṇṭa teṉpa"	(tol. cey. 193)
eṉpuḻik kūṟiṉām.	(195)

kēṭpōr ivar eṉal
508.	maṉiyōḷ kiḷaviyuṅ kiḻavaṉ kiḷaviyum
	niṉiyuṅ kālik kēṭkuna ravarē.
itu, kēṭpōreṉṉum uṟuppuṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) muṉṉark kūṟṟiṟkuriya reṉappaṭṭa paṉṉiruvaruṭ kēṭṭaṟkuriyār talimakaṉun talimakaḷum oḻittu oḻinta patiṉmarum e-ṟu.
avar kēṭpaṉavum avviruvar kiḷaviyumē yeṉavun tattaṅkiḷavi kēḷāreṉavuṅ kūṟiyavāṟu. 'maṉiyōḷ kiḷaviyuṅ kiḻavaṉ kiḷaviyu' meṉpataṉi mēṟpōyiṉa kūṟṟiṟkuṅ kūṭṭiyurikka. urikkavē kūṟuvaṉavuṅ kēṭpaṉavumellām avviruvar kiḷaviyumē yeṉavun tattaṅkiḷavi kūṟavuṅ kēṭkavum peṟāreṉavuṅ kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam vilakkappaṭṭaṉavaṟṟuḷ vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉavum mēṟkūṟu kiṉṟāṉ. maṟṟuk kūṟṟukku uriyareṉappaṭuvār kūṟa, avvayiṉ taliviyun talivaṉuṅ kēḷārōveṉiṉ, avi taṅ kiḷaviyākaliṉ tāṅkēṭṭal vitantōta vēṇṭuvataṉṟeṉpatu.
'niṉiyuṅkāli' yeṉṟataṉāṉ talimakaḷ collak kēṭ pōrun talimakaṉ collak kēṭpōrun talivaṉun taliviyun tammuṭ kēṭṭaṉavaṟṟukkuriyaṉavum ārāyntu koḷḷappaṭum mēṟ kūṟiya vakiyāṉeṉpatu. eṉavē, talimakaṉ kūṟap paratti kēṭṭalun talimakaḷ kūṟap paratti kēṭṭalum, mutalāyiṉa pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkiṟ keytātaṉa vilakkappaṭṭaṉa. oḻintaṉavaṟṟuṭ cila varumāṟu :
" viḷaṅkutoṭi muṉki vaḷintupuṟañ cuṟṟa
niṉmār paṭitali ṉiṉitā kiṉṟē"	(akam . 58)
eṉṉun talimakaḷ kūṟṟut talimakaṉ kēṭṭatu.
" varuntā tēkumati vāleyiṟ ṟōyē"	(naṟ . 9 )
eṉpatu, talimakaṉ kūṟṟu, talimakaḷ kēṭṭatu.
" niṉkēḷ,
putuvatu paṉṉāḷum pārāṭṭa yāṉu
mituvoṉ ṟuṭitteṉa veṇṇi"	(kali . 24)
eṉavum, 
" māliyu muḷḷā rāyiṟ kāli
yāṅkā kuvaṅkol pāṇa"	(akam . 14)
eṉavum,
" uyaṅkiṉ ṟaṉṉiyeṉ meyyeṉ ṟacii'"	(akam. 17)
eṉavum, 
"oṇṭā rakalamu muṇṇumō paliyē"	(kuṟun. 362)
eṉavum,
" ellīru meṉceytī reṉṉi nakutirō"	(kali . 142)
eṉavum iṉṉōraṉṉavellān 1tōḻiyum 2pāṇaṉuñ 3ceviliyuṅ 4kaṇṭōrum 5aṟivaruṅ kēṭpat talimakaḷ kūṟṟu vantaṉa. piṟavum aṉṉa.
"iṭikkuṅ kēḷir nuṅkuṟiyāka
. . . . . . . .. . . 
parantaṉ ṟinnōy nōṉṟukoḷaṟ karitē"	(kuṟun. 58)
eṉavum,
" eluva ciṟāa rēmuṟu naṇpa
. . . . . . . .. . . 
putukkōḷ yāṉiyiṟ piṇittaṟṟā lemmē"	(kuṟun. 129)
eṉavum
" ūrka pāka voruviṉi kaḻiya"	(akam. 44)
eṉavum, 
" pēramar maḻikkaṇiṉ ṟōḻi yuṟīiya
āraña revvaṅ kaḷiyā yōveṉa"
eṉavum,
" cāṉṟavir vāḻiyō cāṉṟavi reṉṟum"	(kali. 139)
eṉavum ivi 1pārppāṉum 2pāṅkaṉum 3pākaṉun 4tōḻiyuṅ 5kaṇṭōrum kēṭpōrākat talimakaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻntaṉa. piṟavum aṉṉa. iṉi,
" oṉṟit tōṉṟun tōḻi "	(tol. akat.  39)
eṉṟamiyiṉ, tōḻi kūṟṟun talimakaḷ (225) kūṟṟēyā meṉpatu.	(196)

pārppār aṟivar kūṟṟu yāvarum kēṭpar eṉal
509.	pārppār aṟivar eṉṟivar kiḷavi
	yārkkum variyār yāppoṭu puṇarntē.
ituvumatu.
(i-ḷ.) itu mēṟkūṟiya pārppārum aṟivarum kūṟiya kūṟṟuk kēṭpōri iṉṉāreṉṟu variyappaṭātu e-ṟu.
eṉṟavaḻi, 'yārkkum' eṉṟatu akattiṇiyōrkkum puṟattiṇi yōrkku meṉṟavāṟu. 'yārkkum variyā' reṉṟataṉi, eccappaṭuttuc cilarkku variyappaṭumeṉpatu koḷka. eṉṉi? pārppār kūṟṟu, talimakaṉuñ ceviliyum naṟṟāyuṅ kēṭpiṉallatu, piṟar kēṭṭaṟkēlā. aṟivar kūṟṟu, talimakaḷun tōḻiyuñ ceviliyum naṟṟāyuṅ kēṭṭaṟ kuriyar.
puṟattiṇikkaṇṇum potuviyaṟ karantiyōrkkum pārppār kiḷavi ēlāveṉavum, aṟivar kiḷavi ēṟkumeṉavum, piṟavu miṉṉō raṉṉa koḷka.
'yāppoṭu puṇarntu' eṉpatu akapporuḷum puṟapporuḷu māka yākkappaṭuñ ceyyuḷellāvaṟṟoṭum poruntu meṉṟavāṟu. ivar kiḷavi variyappaṭāteṉavē, ivar vāyilākiyavaḻit talimakaḷ vāyil maṟutta lilaḷeṉpatūum,  puṟattiṇit talivarkkum avvāṟē vaḻiniṟṟal vēṇṭumeṉpatūuṅ kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu; eṉṉi? aṟiva reṉappaṭuvār mūṉṟukālamun tōṉṟa naṉkuṇarntōrum pulaṉaṉ kuṇarnta pulamiyōru mākalāṉum, iṉip pārppārum avvāṟē ciṟappuṭiyarākalāṉum, avarvaḻi niṟṟal avarkkuk kaṭaṉākalāṉu meṉpatu.	(197)


iṭam itu eṉal
510.	oruneṟip paṭṭāṅku ōriyal muṭiyum
	karuma nikaḻcci yiṭameṉa moḻipa.
itu, kaḷaṉeṉappaṭṭa uṟuppuṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) palavum oruvaḻippaṭṭu ōrilakkaṇattāṉ muṭiyuṅ karumanikaḻcciyi iṭameṉṟu kūṟupa e-ṟu.
iṭameṉiṉuṅ kaḷameṉiṉum okkum. oru ceyyuḷ kēṭṭāl iḵtu iṉṉaviṭattu nikaḻntateṉṟu aṟitaṟkētuvākiyatōr uṟuppiṉi iṭameṉṟāṉeṉpatu. oru neṟippaṭutaleṉpatu, oruvaḻip palavun tokutal; ōriyaleṉpatu avaṟṟukkellām ilakkaṇamoṉ ṟātal. aḵtāvatu, kāṭciyum aiyamun tuṇivum puṇarcciyum nayappum pirivaccamum vaṉpuṟiyumeṉṟu iṉṉōraṉṉa ellām oruneṟip paṭṭu iyaṟkippuṇarcci yeṉṉum ōrilakkaṇattāṉ muṭiyumeṉpatu. karumanikaḻcci yeṉpatu kāmappuṇarcci yeṉṉuñ ceyappaṭuporu ṇikaḻcci. aḵtu iṭameṉappaṭṭatu. itu viṉiceyyiṭam. nilamāyiṉa muṉṉart tiṇiyeṉappaṭṭaṉa. kālam muṉṉarc collutum.
"eluva ciṟāa rēmuṟu naṇpa"	(kuṟun. 129)
eṉṉum pāṭṭum,
" kēḷir vāḻiyō kēḷir nāḷumeṉ
ṉeñcupiṇik koṇṭa vañcil lōtip
peruntōṭ kuṟumakaḷ ciṟumel lāka
morunāḷ puṇarap puṇari
ṉarināḷ vāḻkkiyum vēṇṭaleṉ yāṉē."	(kuṟun. 280)
eṉṉum pāṭṭum pāṅkaṟkūṭṭamē iṭaṉāka oruvaḻippaṭṭaṉa. eṉṉi? niṉ vēṟupāṭu eṟṟiṉā ṉāyiṟṟeṉṟu viṉaviya pāṅkaṟku, itaṉiṉā ṉāyiṟṟeṉṟu urittatūum, ataṟkup pāṅkaṉ kaḻaṟiṉāṉi etir maṟuttatūumeṉa iraṇṭum pāṅkaṟkūṭṭattup paṭṭu ōriyalāṉ muṭintaṉa. avi mēṟkūṟiya vakiyāṉē  kaṇṭukoḷka.	(198)

payaṉil kūṟṟu
511.	paratti vāyi leṉiru vīṟṟuṅ
	kiḻattiyic cuṭṭāk kiḷappuppaya ṉilavē.
itu, payaṉil kūṟṟuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu,
(i-ḷ.) parattiyum vāyilkaḷu meṉṉum iraṇṭu vēṟupāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇum eḻunta kiḷavi kiḻatti kēṭpāḷākak karutic collākkāṟ payaṉila e-ṟu.
kiḻattiyic cuṭṭutaleṉavē, pāṅkāyiṉār kēṭpac colli ṉum amiyumeṉpatāyiṟṟu.
ceṟitoṭi teḷirppa vīcic ciṟitava
ṇulamantu varukañ ceṉmō tōḻi,	(akam. 106)
eṉṟakkāl, itu paratti talivikkup pāṅkāyiṉār kēṭpak kūṟal vēṇṭumeṉpatu. vāyilkaḷun talimakaḷi ūṭal kāppaallatu collārākalāṉ talimakaḷ kēṭpāḷākac colla vēṇṭumeṉpatu. 'cuṭṭāk kiḷappup payaṉila' veṉṟataṉāṉ vāyilkaḷ kūṟuvaṉavuṅ kiḻattikkup  pāṅkāyiṉa tōḻi kēṭpac colliṉum amiyum. avaṟṟukkuc ceyyuḷ :
ōruyir māta rākali ṉivaḷun
tērā tuṭaṉṟaṉaḷ maṉṉē
eṉpatu kiḻatti kēṭpa vāyilkaḷ kūṟiyatu; tōḻi kēṭpac colliyatū umām.	(199)


talimakaṭ cuṭṭi vāyilkaḷ kūṟṟu
512.	vāyil ucāvē tammuḷum uriya.
ituvumatu,
(i-ḷ.) mēṉiṉṟa atikārattāṉ talimakaḷic cuṭṭi oruvark kurippār pōla vāyilkaḷ tammuṭ kūṟavum peṟum, aviyun talimakaḷ kēṭpaṉa e-ṟu.
ataṟkuc ceyyuḷ :
" taṇṇan tuṟivaṉ koṭumi
nammu ṉāṇik karappā ṭummē"	(kuṟun. 9)
eṉpatu, pāṭiṉi pāṇarkkurittatu. 'tammuḷum' ummi etirmaṟi yākalāṉ, kēṭkunaḷ avaḷeṉappaṭṭavāṟaṉṟit tammuḷ tāṅ kēṭṭal ciṟupāṉmiyeṉak koḷka.	(200)

kūṟātaṉa kūṟuvaṉavākavum kēḷātaṉa
kēṭpaṉavākavum kūṟavum paṭutal
513.	ñāyiṟu tiṅkaḷ aṟivē nāṇē
	kaṭalē kāṉal vilaṅkē maraṉē
	pulampuṟu poḻutē puḷḷē neñcē 
	aviyala piṟavum nuvaliya neṟiyāṉ
	colluna pōlavum kēṭkuna pōlavum
	colliyāṅku amiyum eṉmaṉār pulavar.
itu, kēḷātaṉa cila poruḷ, kēṭpaṉavākap poruḷiyaluḷ vaḻuvamikkappaṭṭaṉavaṟṟi ilakkaṇavakiyāṟ kūṟuvaṉavum kēṭkunavum ākaṟkaṇṇum ārāykiṉṟāṉeṉpatu.
(i-ḷ.) ñāyiṟun tiṅkaḷum aṟivum nāṇuṅ kaṭaluṅ kāṉalum vilaṅkum maraṉum poḻutum puḷḷum neñcum iṉṉōraṉṉa piṟavum mēṟ poruḷiyaluḷ,
" ceyyā marapiṟ ṟoḻiṟpaṭut taṭakkiyum"	(tol. poruḷ. 2)
eṉṟu potuvakiyāṟ kūṟiyavāṟṟāṉ, avitām colluvaṉa pōlavuṅ kēṭpaṉa pōlavuñ colla amiyum e-ṟu.
īṇṭuk kūṟṟum uṭaṉ kūṟiṉāṉ, mēlivaṟṟi variyaṟuttuk kūṟṟiṟku uriya veṉṟilaṉākaliṉ. pulampuṟu poḻuteṉpatu māliyum yāmamum eṟpāṭum kārum kūtirum paṉiyum iḷavēṉilum pōlvaṉa. 'piṟavu'meṉṟataṉāṉ pul putal mutaliyaṉa koḷka. avaṟṟuṭ cilavarumāṟu:
" katirpakā ñāyiṟē kalcērti yāyi
ṉavari niṉittu niṟutteṉki nīṭṭit
tarukuvi yāyiṟ ṟavirumeṉ ṉeñcat
tuyirtiriyā māṭṭiya tī"	(kali . 142)
eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa kaṇṭukoḷka.	(201)


kālam āvatu
514.	iṟappē nikaḻvē yetira teṉṉun
	tiṟattiyal maruṅkiṉ terintaṉar uḷḷap
	poruḷnikaḻvu urippatu kāla mākum.
itu, kālamuṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟu kālattiṉum nikaḻkiṉṟa nikaḻcci acceyyuḷuṭ ṭōṉṟac ceyyiṟ kālameṉṉum uṟuppām e-ṟu.
poruṇikaḻcciyik kālameṉṟateṉṉi? kālameṉa vēṟu poruḷ illatu pōla veṉiṉ, aḵtu īṇṭārāycciyiṉṟu; niyāya nūlārāycci yeṉka.
" villōṉ kālaṉa kaḻalē toṭiyōḷ
. . . . . . . .. . . 
vēypayi laḻuvam muṉṉi yōrē"	(kuṟun . 7)
eṉṉum pāṭṭiṉuḷ villōṉun toṭiyōḷum poruḷeṉappaṭum;
"vēypayi laḻuvam muṉṉi yōrē"
eṉpatu apporuṇikaḻcciyāṉ iṟantakāla meṉappaṭum. appāṭ ṭiṟkuc ciṟantār avarākaliṉ avarē poruḷāyiṉāreṉpatu.
" marunteṉiṉ maruntē vippeṉiṉ vippē
arumpiya cuṇaṅki ṉampakaṭ ṭiḷamulip
peruntō ṇuṇukiya nucuppiṟ
kalkeḻu kāṉavar nalkuṟu makaḷē"	(kuṟun . 71)
eṉpatu nikaḻkālam.
" pakalum peṟuviyiva ṭaṭameṉ ṟōḷē"	(kali . 49)
eṉpatu etirkālam.
puṟattiṟkum ivvāṟē iṉṟiyamiyāteṉpatu. perumpoḻutu ciṟupoḻuteṉpaṉa īṇṭut tiṇiyeṉappaṭ ṭaṭaṅkiṉa; eṉṉi ? mutalum karuvum uripporuḷuṅ kūṭṭit tiṇiyākaliṉ.	(202)


payaṉ āvatu
515.	itunaṉi payakkum itaṉāṉ eṉṉum
	tokinilik kiḷavi payaṉeṉap paṭumē.
itu, muṟiyāṉē payaṉeṉṉum uṟuppuṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) itu mikavum payakkum itaṉāṉeṉat tokuttuc collap paṭum poruḷ payaṉeṉṉum uṟuppām e-ṟu.
" māṟāk kātalar malimaṟan taṉarē
yāṟāk kaṭpaṉi varalā ṉāvē
vēṟā meṉṟōḷ vaḷineki ḻummē
kūṟāy tōḻiyāṉ vāḻu māṟē"
eṉṉum pāṭṭu, tōḻiyit tūtuviṭuvāḷātaṟ payaṉpaṭa vantatu. ivvā ṟellāp pāṭṭum payaṉuṟuppākavaṉṟi vārāveṉka. ivi puṟattiṟkum okkum.	(203)
meyppāṭu āvatu
516.	uyttuṇarvu iṉṟit talivaru poruḷāṉ
	meyppaṭa muṭippatu meyppā ṭākum.
itu, meyppāṭṭuṟup puṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) uyttuṇarcciyiṉṟic ceyyuḷiṭattuc collappaṭum poruḷ tāṉē veḷippaṭṭāṅkuk kaṇṇīrarumpal meymmayir cilirttal mutalākiya cattuvam paṭumāṟṟāṉ veḷippaṭac ceyvatu meyppāṭeṉṉum uṟuppām e-ṟu.
kavipporu ḷuṇarntāl ataṉāṉē collappaṭu poruḷ uyttu vēṟu kaṇṭāṅku aṟitali meyppāṭeṉṟāṉ. atu tēvarulakaṅ kūṟiṉum ataṉik kaṇṭāṅ kaṟiyacceytal ceyyuḷuṟup pām eṉṟavāṟu. avi,
" miyaṟa viḷaṅkiya maṇimaru ḷavvāytaṉ
meypeṟā maḻaliyiṉ viḷaṅkupū ṇaṉittara"	(kali . 81)
eṉṟāṟpōla  varuvaṉa. ' talivaru poruḷā'ṉeṉavē, nōkkuṟuppāṉ uṇarnta poruṭpiḻampiṉik kāṭṭuvatu meyppāṭeṉpatu itaṉ karuttu. ikkaruttiṉāṟ 'kavi kaṇkāṭṭum' eṉavuñ collupa.	(204)

ituvumatu
517.	eṇvaki yiyaṉeṟi piḻiyā tāki
	muṉṉuṟak kiḷanta muṭiviṉa tatuvē.
iḵtu, eytiya tikantupaṭāmik kāttatu.
(i-ḷ.) ituvum mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa meyppāṭē; piṟitilakkaṇatta teṉṟu koḷḷaṟka e-ṟu.
eṉavē,
" nakiyē yaḻuki yiḷivaraṉ maruṭki
yaccam perumitam vekuḷi yuvaki"	(tol. meyp . 3)
eṉa avi nannāṉkāmeṉavum meyppāṭṭiyaluṭ kūṟiyavāṟellām piṟavāṟṟāṉ vēṟupaṭuvaṉavuṅ koḷḷappaṭumeṉṟavāṟu.	(205)

eccam āvatu
518.	colloṭum kuṟippoṭum muṭivuko ḷiyaṟki
	pulliya kiḷavi yecca mākum.
iḵtu, eccaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu,
(i-ḷ.) kūṟṟiṉāṉuṅ kuṟippiṉāṉum muṭikkappaṭum ilakkaṇat toṭu poruntiya kiḷavi, eccameṉṉum uṟuppām e-ṟu.
'muṭivukoḷ iyaṟki' yeṉavē, ceyyuḷiṉkaṇṇataṉṟip piṉ koṇarntu muṭikkappaṭu meṉpatu peṟṟām.
u-m :
"ceṅkaḷam paṭakkoṉ ṟavuṇart tēytta
ceṅkō lampiṉ ceṅkōṭ ṭiyāṉik 
kaḻaṟoṭic cēey kuṉṟam
kurutip pūviṉ kulikkān taṭṭē"	(kuṟun . 1)
eṉac ceyyuṇ muṭintavaḻiyum, 'ivaṟṟāṉ yāṅkuṟiyuṭiyam allē meṉṟu talivaṟkuc coṉṉāḷēl atu kūṟṟeccamām; eṉṉi? avvāṟu kūṟavuñ citintatiṉmiyiṉ. talimakaṭkuc coṉṉāḷēl atu kuṟippeccam; eṉṉi? atu kāṇpāyākiṟ kāṇ eṉat talimakaḷi iṭattuyttu nīkkiya kuṟippiṉaḷāki atu tāṉ kūṟāḷākali ṉeṉpatu.			(206)


muṉṉam āvatu
519.	ivviṭattu immoḻi ivarivarkku uriyaveṉṟu
	avviṭattu avaravarkku urippatu muṉṉam.
itu, muṉṉameṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa uṟuppuk kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) yātōriṭattāṉum yātāṉumoru moḻi tōṉ ṟiyakkāl, immoḻi collutaṟkuriyāruṅ kēṭṭaṟkuriyārum iṉṉāreṉṟu aṟiyumāṟṟāṉ, aṅṅaṉam aṟitaṟku ōriṭam nāṭṭi av viṭattuk kūṟuvārkkuṅ kēṭpārkku mēṟṟa ceyyuṭku īṭākac colluvatu muṉṉam e-ṟu.
'ivarivark kuriyaveṉṟu' aṟivāṉ, ceyyuṭ kēṭṭāṉeṉavum, avviṭattavaravark kurikka veṉṟāṉ āciriyaṉeṉavuṅ koḷka. eṉṟeṉpatu eṉaveṉpatu pōlac colliṉeccamātalum viṉi yeccamātalum uṭimiyiṉ iḵtu,
" eṉave ṉeccam viṉiyoṭu muṭimē"	(tol. col. ecca. 42)
eṉpatoru viṉiyeñci niṟkum. avviṉitāṉ eccamākalāṉ urippateṉṉum viṉikoṇṭu muṭiyumeṉpatu.
" yāriva ṉeṅkūntaṟ koḷvā ṉituvumō
rūrāṇmik kotta paṭiṟuṭittu"	(kali. 89)
eṉṟakkāl immāṟṟañ collukiṉṟāḷ taliviyeṉavuñ collap paṭṭāṉ talimakaṉeṉavum  muṉṉattāṉ aṟiyappaṭuttaliṉ, itu muṉṉavuṟuppāyiṟṟu. oḻintaṉavaṟṟiṟkum iḵtokkum. iviyellām, akattiṟkum puṟattiṟkum potuvākak kūṟukiṉṟāṉeṉpatu.	(207)



poruḷvaki āvatu
520.	iṉpamum miṭumpiyum puṇarvum pirivum
	oḻukkamum eṉṟivi yiḻukku neṟiyiṉṟi
	ituvā kittiṇikku uripporuḷ eṉātu
	potuvāy niṟṟal poruḷvaki yeṉpa.
itu, poruḷvaki kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) iṉpamun tuṉpamum puṇartalum piritalum oḻukkamu meṉappaṭṭa  ivi  iḻukkātavāṟṟāṉ ittiṇikku itu poru ḷeṉṟu āciriya ṉōtiya uripporuḷaṉṟi avaṟṟukkellām potuvākap pulavaṉāṟ ceyyappaṭuvatu poruṭ kūṟeṉappaṭum e-ṟu.
'vaki'yeṉṟataṉāṟ pulavaṉ tāṉ vakintatē poruḷeṉṟu koḷka. aḵtaṉṟic ceyyuḷ ceytalākāteṉpatu itaṉ karuttu. avi,
"kikaviyāc ceṉṟu kaṇputiyāk kuṟukip
piṭikki yaṉṉa piṉṉakan tīṇṭit
toṭikki tivarat tōyntaṉṟu kollō
nāṇoṭu miṭinta kaṟpiṉ vāṇutal
antīṅ kiḷavik kuṟumakaḷ
meṉṟōḷ peṟaṉaciic ceṉṟaveṉ ṉeñcē''	(akam. 9)
eṉṟāṟ pōlac ceyyuḷ ceytavaṉ tāṉē vakuppaṉa vellāṅ kōṭal. itu pālippāṭṭiṉuḷ vantatāyiṉum, mulli mutalāyavaṟṟukkum potuvāmeṉpatu. potumi eṉpatu ellā vuripporuṭkum ēṟkap palavēṟu vakiyāṟ ceytal.	(208)


tuṟi āvatu
521.	avvam mākkaḷum vilaṅkum aṉṟip
	piṟaavaṇ variṉum tiṟavatiṉ nāṭit
	tattam iyaliṉ marapoṭu muṭiyiṉ
	attiṟam tāṉē tuṟiyeṉap paṭumē.
itu, tuṟiyeṉṉu muṟuppuṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aivaki nilattiṟkum uriyaṉa veṉappaṭum palvēṟu vakippaṭṭa makkaḷum māvum  puḷḷum ōtivantavāṟaṉṟip paṭittuc ceyiṉum avvattiṇikkēṟṟa ilakkaṇamum varalāṟṟu muṟimiyum piṟaḻāmic ceyyiṉ atu mārkkameṉappaṭum e-ṟu.
mārkkameṉiṉum tuṟiyeṉiṉum okkum.
" ūrkkā ṉivanta potumparu ṇīrkkāl" 	(kali. 56)
eṉṉuṅ kaliyiṉuḷ,
" koḻuniḻaṉ ñāḻaṉ mutiriṇar koṇṭu
kaḻuma muṭittuk kaṇkūṭu kūḻi"
eṉa, neytaṟṟalimakaḷ pōlak kūṟi, avaḷi marutanilattuk kaṇṭāṉ pōla
" ūrkkā ṉivanta potumparuḷ"
eṉavuñ collip piṉ kuṟiñcip poruḷākiya puṇartalurip poruḷāṉ muṭittāṉ. ivvāṟu mayaṅkacceyyiṉuṅ kuṟiñcit tuṟip pāṟpaṭac ceytamiyiṉ attuṟiyuṟuppāṉ vantateṉpatu. ituvum mēlip poruḷvaki pōlap pulavarāṟ ceytu koḷḷappaṭuva tākalāṉ ataṟkup piṉ vittāṉeṉpatu. maṟṟu,
" ennila maruṅkiṟ pūvum puḷḷum"	(tol. akat. 19)
eṉṟataṉāṉ itu muṭiyātōveṉiṉ, atu karupporuṇ mayaṅkutaṟkuk kūṟiṉāṉ; itu makkaḷēyaṉṟit talivaṉun taliviyum mayaṅkumāṟum nāṉku tiṇiyum oṉṟoṉṟaṉoṭu mayaṅkumāṟuṅ kūṟi atu pulavarāṟ ceytukoḷḷuvatōr uṟuppeṉavum, ōtivanta ilakkaṇatta taṉṟāyiṉum atu peṟumeṉavum, kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu.	(209)


māṭṭu āvatu
522.	akaṉṟuporuḷ kiṭappiṉum aṇukiya niliyiṉum
	iyaṉṟuporuṇ muṭiyat tantaṉar uṇarttal
	māṭṭeṉa moḻipa pāṭṭiyal vaḻakkiṉ.
itu, māṭṭeṉṉum uṟuppuṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) poruḷ koḷḷuṅkāl, akaṉṟu poruḷ kiṭappac ceyyiṉum aṇukik kiṭappac ceyyiṉum, iruvakiyāṉuñ ceṉṟu poruḷ muṭiyumāṟṟāṉ koṇarnturippac ceytali māṭṭeṉṉum uṟuppeṉṟu colluvar, ceyyuḷ vaḻakkiṉ e-ṟu.
ituvum nālvakip poruḷkōḷaṉṟip pulavaratu vēṟu
ceyki. atu;
" muṭṭāc ciṟappiṟ paṭṭiṉam peṟiṉum
vāriruṅ kūntal vayaṅkiḻi yoḻiya
vārēṉ vāḻiya neñcē"	(pattup. paṭṭiṉa. 218 - 20)
eṉa niṉṟatu, piṉṉar,
"vēliṉum veyya kāṉamavaṉ
kōliṉun taṇṇiya taṭameṉ ṟōḷē"	(paṭṭiṉa. 300, 301)
eṉac cēyttākac ceṉṟu poruḷkōṭaliṉ aḵtu akaṉṟu poruḷ kiṭappiṉum iyaṉṟu poruṇ muṭiyat tantu urittatām.
" tiṟantiṭumiṉ ṟīyavi piṟkāṇṭu mātar
iṟantu paṭiṟperitā mētam- uṟantiyarkōṉ
taṇṇāra mārpiṟ ṟamiḻar perumāṉik
kaṇṇārak kāṇak katavu"	(muttoḷ. 42)
eṉpatu, aṇukiya nili yeṉappaṭum. piṟavum aṉṉa. māṭṭutaleṉpatu koṇṭuvantu koḷuttutal .	(210)
māṭṭum eccamum iṉṟiyum ceyyuḷ amital
523	māṭṭum eccamum nāṭṭal iṉṟi	
	uṭaṉili moḻiyiṉum toṭarnili peṟumē.
itu, mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ oru cāraṉavaṟṟukkup puṟaṉaṭi..
(i-ḷ.) mēṟkūṟiya eccamum māṭṭum iṉṟiyum acceyyuḷuṭ kiṭantavāṟē amiyac ceyyavumpeṟuñ ceyyuḷ e-ṟu.
eccamum māṭṭum eṉṉātu māṭṭu muṟkūṟiyateṉṉi yeṉiṉ eccam mutalākiya aintu uṟuppum ivviti peṟumeṉṟu etirceṉṟu taḻīiyiṉāṉeṉpatu.
u-m :
"vārā rāyiṉum variṉu mavarnamak
kiyārā kiyarō tōḻi nīra
nīlap pimpō tuḷarip putala
pīli yoṇpoṟik karuviḷi yāṭṭi
nuṇmu ḷīṅkic cevvarum pūḻtta
vaṇṇat tuymmala rutirat taṇṇeṉ
ṟiṉṉā teṟitarum vāṭiyoṭu
eṉṉā yiṉaḷko leṉṉā tōrē"
eṉpatu, eccamiṉṟi vantatu.
" tāmari puriyuṅ kāmar cēvaṭi."	(kuṟun. kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu)
eṉpatu, muṉṉamiṉṟi vantatu.
" marunteṉiṉ maruntē"	(kuṟun . 71)
eṉpatu, poruḷiṉṟi vantatu.
" īyaṟ puṟṟat tīrmpuṟat tiṟutta"	(akam 8)
eṉpatu, tuṟivakiyiṉṟi vantatu.
" yāṉē yīṇṭi yēṉē"	(kuṟun. 54)
eṉpatu, māṭṭiṉṟi vantatu. iṭiniṉṟa mūṉṟaṉi ilēciṉāṟ koṇṭu iraṇṭaṉi eṭuttōtiṉāṉ. ivi iraṇṭum avaṟṟatu tuṇi iṉṟiyamiyāc ciṟappiṉavalla eṉṟaṟ keṉpatu.	(211)


vaṇṇam irupatu pakutittu ātal
524.	vaṇṇan tāmē nālin teṉpa.
itu, muṟiyāṉē īṟṟu niṉṟa vaṇṇam ittuṇip pakutittu eṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vaṇṇam irupatu vakippaṭum e-ṟu.
vaṇṇameṉpatu, cantavēṟupāṭu. nūṟum palavumāki vēṟupaṭiṉum avi īṇṭaṭaṅkumeṉpatūum., avvēṟupāṭellāñ canta vēṟṟumi ceyyāveṉpatūuṅ kūṟiyavāṟu. atu nuṇṇuṇar vuṭiyārkkup pulaṉāmeṉpatu. 	(212)


ituvumatu
525.	avitām,
	pāa vaṇṇam tāa vaṇṇam
	vallici vaṇṇam mellici vaṇṇa
	miyipu vaṇṇam aḷapeṭi vaṇṇam
	neṭuñcīr vaṇṇam kuṟuñcīr vaṇṇam
	cittira vaṇṇam nalipu vaṇṇam
	akappāṭṭu vaṇṇam puṟappāṭṭu vaṇṇam
	oḻuku vaṇṇam morūu vaṇṇam
	eṇṇu vaṇṇam akippu vaṇṇam
	tūṅkal vaṇṇam mēntal vaṇṇam
	uruṭṭu vaṇṇam muṭuku vaṇṇameṉṟu
	āṅkaṉam moḻipa aṟintici ṉōrē.
itu, mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa vaṇṇaṅkaḷatu peyar vēṟupāṭu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) mēṟcolliya nālintu vaṇṇamum ivvirupatu peyar vēṟupāṭṭiṉa veṉṟu collupa, avaṟṟi uṇarnta āciriyar e-ṟu.
itu, muṟiyāyiṉavāṟeṉṉiyeṉiṉ, itu varalāṟṟu muṟimi yeṉṟaṟku 'āṅṅaṉam moḻipa aṟinticiṉō' reṉṟā ṉeṉpatu. avi yāmāṟu collukiṉṟāṉ.	(213)
pāa vaṇṇam
526.	avaṟṟuḷ, pāa vaṇṇañ
	coṟcīrttu āki nūṟpāl payilum.
itu, niṟutta muṟiyāṉē pāa vaṇṇa muṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) pāavaṇṇameṉpatu, nūṟpāvaṇṇam e-ṟu.
eṟṟukku? ' nūṟpāṟ payilu' meṉṟamiyiṉ. itu coṟcīraṭittāki varumeṉpatu. itaṉuṭ 'payilu' meṉavē annūṟpāviṉuḷallatu akavaluḷ ittuṇip payilāteṉpatu. itu
" vaṭavēṅkaṭan teṉkumari"	(tol. pāyiram)
eṉavum,
" eḻutteṉap paṭupa
akaramuta
ṉakara viṟuvāy
muppaḵ teṉpa"	(tol. eḻuttu. nūṉ.1)
eṉavum, 
" avaṟṟuḷ,
aiu,
eo eṉṉu
mappā lintum"	(tol. eḻut. nūṉ. 3)
eṉavum payiṉṟu varum.
" periyakaṭ peṟiṉē"	(puṟam. 235)
eṉpatu coṟcīrāki akavaluṭ payilātu vantatu. coṟcīraṭi yeṉṉātu 'coṟcīr' eṉṟāṉ aṭiyoṭu toṭarātuñ coṟcīr varumātali ṉeṉpatu. avi,
" naṉṟuperi tākum"	(tol. col. uri. 45)
eṉṟāṟ pōla vēṟāki niṟpaṉa. kaliyuḷḷum paripāṭaluḷḷuñ coṟcīr varumeṉa mēṟkūṟiṉā ṉākaliṉ avaṟṟuḷḷuñ cantavēṟṟumip paṭṭaṉa pāavaṇṇamēyā meṉpatu.	(214)


tāa vaṇṇam
527.	tāa vaṇṇam
	iṭiyiṭṭu vanta vetukittu ākum.
(i-ḷ.) iṭiyīṭupaṭat toṭuppatu tāavaṇṇam e-ṟu.
avi poḻippum orūuvu mākaliṉ etukiyeṉa varintu kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu. aṭi yiṭiyiṭṭu varuvatu toṭi vēṟṟumiyāva tallatu vaṇṇavēṟṟumi yākāteṉpatu. oruceyyuḷuṭ palaaṭi vantāl aviellām iṭiyiṭṭut toṭuttal vēṇṭumōveṉiṉ, vēṇṭā; avi vantavaḻit tāa vaṇṇameṉappaṭumeṉpatu. eṉavē, ivvaṇṇavakika ḷellāñ ceyyuṇ muḻuvatumē peṟuvaṉavākak koḷḷak kiṭantaṉavalla; ivaṟṟi uṟuppeṉṟa taṉmiyāṟ kantiruva nūlār vaṇṇaṅ kūṟiyavāṟu pōla oru ceyyuḷuṭ palavum varappeṟu meṉpatām. avi.
" ulaka muvappa valaṉērpu tiritaru"	(muruku. 1)
eṉavum,
" uḷḷār kollō tōḻi muḷḷuṭi"	(aiṅkuṟu. piṟ. 143)
eṉavum varum. piṟavum aṉṉa.	(215)


vallici vaṇṇam
528.	vallici vaṇṇam valleḻuttup payilum.
(i-ḷ.) vallicivaṇṇam valleḻuttup payiṉṟu varum e-ṟu.
valleḻuttup payiṉṟu varutalāṉ appeyarttāyiṟṟu.
" muṭṭāc ciṟappiṟ paṭṭiṉam peṟiṉum"	(paṭṭiṉap. 218)
eṉavum,
" muṭṭāṭ ṭāmarit tuñci"	(muruku. 73)
eṉavum varum. piṟavum aṉṉa.	(216)


mellici vaṇṇam
529.	mellici vaṇṇam melleḻuttu mikumē.
(i-ḷ.)  melleḻuttu mikuvatu melleḻuttu vaṇṇam e-ṟu.
oru ceyyuṇ muḻuvatum ōriṉatteḻuttē payilacceytal iṉṉātātaliṉ ivi uṟuppeṉappaṭṭu iṭiyiṭṭu varumeṉṟā ṉeṉpatu. avvāṟu ceyyiṉ avi miṟikkavi yeṉappaṭum. oḻinta eḻuttiṟkum iḵtokkum.
" poṉṉi ṉaṉṉa puṉṉinuṇ ṭātu"	(yā. vi. mēṟ.)
eṉa melleḻuttup payiṉṟavāṟu.	(217)


iyipu vaṇṇam
530.	iyipu vaṇṇam miṭiyeḻuttu mikumē.
(i-ḷ.) iṭiyeḻuttu mikuvatu iyipuvaṇṇam e-ṟu.
" aravi ṉatira vurīiya varakukatiriṉ"
eṉa iṭiyeḻuttu mikku vantamiyiṉ iyipu vaṇṇamāyiṟṟu. meṉmi vaṉmikku iṭinikaravākiya eḻuttāṉ varutaliṉ iyipu vaṇṇa meṉṟār	(218)


aḷapeṭi vaṇṇam
531.	aḷapeṭi vaṇṇam aḷapeṭi payilum.
(i-ḷ.) iraṇṭaḷapeṭiyum payilac ceyvaṉa aḷapeṭi vaṇṇamām e-ṟu.
" marāa malaroṭu virāayp parāam"	(akam. 99)
eṉpatu aḷapeṭivaṇṇam.
" kaṇṇ ṭaṇṇṇeṉak kaṇṭuṅ kēṭṭum"	(malipaṭu. 352)
eṉpatum atu.	(219)

neṭuñcīr vaṇṇam
532.	neṭuñcīr vaṇṇa neṭṭeḻuttup payilum.
(i-ḷ.) neṭṭeḻuttup payiṉṟu varuvatu neṭuñcīr vaṇṇam 
e-ṟu.
atu,
" māvā rātē māvā rātē."	(puṟam . 273)
eṉpatu. neṭitāy varuvatu neṭuñcīreṉappaṭṭatu.	(220)
kuṟuñcīr vaṇṇam itu eṉal
533.	kuṟuñcīr vaṇṇam kuṟṟeḻuttup payilum.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟeḻuttup payilvatu kuṟuñcīr vaṇṇam e-ṟu.
" kuraṅkuḷip polinta koycuvaṟ puravi"	(akam. 4)
eṉa varum.	(221)

cittira vaṇṇam
534.	cittira vaṇṇam
	neṭiyavuṅ kuṟiyavum nērntuṭaṉ varumē.
(i-ḷ.) cittiravaṇṇam neṭṭeḻuttuṅ kuṟṟeḻuttum oppa virāayc ceyyappaṭuvatu (e -ṟu).
atu,
" cāra ṉāṭa nīvara lāṟē"
eṉa varum.
cittiravaṇṇameṉpatu palavaṇṇam paṭac ceyvatākaliṉ appeyarttāyiṟṟu.	(222)

nalipu vaṇṇam
535.	nalipu vaṇṇam āytam payilum.
(i-ḷ.) āytam payiṉṟu varuvatu nalipu vaṇṇamām e-ṟu.
aḵtu,
"aḵkāmi celvattiṟ kiyāteṉiṉ veḵkāmi"	(kuṟaḷ. 178)
eṉavum,
" ṉaḵkāṉ ṟaḵkā ṉāṉka ṉurupiṟku"	(tol. eḻut. 123)
eṉavum varum. nalipeṉpatu āytam.	(223)


akappāṭṭu vaṇṇam
536.	akappāṭṭu vaṇṇam
	muṭiyāt taṉmiyiṉ muṭintataṉ mēṟṟē.
(i-ḷ.) akappāṭṭu vaṇṇameṉpatu iṟutiyaṭi iṭiyaṭi pōṉṟu niṟpatu e-ṟu.
aviyāvaṉa muṭittuk kāṭṭum īṟṟaci ēkārattāṉaṉṟi, oḻinta uyirīṟṟāṉum oṟṟīṟṟāṉum varuvaṉa. avi :-
" tavaḻpavi tāmu mavaṟṟō raṉṉa"	(tol.mara. 5))
eṉavum,
"uṇkaṇ pacappa tevaṉko laṉṉāy"	(aiṅkuṟu. 21)
eṉavum, 
"kōṭuyar veṇmaṇa lēṟi
yōṭukalaṉ eṇṇun tuṟivaṉ ṟōḻi"
eṉavum,
" āṇamil poruḷemak kamarntaṉi yāṭi"	(kali. kaṭavuḷ vāḻttu)
eṉavum,
" ceṉṟī perumaniṟ ṟakikkunar yārō."	(akam. 46)
eṉavum, ivi āciriya īṟṟaṉa.
" kuḷiṟu kuralaruvik kuṉṟat titaṇmēṟ
kaḷiṟu varuvatu kaṇṭu- - veḷileṉṉa
lāyiṉāṉ piṉṉi yaṇaṅkiṟ kuyiraḷittup
pōyiṉāṉ yāṇṭiyāṉ pōṉm"
eṉa itaṉuḷ iṟutiyaṭi muṭiyāt taṉmiyiṉ muṭintatākaliṉ akappāṭṭu vaṇṇamāyiṟṟu.
" koṭi yuvaṇat tavaṇarō"
eṉak kalippāvuḷ arō vantu piṉ muṭiyāt taṉmiyiṉ muṭintatu.
ā ī ū ē ai ō eṉpaṉavaṟṟāṉ āciriyam iṟumeṉa vari yaṟuppā ruṇmiyiṉ, avvaccollāṉē avi muṭintaṉa veṉṟu koḷvaleṉiṉ, aṅṅaṉam variyaṟiyila veṉpatu mēṟ kāṭṭiya utāraṇaṅkaḷāṉ aṟintāmākaliṉ, akkaṭā variyaṟuppār mēṟṟeṉa viṭukka.	(224)


puṟappāṭṭu vaṇṇam
537.	puṟappāṭṭu vaṇṇam
	muṭintatu pōṉṟu muṭiyā tākum.
(i-ḷ.) puṟappāṭṭu vaṇṇameṉpatu, iṟutiyaṭip puṟattatākavun tāṉ muṭintatu pōla niṟṟal e-ṟu.
" iṉṉā vikal vārā muṉṉē
ceynnī muṉṉiya viṉiyē
munnīr varippaka muḻutuṭaṉ ṟuṟantē"	(puṟam. 363)
eṉpuḻi, īṟṟayalaṭi 'muṉṉiya viṉiyē' yeṉa muṭintatu pōṉṟu muṭiyātāyiṟṟu.	(225)




oḻuku vaṇṇam
538.	oḻuku vaṇṇa mōciyi ṉoḻukum.
(i-ḷ.) muṟkūṟiya vakiyāṉaṉṟi oḻukiya lōciyāṟ celvatu oḻukuvaṇṇam e-ṟu.
oḻintaṉavum oḻukumāyiṉum avi vēṟu vēṟilakkaṇamuṭiyaṉa eṉpatu.
" amma vāḻi tōḻi kātalark
kiṉṉē paṉikku miṉṉā vāṭiyoṭu
puṉkaṇ māli yaṉpiṉṟu naliya
vuyyala ḷivaḷ"	(yā. vi. mēṟ. 638)
eṉa varum.
orūu vaṇṇam
539.	orūu vaṇṇa morīit toṭukkum.
(i-ḷ.) yāṟṟoḻukkup pōlac colliya poruḷ piṟitoṉ ṟaṉi avāvāmi aṟuttuc ceyvatu orūuvaṇṇam e-ṟu.
orīit toṭutta leṉpatu ellāt toṭiyum ōrīiya centoṭiyāṉ toṭuttaleṉpārum uḷar. centoṭiyun toṭiyākalāṉ aṟṟaṉṟeṉpatu; atu,
"yāṉē yīṇṭi yēṉē yeṉṉalaṉē
āṉā nōyoṭu kāṉa laḵtē
tuṟivaṉ ṟammū rāṉē
maṟiyala rāki maṉṟat taḵtē"	(kuṟun. 97)
eṉa varum.
" ciṟiyakaṭ peṟiṉē yemakkīyu maṉṉē"	(puṟam. 235)
eṉpatum atu.
yāppup poruṇōkkiyavāṟupōla itu poruṇōkkātu ōciyē kōṭalāṉum aṭiyiṟantu kōṭalāṉum yāppeṉappaṭātu.(227)

eṇṇu vaṇṇam
540.	eṇṇu vaṇṇam eṇṇup payilum.
(i-ḷ.) eṇṇup payilvatu eṇṇuvaṇṇam (e -ṟu).
iḵtu, aṭi yeṇṇup payiṟalāṉ eṇṇuvaṇṇameṉak kāraṇappeyarāyiṟṟu. avi,
" naṉṉaṉ ēṟṟi naṟumpū ṇatti
tuṉṉaruṅ kaṭuntiṟaṟ kaṅkaṉ kaṭṭi"	(akam . 44)
eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa.
" nutalun tōḷun titali yalkulum"	(akam . 119)
eṉpatum atu.	(228)

akippu vaṇṇam
541.	akippu vaṇṇa maṟuttaṟut toḻukum.
(i-ḷ.) aṟuttaṟuttup payilvatu akippu vaṇṇam e-ṟu.
itu viṭṭuviṭṭuc cēṟaliṉ akippu vaṇṇameṉṉum peyar peṟṟatu.
" vārā rāyiṉum variṉu mavarnamak
kiyārā kiyarō tōḻi"	(kuṟun.110)
eṉpuḻi, oruvaḻi neṭil payiṉṟum oruvaḻik kuṟil payiṉṟum aṟut toḻukiya akippuvaṇṇamām.	(229)

tūṅkal vaṇṇam

542.	tūṅkal vaṇṇam vañci payilum.
(i-ḷ.) tūṅkalvaṇṇan tūṅkalōcittāki varuvatu e-ṟu.
vañci yeṉpatu vañcittūkkup pōla, ituvum aṟṟuc cēṟaluṭitteṉpatu.
atu.
" yāṉūṭat tāṉuṇartta yāṉuṇarā viṭṭapiṉ
ṟāṉūṭa yāṉuṇarttat tāṉuṇarāṉ"	(muttoḷ. 104)
eṉṟa niṉṟa toṭarnilikkaṇṇē attūṅkal kaṇṭukoḷka. ik karuttaṟiyātār kalippāviṉuḷ vañcippāp piṟakkumeṉavum vañciyuṭ kalippāp piṟakkumeṉavum mayaṅkupa.	(230)

ēntal vaṇṇam
543.	ēntal vaṇṇañ
	colliya colliṟ colliyatu ciṟakkum.
(i-ḷ.) colliya collāṉē collappaṭum poruḷ ciṟappac ceyvatu ēntalvaṇṇam e-ṟu.
ēntaleṉpatu, mikutal; oru collē mikku varutaliṉ ēntal vaṇṇa meṉṟāyiṟṟu. atu,
" vikalum vikal varakkaṇṭu maḵtuṇarār"	(nālaṭi. 3 : 9)
eṉavarum.	(231)

uruṭṭu vaṇṇam
544.	uruṭṭu vaṇṇa marākan toṭukkum.
(i-ḷ.) uruṭṭic collappaṭuvatu arākamākaliṉ arākan toṭuppatu uruṭṭu vaṇṇamām e-ṟu.
" urumuraṟu karuviya perumaḻi taliiya"	(akam. 158)
eṉavum,
" eriyuru vuṟaḻa vilava malara" 	(kali. 33)
eṉavum varum. itu nekiḻātu uruṇṭa vōciyākaliṟ kuṟuñcīr vaṇṇameṉappaṭātu uruṭṭu vaṇṇameṉappaṭu meṉpatu.	(232)

muṭuku vaṇṇam
545.	muṭuku vaṇṇam
	aṭiyiṟantu ōṭi yataṉō raṟṟē.
(i-ḷ.) muṭukuvaṇṇameṉpatu, nāṟcīraṭiyiṉ mikku ōraṭi arākattō blh¡F« e-ṟu.
atu,
" neṟiyaṟi ceṟikuṟi puritiri paṟiyā vaṟivaṉi muntuṟīit
takimiku tokivaki yaṟiyuñ cāṉṟava riṉamāka
vēypuri meṉṟōṭ pacaliyu mampalum"	(kali. 39)
eṉa varum.
ivaṟṟik koccakam arākameṉavuñ coṟcīraṭiyum muṭukiya laṭiyumeṉavum paripāṭaṟku vēṟupaṭuttōtiṉāṉ ivvēṟupāṭu nōkki yeṉpatu.	(233)
(ghl«) arākantoṭutta aṭiyoṭu piṟaaṭi toṭarn tōṭuvatu.

puṟaṉaṭi
546.	vaṇṇan tāmē yiviyeṉa moḻipa.
itu puṟaṉaṭi.
(i-ḷ.) vaṇṇameṉpaṉa cantamātalāṉ accanta vēṟṟumi ceyvaṉa iviyallatilli e-ṟu.
eṉavē, eṉ collappaṭṭatām? nāṉku pāviṉōṭum ivaṟṟi vittuṟaḻavum avi mayaṅkiya potuppā iraṇṭaṉoṭum uṟaḻavum nūṟṟirupatākalum, uyirmey varukka mellāvaṟṟōṭum uṟaḻntu perukkiṉ ettuṇiyum palavākalum, iṉip piṟavāṟṟāṟ cila peyar niṟīi avaṟṟāṉ uṟaḻntu perukka variyaṟiyilavākalum uṭiyavāyi ṉum, ivvirupatu vakiyāṉallatu cantavēṟṟumi viḷaṅkāteṉpatu karuttu.	(234)
vaṉappiṉuḷ ammi āmāṟu
547.	vaṉappiyal tāṉē vakukkuṅ kālic
	ciṉmeṉ moḻiyāṉ tāya paṉuvalōṭu
	ammi tāṉē yaṭinimir piṉṟē.
itu tokic cūttirattuḷ,
"āṟutali yiṭṭa vannā lintum"	(tol. cey. 1)
eṉak kūṟuceytu niṟīip, piṉṉar eṭṭuṟuppuk kūṟiṉāṉaṉṟē, ivi avaṟṟōṭotta ilakkaṇatta aṉmiyāṉ; eṉṉi? avi orō ceyyuṭkē ōtiya uṟuppākalāṉum ivi pala ceyyuḷun tiraṇṭavaḻi ivveṇvakiyum paṟṟit toṭukkappaṭumeṉak kūṟappaṭṭa tākalāṉu meṉpatu. ivaṟṟi vaṉappeṉṟu kūṟappaṭumāṟeṉṉi? accūttirattup peṟṟilamāleṉiṉ, vaṉappeṉpatu, perumpāṉmiyum pala uṟuppun tiraṇṭavaḻip peṟuvatōr aḻakākaliṉ avvāṟu kōṭum. ataṉāṟ pala ceyyuḷum uṟuppāyt tiraṇṭu perukiya toṭarniliyatē vaṉap peṉṉum peyarppakuti vakiyāṉ ēṟpateṉpatu. aḵtēl, ivveṭṭun taṉivaruñ ceyyuṭkaṇ vantāl aḻaku ceyyāvō veṉiṉ, avi pōl ivi taṉivarum ceyyuṭkumākum eṉṟaṟkaṉṟē avvirupattā ṟuṟuppōṭum ivaṟṟi ōriṉappaṭuttu ōtiyateṉpatu. aḵtu, iyipiṟ koppa vārāteṉpatu muṉṉarc (552) collutum. itaṉāṉē muṉṉi yuṟuppukkaḷ toṭarnilic ceyyuṭku varumeṉpatūuṅ koḷka. allākkāl, māttiri mutalākiya orōvuṟuppāṉ aḻaku piṟavā tākiyē cellum, ivaṟṟiyē vaṉappeṉṟu orō ceyyuṭkē koḷḷiṉeṉpatu. iṅṅaṉam irupattāṟum eṭṭumeṉa vakuppavē avi akameṉpaṉa, taṉinilic ceyyuṭku muṟkūṟappaṭā, ittoṭar nilic ceyyuṭku muṟkūṟappaṭumeṉpatu. iṉi, ivaṟṟic cūttirattāṉ vaṉappeṉṉuṅ kuṟi yeytuvikka vēṇṭuvāṉākac cūttiratti,
"vaṉappiya ṟāṉē vakukkuṅ kālic
ciṉmeṉ moḻiyāṟ ṟāya paṉuvaliṉ"
eṉpatu pāṭamāka urittāṉeṉka; atuvum aṟintavāṟē koḷka.
(i-ḷ.) cilavāya melliyavāya collōṭum iṭiyiṭṭu vanta paṉuvalilakkaṇattōṭum aṭinimirvillatu ammiyām e-ṟu.
'aṭinimirāteṉavē, ammi yeṉpatu muḻuvatum oru ceyyu ḷākal vēṇṭum; vēṇṭavē, aḵtu uṟuppaṉṟākiyē cellum; ataṉi uṟuppeṉal vēṇṭumātalāṉ, aṭinimirā teṉappaṭṭa ceyyuḷ uṟuppāka avi pala toṭarntu muṭintu īṇṭuc ceyyuḷā meṉpatu. cilavāka veṉpatu, eṇṇuc curuṅkutal. melliyavātal, cilavākiya coṟkaḷ eḻuttiṉāṉ akaṉṟu kāṭṭātu cilaveḻuttiṉāṉ varuvatu. aṭi nimirāteṉṟatu, aintaṭiyiṉ ēṟāteṉṟavāṟu.
tāya paṉuvalōṭeṉṟatu, aṟamporuḷiṉpameṉṉum mūṉṟaṟ kum ilakkaṇañ colluva (pōṉṟu) vēṟiṭiyiṭi aviyaṉṟiyun tāyc celvateṉṟavāṟu, aḵtāvatu, patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇakkeṉa vuṇarka. ataṉuḷ, iraṇṭaṭiyāṉum aintaṭiyāṉum orō ceyyuḷ vantavāṟum, avi cilavāya melliya coṟkaḷāṉ vantavāṟum, aṟamporuḷiṉpameṉa avaṟṟukku ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiya pāṭṭup payiṉṟu varumāṟuṅ avvāṟaṉṟik kārnāṟpatu, kaḷavaḻināṟpatu mutalāyiṉa vantavāṟuṅ kaṇṭukoḷka.
"poruḷkaruvi kālam viṉiyiṭaṉō ṭintu
miruṭīra veṇṇic ceyal"	(kuṟaḷ. 675)
eṉpatu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiyatākaliṟ paṉuvalōṭeṉṟāṉ.
"malarkāṇiṉ miyātti neñcē yivaḷkaṇ
palarkāṇum pūvokku meṉṟu"	(kuṟaḷ. 1112)
eṉa iḵtu ilakkiyamākalāṟ ṟāya paṉuva leṉappaṭṭatu.
ivi taṉittu variṉum av vaṉappeṉappaṭum; tāvutaleṉpatu, iṭiyiṭutal. ivviruvakiyuñ ceyyuḷeṉappaṭum. ammiyeṉpatu, kuṇappeyar. amitippaṭṭu niṟṟaliṉ ammiyeṉṟāyiṟṟu. ataṉuḷ uṟuppākiya pāṭṭukkaṭōṟum māttiriyuṟuppu mutalākiya uṟuppiṉuḷ ēṟpaṉa palavum varumāṟum, ivvaṉappiṉuḷ ēṟpaṉa palavum varumāṟum aṟintukoḷka.	(235)

aḻaku āmāṟu
548.	ceyyuḷ moḻiyāṉ cīrpuṉintu yāppiṉ
	avvaki tāṉē yaḻakeṉap paṭumē.
iḵtu, iraṇṭā meṇṇumuṟimikka ṇiṉṟa aḻakuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tericoṟ payilātu ceyyuḷuṭ payiṉṟu varum moḻika ḷāṟ cīraṟuttup polivupaṭa yāppiṉ, apporuḷ aḻaku e-ṟu.
'avvaki' yeṉṟataṉāṉ avi vēṟuvēṟu vantu īṇṭiya tokinilic ceyyuḷeṉṟavāṟu. aviyāvaṉa: neṭuntoki mutalākiya tokiyeṭṭu meṉṟavāṟu. aḻaku ceyyuṇmoḻi yeṉṟa teṉṉi yeṉiṉ, atu perumpāṉmiyāṟ kūṟiṉāṉ. ammoḻi yāṉē, iṭiccaṅkattāruṅ kaṭiccaṅkattārum ivvilakkaṇattāṟ ceyyuḷ ceytār. ikkālattuc ceyyiṉum vilakkiṉṟeṉpatu. maṟṟu mūvaṭimuppatu mutalāyiṉa ammi yeṉappaṭumō aḻakeṉappaṭumō veṉiṉ, tāya paṉuvaliṉmiyiṉ ammi yeṉappaṭā veṉpatu. ivaṟṟuḷḷum orō ceyyuṭkaṇṇē māttiri mutalākiya uṟuppum ēṟṟavakiyāṉ varuvaṉa aṟintukoḷka. oḻintaṉavaṟṟiṟkum iḵtokkum.		(236)

toṉmi āmāṟu
549.	toṉmi tāṉē
	uriyoṭu puṇarnta paḻimi mēṟṟē.
itu, toṉmi yuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) toṉmiyeṉpatu uri virāayp paḻamiyavākiya katipporuḷākac ceyyappaṭuvatu e-ṟu.
avi peruntēvaṉārāṟ pāṭappaṭṭa pāratamum, takaṭūr yāttiriyum pōlvaṉa.	(237)
tōl āmāṟu
550.	iḻumeṉ moḻiyāṉ viḻumiyatu nuvaliṉum
	paranta moḻiyāṉ aṭinimirn toḻukiṉun
	tōleṉa moḻipa toṉmoḻip pulavar.
itu, muṟiyāṉē tōleṉṉum vaṉappuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu. aḵtiruvakippaṭum: koccakakkaliyāṉum āciriyattāṉuñ ceyyap paṭuvaṉa.
"yāppiṉum poruḷiṉum vēṟṟumi yuṭiyatu."
eṉṟavaḻit tēvapāṇiyum kāmamum poruḷākavaṉṟiyuṅ koccakak kali varumeṉṟāṉākalāṉ, avimēl varutal īṇṭuk koḷḷappaṭṭatu.
(i-ḷ.) iḻumeṉ moḻiyāṉ viḻumiyatu nuvaliṉum -  mel leṉṟa collāṉ aṟamporuḷiṉpam vīṭeṉṉum viḻupporuḷ payappac ceyvaṉa. avi ceyta kālattuḷḷaṉa kaṇṭilam; piṟkālattu vantaṉa kaṇṭukoḷka.
paranta moḻiyāṉ aṭi nimirntu oḻukiṉum - āciriyappāṭṭāṉ oru katimēl toṭukkappaṭṭaṉa; avi poruṭṭoṭarnili.
tōl eṉa moḻipa toṉmoḻip pulavar - tōleṉṟu collupa pulavar e-ṟu.
'toṉmoḻi'yeṉṟār, paḻiya katiyic ceytal paṟṟi. itu muṉvaruñ cūttirattāṉum peṟutum. 	(238)

viruntu āmāṟu
551.	viruntē tāṉum
	putuvatu kiḷanta yāppiṉ mēṟṟē.
(i-ḷ.) viruntutāṉum putitākat toṭukkappaṭun toṭarnili mēṟṟu e-ṟu.
'tāṉu' meṉṟa ummiyāṉ, muṉṉit tōleṉappaṭṭatūum paḻiya katiyip putiyatākac colliyatāyiṟṟu. itu paḻaṅkati mēṟṟaṉṟip putitākac collappaṭutal oppumiyiṉ ummiyāṉ iṟantatu taḻīiyiṉāṉeṉpatu. 'putuvatu kiḷanta yāppiṉ mēṟṟu' eṉṟateṉṉi yeṉiṉ, putitākat tām vēṇṭiyavāṟṟāṟ pala ceyyuḷun toṭarntuvarac ceyvatu. atu, muttoḷḷāyiramum, poykiyār mutalāyiṉōr ceyta antātic ceyyuḷumeṉa uṇarka. kalampakam mutalāyiṉavuñ collupa.	(239)

iyipu āmāṟu
552.	ñakāri mutalā ṉakāri yīṟṟup
	puḷḷi iṟuti iyipeṉap paṭumē.
iḵtu, iyipuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ña ṇa na ma ṉa, ya ra la va ḻa ḷa veṉṉum patiṉoru puḷḷi yīṟṟiṉuḷ oṉṟaṉi iṟutiyākac ceyyuñ ceyyuḷ poruṭṭoṭarāka vuñ, coṟṟoṭarākavuñ ceyvatu iyipeṉappaṭum e-ṟu.
iyipu eṉṟataṉāṉē poruḷum iyintu, collum iyintu varumeṉpatu karuttu; cīttalic cāttaṉārāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa maṇimēka liyuṅ koṅkuvēḷirāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa toṭarnilicceyyuḷum pōlvaṉa. avi ṉakāra īṟṟāṉ iṟṟaṉa. maṟṟiyīṟṟāṉ varuvaṉavaṟṟuk kum īṇṭilakkaṇam uṇmiyiṉ ilakkiyam peṟṟavaḻik koḷka. ippoḻutu avi vīḻntaṉapōlum.
paranta moḻiyāṉ aṭi nimirntoḻukiya tōlōṭu itaṉiṭi vēṟṟumi yeṉṉiyeṉiṉ, avi uyirīṟṟavātal perumpāṉmi yākalāṉ vēṟupāṭuṭiya coṟṟoṭarāṉ varutalumuṭiya veṉpatu. coṟṟoṭareṉpatu, antāti yeṉappaṭuvatu; eṉṟataṉāṉ, uyirīṟṟuc coṟṟoṭar ciṟupāṉmi yeṉpatu koḷka.	(240)

pulaṉ āmāṟu
553.	cēri moḻiyāṉ cevvitiṟ kiḷantu
	tērtal vēṇṭātu kuṟittatu tōṉṟiṉ
	pulaṉeṉa moḻipa pulaṉuṇarn tōrē.
itu, pulaṉāmā ṟuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ) cērimoḻi yeṉpatu pāṭimāṟṟaṅkaḷ. avaṟṟāṉē cevvitākak kūṟi ārāyntu kāṇāmip poruṭṭoṭarāṉē toṭuttuc ceyvatu pulaṉeṉṟu colluvar pulaṉ uṇarntōr e-ṟu.
avi viḷakkattār kūttu mutalākiya nāṭakac ceyyuḷākiya veṇṭuṟic ceyyuḷ pōlvaṉa veṉpatu kaṇṭukoḷka.	(241)

iḻipu āmāṟu
554.	oṟṟoṭu puṇarnta valleḻuttu aṭakkātu
	kuṟaḷaṭi mutalā vintaṭi yoppittu
	ōṅkiya moḻiyāṉ āṅkaṉam oḻukiṉ
	iḻipiṉ ilakkaṇam iyinta tākum.
iḵtu, iṟuti niṉṟa iḻipilakkaṇa muṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ) oṟṟoṭu puṇarnta valleḻuttu aṭakkātu - oṟṟaṭutta valleḻuttup payilātu; kuṟaḷaṭi mutalā aintu aṭi oppittu - irucīraṭi mutalāka eḻucīraṭiyaḷavum vanta aṭi aintaṉiyum oppittu; oppitta leṉpatu, perumpāṉmiyāṉ nāṟcīraṭiyākap pakukkappaṭṭeṉṟa vāṟu; ōṅkiya moḻiyāṉ - neṭṭeḻuttum anneṭṭeḻuttup pōl ōci eḻum melleḻuttum lakāra ḷakāraṅkaḷum uṭiya collāṉ; āṅṅaṉam oḻukiṉ - iviyum,
"cēri moḻiyāṟ cevvitiṟ kiḷantu
tērtal vēṇṭātu"	(513)
poruḷ pulappaṭac ceṉṟu naṭappiṉ; iḻipiṉ ilakkaṇam iyinta tākum - iḻipeṉṟu collappaṭum ilakkaṇattatu e-ṟu.
aviyāvaṉa, kaliyum paripāṭalum pōlum icippāṭṭākiya centuṟi mārkkattaṉa veṉpatu. ivaṟṟukkuk kāraṇan 'tērtal vēṇṭātu,  poruḷ iṉitu viḷaṅkal vēṇṭumeṉṟatu, avinayattiṟku uriyavātal nōkki yeṉpatu. maṟṟitaṉi veṇṭuṟic ceyyuṭkumuṉ vippiṉeṉṉeṉiṉ, iḵticippāṭṭākaliṉ iṉi varukiṉṟatu icittamiḻākaliṉ ataṟkupakārappaṭa itaṉi iṟutikkaṇ vittā ṉeṉpatu.	(242)
ōttup puṟaṉaṭi
555.	ceyyuṇ maruṅkiṉ meypeṟa nāṭi
	iḻitta vilakkaṇam piḻittaṉa pōla
	varuvuḷa eṉiṉum vantavaṟṟu iyalāṉ
	tiriviṉṟi muṭittal teḷḷiyōr kaṭaṉē.
iḵtu, ivvōttiṟkellām puṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ceyyuḷiṭattup poruḷ peṟa ārāyntu tantiram ceyyappaṭṭa ilakkaṇattiṉ vaḻīiyiṉa pōṉṟu piṉ tōṉṟuvaṉa vuḷavēl muṟkūṟappaṭṭa ilakkaṇattoṭu tiriyāmal muṭittukkōṭal aṟivuṭiyōratu kaṭaṉ e-ṟu.
avi eṇcīr mutalāyiṉa variṟ kaḻineṭilaṭippāṟ cārttik kōṭalum, ētu nutaliya mutumoḻiyoṭu, pāṭṭiṟku iyipiṉṟiyun toṭarpu paṭuppaṉavum yāppeṉṉum uṟuppiṉpāṟ kōṭalum, piṟavum īṇṭōtātaṉa uḷaveṉiṉ aviyumellāñ ceyyuḷilakkaṇa muṭipāku meṉṟavāṟu. 	(243)
ceyyuḷiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.

9
marapiyal
marapu paṟṟi varum iḷamippeyar
556.	māṟṟaruñ ciṟappiṉ marapiyal kiḷappiṉ
	pārppum paṟaḻuṅ kuṭṭiyuṅ kuruḷiyum
	kaṉṟum piḷḷiyum makavum maṟiyumeṉṟu
	oṉpatuṅ kuḻaviyōṭu iḷimip peyarē.
eṉpatu cūttiram. ivvōttu eṉṉa peyarttōveṉiṉ, marapiya leṉṉum peyarttu. itaṉāṉē ōttu nutaliyatūum marapu uṇarttuta leṉpatu peṟṟām.
marapeṉṟa poruṇmi eṉṉiyeṉiṉ, kiḷaviyākkattu marapeṉṟu variyaṟuttu ōtappaṭṭaṉavum ceyyuḷiyaluḷ (ceyyuḷiyal 1-uri.) marapeṉṟu variyaṟuttu ōtappaṭṭaṉavumaṉṟi, irutiṇip poruṭkuṇaṉākiya iḷamiyum āṇmiyum peṇmiyum paṟṟiya varalāṟṟu muṟimiyum, uyartiṇi nāṉku cātiyum paṟṟiya marapum, aḵṟiṇip pullum maraṉum paṟṟiya marapum, avipaṟṟivarum ulakiyal marapum, nūṉmarapumeṉa iviyellām marapeṉappaṭumeṉpatu.
maṟṟup poruḷkaḷiṉ iḷamipaṟṟi varum marapu kūṟiṉāṉ, mūppuppaṟṟi varum marapu kūṟāṉōveṉiṉ, - atu variyaṟi yiṉmiyiṟ kūṟāṉeṉpatu.
maṟṟu, mēli ōttiṉōṭu ivvōttiṭi iyipeṉṉi yeṉiṉ, muṉṉar vaḻakkilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiyataṉpiṉ ceyyuḷilakkaṇam ceyyuḷiyaluṭ kūṟiṉāṉ, avviraṇṭaṟkum potuvākiya marapu īṇṭuk kūṟiṉamiyiṉ itu ceyyuḷiyalōṭu iyipu uṭittāyiṟṟu.
maṟṟu, vaḻakkilakkaṇañ ceyyuṭkum potuvākaliṉ iṅṅaṉam iraṇṭaṟkum potuvākiya marapiṉiyum ceyyuḷiyaliṉmuṉ vikka veṉiṉ, -  avvāṟu vaḻakkum ceyyuḷumeṉṟa iraṇṭumallāta nūliṟ kum īṇṭu marapu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ itu ceyyuḷiyaliṉ piṉ vikkappaṭṭatu.
ivvōttiṉ mutaṟcūttiram eṉṉutaliṟṟōveṉiṉ ellāp poruḷiṉkaṇṇum iḷamikkuṇampaṟṟi nikaḻuñ col iviyeṉṟu variyaṟuttuk kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) māṟṟaruñ ciṟappiṉ marapiyal kiḷappiṉ - vilakkaruñ ciṟappiṟṟākiya marapilakkaṇaṅkūṟiṉ; pārppum paṟaḻum kuṭṭiyum kuruḷiyum kaṉṟum piḷḷiyum makavum maṟiyum eṉṟu oṉpatum kuḻaviyōṭu iḷamippeyar - kuḻaviyōṭu iviyoṉpatum iḷamippeyar e-ṟu.
mēlaṉa eṭṭuṅ kuḻaviyumeṉa iḷamippeyar oṉpatāyiṉa. kuḻaviyōṭoṉpateṉṉātu 'oṉpatuṅ kuḻaviyō' ṭeṉa mayaṅkak kūṟiyataṉāṟ pōtteṉpatum iḷamippeyareṉavum piṟavum varuvaṉa uḷavāyiṉuṅ koḷḷappaṭum. ivaṟṟiyellām mēlvariyaṟuttu iṉṉa poruṭku iṉṉa peyar uritteṉpatu collutum.
"māṟṟaruñ ciṟappiṉ"
eṉṟataṉāṉē, ivi orutaliyākat tattam marapiṟ piṟaḻāmaṟ ceyyuḷ ceyyappaṭumeṉpatūum, īṇṭuk kūṟātaṉavāyiṉ vaḻakkoṭu paṭṭa  marapu piṟaḻavum ceyyuḷiṉpam paṭiṉ avvāṟu ceypaveṉpatūuṅ kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu. avi,
"akaliru vicumpiṟ pāyiruḷ parukip
pakalkāṉ ṟeḻutaru palkatirp pariti"	(perumpāṇ. 1, 2)
eṉavum,
"nummiṉuñ ciṟantatu nuvvi yākumeṉ
ṟaṉṉi kūṟiṉaḷ puṉṉiyatu ciṟappē"	(naṟ. 172)
eṉavum varum.
avaṟṟuḷ paritiyañ celvaṉip 'parukum' 'kālum' eṉṟalum, puṉṉimarattiṉi 'nuvvi'yeṉṟalum marapaṉmiyiṉ vaḻakkiṉuḷ māṟṟutaṟku uriyavāmeṉpatu karuttu.
"nilavuk kuvittaṉṉa veṇmaṇal"	(kuṟun. 123)
eṉavum,
"iruṭuṇin taṉṉa vēṉaṅ kāṇiṉ"	(malipaṭu. 247)
eṉavum,
"iruḷnūṟ ṟaṉṉa virumpal kūntal"
eṉavum varuvaṉavum avi.	(1)

marapu paṟṟi varum āṇpāṟpeyar
557.	ēṟum ēṟṟiyum oruttalum kaḷiṟuñ
	cēvum cēvalum iraliyum kaliyum
	mōttiyum takarum utaḷum apparum
	pōttum kaṇṭiyum kaṭuvaṉum piṟavum
	yātta vāṇpāṟ peyareṉa moḻipa.
(i-ḷ.) āṇpāṟpeyar ivveṇṇappaṭṭa patiṉintum e-ṟu.
'piṟavum' eṉṟataṉāṉ āṇeṉṟum viṭiyeṉṟum varuvaṉa pōlvaṉavuṅ koḷka.
ivaṟṟi variyaṟi kūṟumvaḻi (589 - 605.) utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭutum. 'yātta āṇpāṟpeyar' eṉṟataṉāṟ pōtteṉpatu iḷamippeyarā māyiṉum iṅṅaṉam āṇpāṟkuc ciṟantu varumāṟupōlac ciṟavātu ataṟkeṉpatu koḷka.	(2)

marapu paṟṟi varum peṇpāṟ peyar
558.	pēṭiyum peṭiyum peṭṭiyum peṇṇum
	mūṭūm nākum kaṭamiyum aḷakum 
	mantiyum pāṭṭiyum piṇiyum piṇavum
	antañ cāṉṟa piṭiyoṭu peṇṇē.
(i-ḷ.) kūṟappaṭṭa patiṉmūṉṟu peyarum peṇmip peyar e-ṟu.
kaṭamiyeṉpatoru cātippeyar uṇṭēṉum atuvaṉṟu, īṇṭeṇṇappaṭṭatu peṇmi mēṟṟeṉṟu koḷka.
'antañ cāṉṟa' eṉpatu, muṭipamintaṉa ivi yeṉṟavāṟu. eṉavē, ā eṉpatu peṇ peyarātalum peṇ āṇeṉpaṉa orucār pulliṟkum nērtaluṅ koḷka; eṉṉi? āṉēṟeṉpatu āviṉuḷ ēṟeṉappaṭutaliṉ, āveṉpatu āṇpāṟkum potuvākaliṉ atu muṭipamiyāteṉappaṭṭatu. atu mēṟkāṭṭutum.
āṇmi peṇmi pulliṟkiṉmiyiṉ aviyum muṭipuṭiyaṉa ākāvāyiṉuṅ kāyāp paṉiyi āṇpaṉi yeṉṟuṅ kāyppaṉa vaṟṟip peṇpaṉi yeṉṟum vaḻaṅkupa. iviyellām vaḻakkākaliṟ ceyyuḷuḷ varumāṟu aṟintukoḷḷappaṭum. ivaṟṟukku mēl variyaṟi kūṟumvaḻi (nūṟpā 606- 22) utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭutum.	(3)

iḷamippeyaruḷ pārppum piḷḷiyum
559.	avaṟṟuḷ
	pārppum piḷḷiyum paṟappavaṟ ṟiḷami.
itu, niṟutta muṟiyāṉē iḷimippeyaruḷ muṟkūṟiya pārppiṉik kūṟuvāṉ ataṉōṭoppumi kaṇṭu piḷḷip peyaruṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ivviraṇṭum puḷḷiḷamikkuriya e-ṟu.
iviyellām ikkālattu vaḻakkiṉuḷ ariyavākaliṟ cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷuṭ kāṇappaṭum; allaṉa vaḻakkiṉmēṟ kāṭṭutum.
1"mēṟkavaṭ ṭirunta pārppiṉaṅ kaṭku"	(akam. 31)
eṉavum,
2"illiṟip paḷḷittam piḷḷiyoṭu vatiyum"	(kuṟun. 46)
eṉavum,
3"pitaṟ piḷḷik kiḷipayirn tāaṅku"	(kuṟun. 139)
eṉavum ivi 1 paruntum 2 ūrkkuruviyum 3 kiḷiyumeṉṉum paṟavimēl vantaṉa. piṟavum aṉṉa.
puḷḷukkulam palavākalāṉ iccūttirattuḷ aṭaṅkiya marapu ettuṇiyum palavātal nōkki mutaṟ cūttirattuṭ pārppiṉi muṟkūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu.	(4)


ituvumatu
560.	tavaḻpavi tāmum avaṟṟō raṉṉa.
(i-ḷ.) pārppum piḷḷiyun tavaḻpavaṟṟiṟkum uriya e-ṟu.
avi, āmiyum uṭumpum ōntiyum mutaliyum mutalāyiṉa. āmiyum mutaliyum nīruḷ vāḻiṉum nilattiyaṅkuṅkāl tavaḻpavi yeṉappaṭum.
u-m :
"yāmip pārppi ṉaṉṉa
kāmaṅ kātalar kiyaṟa viṭiṉē"	(kuṟun. 152)
eṉavum,
"taṉpārpput tiṉṉu maṉpiṉ mutali"	(aiṅkuṟu. 41)
eṉavum,
"tāycāp piṟakkum puḷḷik kaḷvaṉoṭu
piḷḷi tiṉṉu mutalit tavaṉūr"	(aiṅkuṟu. 24)
eṉavum varum.
'tāmum' eṉṟataṉāṉ ūrvaṉa naṭappaṉavuñ ciṟupāṉmi piḷḷippeyarkku uriyaṉa koḷka. atu, piḷḷippāmpeṉa ūrvaṉa mēl vantatu.
"piḷḷi verukiṟ kalkiri yāki"	(kuṟun. 107)
eṉa naṭappaṉamēl vantatu. 'mūṅkāppiḷḷi' eṉpatum īṇṭē koḷḷappaṭum. pārppum avvāṟē varuvaṉa uḷavēṟ koḷka. ituvun tavaḻuñ cātikkellām potuvākiya parappuṭimiyiṉ iraṇṭāvatu vittāṉeṉpatu.	(5)
kuṭṭi eṉṉum peyarkku uriyaṉa
561.	mūṅkā verukueli mūvari yaṇilōṭu
	āṅkavi nāṉkuṅ kuṭṭik kuriya.
itu, mēl eṭuttōttāṉum ilēcāṉum atikārappaṭṭa tavaḻvaṉavum naṭappaṉavum paṟṟik kuṭṭiyeṉṉum peyariṉiyum muṟiyaṉṟik kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṭṭiyeṉappaṭuvaṉa ivi nāṉkum e-ṟu.
avi, mūṅkākkuṭṭi, verukkukkuṭṭi, elikkuṭṭi, aṇiṟ kuṭṭiyeṉavarum.
'mūvari yaṇi' leṉṟataṉāṉ oḻinta mūṉṟun tammuḷ oru piṟappiṉavām. ivi orunikaraṉavē eṉpatu koḷka.
'āṅkavi nāṉkum' eṉṟataṉāl tattuvaṉavaṟṟukkum kuṭṭip peyar koṭukkappaṭum; tavaḷikkuṭṭi yeṉavarum. mēl ūrvaṉavaṟ ṟukkun tavaḻvaṉavaṟṟilakkaṇam eytuvittamiyāṟ pāmpukkuṭṭi yeṉpatuṅ koḷka.
maṟṟuk kīriyum nāviyum pōlpavaṟṟiyuṅ kuṭṭiyeṉṉārō veṉiṉ, eṉṉār, avaṟṟip piḷḷiyeṉṟalē perumpāṉmiyāka liṉ. 'uriya' veṉṟataṉāṟ ciṟupāṉmi kuṭṭiyeṉpatuṅ koḷka. mūṅkāviṉ vikaṟpameṉpāru muḷar. 	(6)


paṟaḻ eṉṉum peyarkku uriyaṉa
562.	paṟaḻeṉap paṭiṉum uṟaḻāṇ ṭilli.
(i-ḷ.) mēṟkūṟiya nāṉkum paṟaḻeṉavumpaṭum e-ṟu.
ivi ikkālattu vīḻntaṉa. maṟṟu muṟkūṟiya nāṉkiṉiyum ippeyarāṉē muṟkūṟuka, mutaṟcūttirattuḷ ōtiya muṟimik kēṟpaveṉiṉ,- aṟṟaṉṟu; avaṟṟukku ippeyar ciṟupāṉmi yeṉpāṉ piṟkūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu. 'uṟaḻāṇṭilli'yeṉṟa mikiyāṉē,
"kimmi yuyyāk kāmar manti
kallā vaṉpaṟaḻ kiḷimutaṟ cērtti"	(kuṟun. 69)
eṉpatuṅ koḷka.	(7)
kuruḷi eṉṉum peyarkku uriyaṉa
563.	nāyē paṉṟi pulimuyal nāṉkum
	āyuṅ kālik kuruḷi yeṉpa.
itu, muṟiyāṉē nāṉkām eṇṇu muṟimikkaṇiṉṟa kuruḷiyāmā ṟuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nāyum paṉṟiyum puliyum muyalum eṉa nāṉkuṅ kuruḷi yeṉṟu collappaṭum e-ṟu.
"tiṟavāk kaṇṇa cāycevik kuruḷi"	(ciṟupāṇ. 130)
eṉavum,
"viḻiyāk kuruḷi meṉmuli cuvippak
kuḻivayiṟ ṟuñcuṅ kuṟuntāṭ paṉṟi"
eṉavum,
"irumpulik kuruḷiyiṟ ṟōṉṟuṅ kāṭṭiṭi"	(kuṟun. 47) 
eṉavum,
"kuruḷi kōṭpaṭa lañcik kuṟumuyal
valiyiṟ ṟappātu maṉṉuyi ramippa"
eṉavum varum.
'āyuṅkāli' yeṉṟataṉāl,
"ciṟuveḷ ḷaravi ṉavvarik kuruḷi"	(kuṟun. 119)
eṉpatuṅ koḷka	(8)


ituvumatu
564.	nariyu maṟṟē nāṭiṉar koḷiṉē.
(i-ḷ.) nariyuṅ kuruḷiyeṉappaṭum e-ṟu.
"piṇantiṉ peṇṭirkkuk kuruḷi kāṭṭip
puṟaṅkāṭ ṭōri pulavuttaci peṟūum"
eṉavarum.
'nāṭiṉar koḷiṉē' yeṉṟataṉāṉē,
"veyilāṭu mucuviṉ kuruḷi yuruṭṭuṅ
kuṉṟa nāṭaṉ kēṇmi"	(kuṟun. 38)
eṉṟāṟpōla mucuviṟkuṅ kuruḷippeyar koṭukka.	(9)
mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟiṟkuk kuṭṭiyum paṟamum uriyavātal
565.	kuṭṭiyum paṟaḻuṅ kūṟṟavaṇ variyār.
(i-ḷ.) mēṟkūṟiya aintaṉiyuṅ kuṭṭiyeṉṟum paṟaḻeṉṟuṅ kūṟutal variyār e-ṟu.
avi 1 nāykkuṭṭi 2 paṉṟikkuṭṭi 3 pulikkuṭṭi 4 muyaṟkuṭṭi 5narikkuṭṭi eṉa vaḻakkiṉuḷ vantaṉa.
"pācip parappiṟ paṟaḻoṭu vatinta
vuṇṇāp piṇavi ṉuyakkañ coliya
nāḷiri tarīiya veḻunta nīrnāy"	(akam. 336)
eṉavum,
"vayanā yeṟintu vaṉpaṟaḻ taḻīi
. . . . . .  . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . 
taṟukaṭ paṉṟi"	(akam. 248)
eṉavum,
"pulippaṟa ḻaṉṉa pūñciṉi vēṅki"
eṉavum,
"patavumēyal paṟṟi muyaṟpaṟa ḻōmpuñ
cīṟū rōḷē naṉṉutal"
eṉavum,
"narippaṟaḻ kavara nāymutal curakkum"
eṉavum muṟiyāṉē vantaṉa.
nāyeṉac cennāy nīrnāy mutalāyiṉavum aṭaṅkumeṉpatu. 'mūvariyaṇi' leṉṟavaḻic (561) collappaṭṭavāṟum uyttuṇarka. piṟavum aṉṉa.	(10)
mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ nāy oḻiya nāṉkaṟkum piḷḷiyum urittātal
566.	piḷḷip peyarum piḻippāṇ ṭilli
	koḷḷuṅ kāli nāyalaṅ kaṭiyē.
(i-ḷ.) mēṟkūṟiya aintu cātiyuḷḷum nāyoḻittu oḻinta nāṉkaṟkum piḷḷiyeṉṉum peyarkkoṭiyum urittu e-ṟu.
ivi ceyyuṭkaṇ varuvaṉa kaṇṭukoḷka. 'koḷḷuṅkāli' yeṉṟataṉāṉ muṟkūṟiya nāy mutalākiya nāṉkum vilakki narippiḷḷi yeṉpatē kōṭalum oṉṟu. 	(11)


maṟi eṉṟa peyar urimi
567.	yāṭuṅ kutiriyum navviyum uḻiyum
	ōṭum pulvāy uḷappaṭa maṟiyē.
(i-ḷ.) ivvintu cātiyiṉ iḷamip peyar maṟiyeṉappaṭum e-ṟu.
avi,
"maṟitturūut tokutta paṟippuṟa iṭiyaṉ"	(akam. 94)
eṉavum,
"uḷḷil vayiṟṟa veḷḷi veṇmaṟi"	(akam. 104)
eṉavum yāṭṭiṉmēl vantaṉa. maṟikkutiriyeṉavum maṟi nūkkiṟṟeṉavuñ collutaliṉ itu kutirikkum urittāyiṟṟu.
"navvi nāṇmaṟi kavvikkaṭaṉ kaḻikkum"	(kuṟun. 282)
eṉavum,
"maṟiyāṭu maruṅkiṉ maṭappiṇi yaruttit
teḷḷaṟal taḻīiya . . . . . . "	(akam. 34)
eṉavum,
"teṟittunaṭi marapiṟṟaṉ maṟikkuniḻa lāki"	(kuṟun. 213)
eṉavum ivi, navviyum uḻiyum pulvāyum muṟiyāṉē maṟiyeṉṉum peyar eytiyavāṟu avvacceyyuḷuṭ kaṇṭu koḷka.
navviyum uḻiyum pulvāyuḷ aṭaṅkumaṉṟē? avaṟṟi mūṉṟāka ōtiyateṉṉi? nāyeṉṟatupōla aṭaṅkātōveṉiṉ, māṉeṉpatu, kutiriyum yāṉiyum puliyuñ ciṅkamum mutalāyavaṟṟukkellām peyarākaliṉ avvāṟu ōtāṉeṉpatu. 'ōṭum pulvā'yeṉṟataṉāṉē maṭaṉaṭiyaṉa navviyeṉavum iṭinikaraṉa uḻiyeṉavuṅ koḷka. eṭṭām muṟimikkaṇ ōtiya maṟiyiṉi aintāmvaḻik kūṟiyataṉāṉē,
"cevvaric cēkki varuṭi māṉmaṟi"	(kuṟun. 187)
eṉṟatu pōlvaṉa koḷka.	(12)


kuṭṭi eṉṟa peyar urimi
568.	kōṭuvāḻ kuraṅkuṅ kuṭṭi kūṟupa.
(i-ḷ.) kōṭṭiṉiyē vāḻkkiyākavuṭiya kuraṅkuṅ kuṭṭiyeṉṟu kūṟappaṭum e-ṟu.
'kōṭuvāḻ kuraṅku' eṉavē kuraṅkiṉ piṟappup pakutiyellāṅ koḷka. avi kuraṅkukkuṭṭi mucukkuṭṭi ūkak kuṭṭiyeṉpaṉa. ummi iṟantatu taḻīiyiṟṟātalāṉ mēṟkūṟiya yāṭu mutalākiya aintucātikkuṅ kuṭṭiyeṉṉum peyar kūṟappaṭumeṉpatu. avi yāṭṭukkuṭṭi kutirik kuṭṭi navvikkuṭṭi uḻimāṉkuṭṭi pulvāykkuṭṭi eṉavarum, 	(13)


kuraṅkiṟkup piṟa iḷamippeyarum urimi
569.	makavum piḷḷiyum paṟaḻum pārppum
	aviyum aṉṉa vappā lāṉa.
(i-ḷ.) mēlic cūttiratteṭuttōtiya kuraṅkiṟkuk kuṭṭiyeṉ ṉum peyarēyaṉṟi makavum piḷḷiyum paṟaḻum pārppumeṉappaṭṭa innāṉkum kuṭṭiyeṉṉum peyar pōla akkuraṅkiṉ pakutikku uriyavām e-ṟu.
. . . . . . . . . . . . . "uyarkōṭṭu,
makavuṭi manti pōla"	(kuṟun. 29)
eṉavum,
"kuraṅkup piḷḷi"
eṉavum,
"variyāṭu vaṉpaṟaḻt tanti"	(kuṟun. 26)
eṉavum,
"ēṟpaṉa vēṟpaṉa vuṇṇum
pārppuṭi mantiya maliyiṟan tōrē"	(kuṟun. 278)
eṉavum varum. 'aṉṉa' veṉpataṉāṉ muṉṉiyavaṟṟōṭokkum; mikuti kuṟivu ilaveṉpatām.
"veyilāṭu mucuviṉ kuruḷi yuruṭṭum"	(kuṟun. 38)
eṉa (564) ilēciṉāṟ koḷḷumāṟukūṟippōntamiyiṉ īṇṭu atu kūṟāṉāyiṉāṉ; aṉṟi, aḵtu ittuṇip payilāmiyāṉumeṉka. ēḻāmuṟi niṉṟa makaviṉi īṇṭu vittāṉ, atikārappaṭṭa peyark kurimiyāṉeṉpatu.	(14)


kaṉṟu eṉṟa peyarkku urimi
570.	yāṉiyum kutiriyum kaḻutiyum kaṭamiyum
	āṉōṭu aintuṅ kaṉṟeṉaṟ kuriya.
(i-ḷ.) aintām eṇṇumuṟimikkaṇ niṉṟa kaṉṟeṉṉum peyarkku ivi uriya e-ṟu.
avi, yāṉikkaṉṟu kutirikkaṉṟu kaḻutikkaṉṟu kaṭamik kaṉṟu āṉkaṉṟu eṉa varum.
"kaṉṟukā loyyuṅ kaṭuñcuḻi nīttam
puṉṟali maṭappiṭip pūcal palavuṭaṉ
veṇkōṭ ṭiyāṉi viḷipaṭat tuḻavum"	(akam. 68)
eṉpatu, yāṉikkaṉṟu.
"kaṉṟupuku māli niṉṟō ḷeyti"	(akam. 9)
eṉpatu āṉkaṉṟu, piṟavum aṉṉa uḷavēṟ koḷka. iṉi, uriyaveṉṟa taṉāṉē māṉkaṉṟu kutirikkuṭṭi yeṉpaṉavuñ collupa.	(15)




ituvumatu
571.	erumiyum mariyum variyā rāṇṭē.
(i-ḷ.) erumiyum mariyuṅ kaṉṟeṉappaṭum e-ṟu.
avi, erumikkaṉṟu, mariyāṉkaṉṟu eṉpaṉa vaḻakku.
"kaṉṟuṭi mariyā tuñcum"	(kuṟun. 115)
"kaṉṟāṟṟup paṭutta puṉṟalic ciṟāar
. . . . . . . . . . . .  . . . . . . . . . . . . 
kuṉṟa nāṭaṟ kaṇṭaveṅ kaṇṇē"	(kuṟun. 241)
eṉavarum. 'variyā' reṉavē aviyellāmpōlātu ivi ciṟuvaraviṉa eṉpatu peṟṟām.	(16)

ituvumatu
572.	kavariyum karāmum nikaravaṟ ṟuḷḷē.
iḵtu. avaṟṟoṭu māṭṭeṟintatu.
(i-ḷ.) kavariyuṅ karāmuṅ kaṉṟeṉappaṭum e-ṟu.
kavarimāṉkaṉṟu karākkaṉṟu eṉavarum. 'avaṟṟuḷḷē' yeṉpatu muṟkūṟiya ēḻaṉuḷ mutaṉiṉṟa yāṉiyōṭokku meṉṟavāṟu.
itaṉ payam,
"kuñcaram peṟumē kuḻuvip peyarkkoṭi"	(tol. mara. 19)
eṉa varukiṉṟa peyarum ivaṟṟuk keytuvittalāyiṟṟu. atu muṉṉarc collum. iviyellān tammiṉotta varaviṉa aṉmiyiṉ vēṟuvēṟu cūttirañ ceykiṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu.	(17)


ituvumatu
573.	oṭṭaka mavaṟṟōṭu oruvaḻi niliyum.
(i-ḷ.) ciṟupāṉmi oṭṭakamuṅ kaṉṟeṉappaṭum e-ṟu.
aḵtu, oṭṭakakkaṉṟu eṉavarum. 'oruvaḻi'yeṉṟataṉāṉē, evaṟṟiṉum itu ciṟupāṉmi yeṉavuṇarka.	(18)


kuḻavippeyarkku urimi
574.	kuñcaram peṟumē kuḻavip peyarkkoṭi.
(i-ḷ.) oḻintuniṉṟa kuḻavippeyar kuñcarattiṟku uriyatu e-ṟu.
atu,
"oyyeṉa veḻunta cevvāyk kuḻavi"	(akam. 165)
eṉavarum. nikaravaṟṟuḷḷeṉa mēṟ kuñcarattōṭokku meṉappaṭṭa karāttiṉ kuḻaviyuṅ kavarik kuḻaviyum vantavaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka	(19)
575.	āvum erumiyum avicolap paṭumē.
(i-ḷ.) āvum erumiyum avipōlak kuḻavip peyark koṭipeṟum e-ṟu.
kuñcaram, āṇumpeṇṇumeṉa irukūṟṟaṉavākalāṉ 'avi' yeṉṟāṉ. makkaḷmēl varuṅkālum iḵtokkum.
"maṭakkaṭ kuḻavi yaṇavan taṉṉa
nōyē mākuta laṟintuñ
cēyar tōḻi cēynāṭ ṭōrē"	(kuṟun. 64)
eṉpatu āṉkuḻavi.
"mōṭṭerumi muḻukkuḻavi
kūṭṭuniḻaṟ ṟuyilvatiyum"	(paṭṭiṉap. 14)
eṉpatu, erumikkuḻavi.	(20)

ituvumatu
576.	kaṭamiyum mariyum mutaṉili yoṉṟum.
(i-ḷ.) iviyum appeyarkku uriya e-ṟu.
kuñcarampōlak kuḻavippeyar peṟumeṉpāṉ 'mutaṉili yoṉṟu'meṉṟāṉeṉpatu. avi vantuḻik kaṇṭu koḷka.	(21)

ituvumatu
577.	kuraṅku mucuvum ūkamum mūṉṟum
	nirampa nāṭiṉ appeyark kuriya.
(i-ḷ.) immūṉṟuṅ kuḻaviyeṉṉum peyarkku uriya e-ṟu.
'nirampa nāṭiṉ' eṉpatu, mūṉṟupeyarum orupiṟappiṉ pakutiyāka liṉ ammūṉṟaṟkum oppavarumeṉṟavāṟu.
ikkaruttāṉē
"kōṭuvāḻ kuraṅku"	(tol. mara. 13)
eṉṟavaḻi immūṉṟuṅ koṇṭāmeṉpatu. ivaṟṟukku utāraṇaṅ kāṇā miyiṟ kāṭṭāmāyiṉām. ilakkaṇam uṇmiyiṉ amiyu meṉpatu.	(22)


makkaṭku uriyaṉa
578.	kuḻaviyum makavum āyiraṇ ṭallavi
	kiḻava alla makkaṭ kaṇṇē.
(i-ḷ.) kuḻaviyum makavumeṉṉum iraṇṭumallatu makkaṭkaṇiṉṟa iḷami tamakku vēṟupeyaruṭiyavalla. e-ṟu.
āṇiḷamiyumpeṇṇiḷamiyumeṉa iraṇṭākaliṉ 'allavi' yeṉap paṉmi kūṟiṉāṉ.
"kāval,
kuḻavi koḷpavari ṉōmpumati"	(puṟam. 5)
eṉavum,
"māṉṟōṟ paḷḷi makavoṭu muṭaṅki"	(perumpāṇ. 89)
eṉavum varum.
'kiḻava valla' eṉṟa mikiyāṉē, āṇpiḷḷi peṇpiḷḷi yeṉap piḷḷippeyarum makkaṭpāṟpaṭuvaṉa koḷka.	(23)

ōraṟivuyirkku uriyaṉa
579.	piḷḷi kuḻavi kaṉṟē pōtteṉak
	koḷḷavum amiyum ōraṟi vuyirkkē.
(i-ḷ.) piḷḷi kuḻavi kaṉṟu pōtteṉṉum nāṉkum ōraṟi vuyiriṉ iḷimippeyar e-ṟu.
ōraṟivuyireṉpaṉa muṉṉarc (583) collappaṭum ōraṟi vuyireṉpatu, paṇputtoki.
'kamukampiḷḷi' 'teṅkampiḷḷi'
eṉavum,
"vīḻi ṟāḻik kuḻavit tīnīr"	(perumpāṇ. 357)
eṉavum,
'pūṅkaṉṟu'
eṉavum,
'pōttukkāl' 
eṉavum varum.
vīḻiṟāḻi yeṉappaṭṭatu teṅku. pōttukkāleṉpatu karumpu.
ōraṟivuyirkkuk koḷḷavum amiyum eṉṉum ummiyi eccappaṭuttup piṟavaḻiyuṅ koḷḷappaṭum. avi,
'kuḻavivēṉil'	(kali. 36)
eṉavum,
'kuḻavittiṅkaḷ'	(kali. 103, cilap. 2: 38)
eṉavum,
'kuḻaviñāyiṟu'	(peruṅ. 1: 33: 29)
eṉavum,
"pakuvāy varāal palvari yirumpōttu"	(akam. 36)
eṉavum,
"pulippōt taṉṉa pullaṇaṟ kāḷi"	(perumpāṇ. 138)
eṉavum iviyum iḷamikkuṟippiṉavākaliṟ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.
maṟṟu, ōraṟivuyir īṇṭuk kūṟiyateṉṉiyeṉiṉ, kuḻavip peyar atikārappaṭṭamiyāṉeṉpatu. maṟṟup pullum maraṉum uyireṉap paṭumōveṉiṉ, - avaṟṟi yuyireṉṟal marapupaṭṭa vaḻakkākaliṉ ammarapuṅ kōṭaṟkuk kūṟiṉāṉ, 'maram uynta'teṉpavākaliṉ.	(24)

nellum pullum avi peṟāmi
580.	nellum pullum nērā rāṇṭē.
iḵtu, eytiyatu vilakkukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) annāṉku peyarāṉum nellum pulluñ collappaṭā e-ṟu.
maṟṟup,
"puṟakkā ḻaṉavē pulleṉa moḻipa"	(tol. mara. 85)
eṉumākalāṉ, mēṟkāṭṭiya kamuku mutalākiya pullum vilakkuṇṇum piṟaveṉiṉ,-aṟṟaṉṟu; pulleṉpatu pala poruḷoru collākalāṉ nelleṉṉum iṉattāṉē vēṟupaṭuttup pulleṉpatu (puṟam. 248) uṇaviṉmēṟkoḷka.	(25)

iḷamikkup puṟaṉaṭi
581.	colliya marapiṉ iḷimi tāṉē
	colluṅ kāli aviyala vilavē.
itu, puṟaṉaṭic cūttiram.
(i-ḷ.) colliya marapiṉ iḷimi-
"pāṭaluṭ payiṉṟavi nāṭuṅ kāli"	(tol. akat. 3)
eṉavum,
"pāṭal cāṉṟa pulaṉeṟi vaḻakku"	(tol. akat. 53)
eṉavum,
akattiṇiyiyaluṭ kūṟiya pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkiṟṟākiya iḷami;
colluṅkāli aviyala ila- avaṟṟukku ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuṅ kāl, vēṟu palaviṉṟi varum, iṅṅaṉam variyaṟippaṭṭaṉa aṉṟi e-ṟu.
'iḷamiyum aviyala ila'veṉavē, ataṟku maṟutaliyākiya mutumiyāyiṉ, aviyalatilaveṉpatoru variyaṟippaṭuttu avi ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟappaṭāveṉpatu karuttu.
'colluṅkāli' yeṉṟataṉāṟ collāta iḷamip peyaruṅ koḷka. avi,
"naṉṉāṭ pūtta nākiḷa vēṅki"	(akam. 85)
eṉavum,
"nākiḷavaḷi"	(puṟam. 266)
eṉavum,
"kaṇikkōṭṭu vāḷik kamañcūṉ maṭanāku 	(kuṟun. 164)
eṉavum,
"erumi nallāṉ karunāku peṟūum"	(perumpāṇ. 165)
eṉavum varum. ivaṟṟuḷ ōraṟivuyir mutalāka aintaṟivuyiraḷavum nākeṉ ṉum iḷimippeyar variyaṟiyiṉṟic ceṉṟatu; vaṇṭeṉpataṟkum okkum, atu viravup peyarākaliṉ.	(26)

ōraṟivu mutaliyaṉa
582.	oṉṟaṟi vatuvē uṟṟaṟi vatuvē
	yiraṇṭaṟi vatuvē ataṉoṭu nāvē.
	mūṉṟaṟi vatuvē avaṟṟoṭu mūkkē
	nāṉkaṟi vatuvē avaṟṟoṭu kaṇṇē
	yintaṟi vatuvē avaṟṟoṭu ceviyē
	yāṟaṟi vatuvē avaṟṟoṭu maṉaṉē
	nēritiṉ uṇarntōr neṟippaṭut tiṉarē.
itu, mēl atikārappaṭṭa ōraṟivuyir uṇarttumvaḻi avviṉat taṉavellāṅ kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) oṉṟaṟivateṉpatu oṉṟaṉiyaṟivatu; aḵtāvatu uṟṟaṟiva teṉpatum: iraṇṭaṟivateṉpatu ammeyyuṇarviṉoṭu nāvuṇar vuṭiyateṉavum, mūṉṟaṟivuṭiyatu avaṟṟoṭu nāṟṟavuṇarvuṭiya teṉavum, nāṉkaṟivuṭiyatu avaṟṟoṭu kaṇṇuṇarvuṭiyateṉavum, aintaṟivuṭiyatu avaṟṟoṭu ceviyuṇar vuṭiyateṉavum, āṟaṟivuṭi yatu avaṟṟoṭu maṉavuṇarvuṭiyateṉavum, ammuṟiyāṉē nuṇṇuṇarvuṭiyōr neṟippaṭuttiṉar e-ṟu.
itu muṟiyātaṟkuk kāraṇameṉṉiyeṉiṉ,-eṇṇumuṟiyāṟ kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu; allatūum, ellā uyirkkum immuṟiyāṉē piṟakkum avvavaṟṟukkōtiya aṟivukaḷeṉṟaṟku ammuṟiyāṟ kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu. eṉṉi atu peṟumāṟeṉiṉ,- nēritiṉuṇarntōr neṟippaṭuttiṉareṉpataṉāṟ peṟutum.
maṟṟu, oṉṟumutal aintīṟākiya poṟiyuṇarvu maṉamiṉṟiyum piṟappaṉapōla vēṟu kūṟiyateṉṉiyeṉiṉ, - ōraṟivuyirkku maṉamiṉmiyiṉ aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiṉāreṉpatu. ataṟku uyiruṇṭāyiṉ maṉamiṉṟāmōveṉiṉ,- uyiruṭiyavākiya nantu mutalākiyavaṟṟukkuc cevi mutalāya poṟiyiṉmi kaṇṭiliyōveṉpatu. avvāṟē oḻintavaṟṟiṟkum maṉavuṇarvilli yeṉpārum maṉamuṇṭeṉpāru meṉa irupakutiyar. aviyellām vallārvāyk kēṭṭuṇarka.
allatūum poṟiyuṇarveṉpa tāmē uṇarum uṇarcci; aṅṅaṉam uṇarntavaḻip piṉṉar avaṟṟi maṉañceṉṟu koḷḷu meṉpateṉṉiyeṉiṉ,- maṉam oṉṟaṉi niṉiyāniṟka maṟṟoṉṟu kaṭpulaṉāyakkāl ataṉip poṟiyuṇarvukoḷḷa ataṉvaḻiyē maṉantirintu cellumākaliṉ; eṉṉi? maṉaṉuṇarvu maṟṟoru poruṭkaṇ niṉṟakālattup piṟaporuṭkaṭ ceṉṟateṉap paṭātaṉṟē? piṉṉar ataṉi aṟivittatu poṟiyuṇarvākalāṉ avi tammiṉ vēṟṟumiyuṭiyaṉa veṉpatu.
allatūun tēṉeyyiṉi nāviṉpoṟi uṇarntavaḻi iṉpuṟṟuṅ, kaṇṇuḷ vārttu meyyuṇarvuṇarntavaḻit tuṉpuṟṟum, naṟitāyiṉa māṉmatattiṉi mūkkuṇarvuṇarntavaḻi iṉpuṟṟuṅ, kaṇṇuṇarvuṇarntavaḻi iṉpaṅkoḷḷāmiyum varutaliṉ avi poṟiyuṇarveṉappaṭum. maṉavuṇarvum orutaṉmittātal vēṇṭumā leṉiṉ, - aiyuṇarviṉṟik kaṉāppōlat tāṉē yuṇarvatu maṉavuṇar veṉappaṭum. poṟiyuṇarvu maṉamiṉṟip piṟavāteṉiṉ,-muṟpiṟantatu maṉavuṇarvāmākavē poṟiyuṇarveṉpatu ōraṟiviṉṟākiyē cellu meṉpatu. aṟṟaṉṟiyum, oruvaṉuṟuppiraṇṭu tīṇṭiyavaḻi avviraṇṭum paṭiṉum orukaṇattuḷ orumaṉamē irumaṉappaṭṭu avvuṟuppiraṇṭaṟ kum ūṟṟuṇarvu keṭātu kavarppa vāṅkik kikkoṇṭu mīḷu meṉpatu kāṭṭalākāmiyāṉum aḵtamiyāteṉpatu.	(27)

ōraṟivu uṭiyaṉa
583.	pullum maraṉum ōraṟi viṉavē
	piṟavum uḷavē akkiḷip piṟappē.
itu, muṟiyāṉē ōraṟivuṭiyaṉa vuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) puṟakkāḻaṉavākiya pullum akakkāḻaṉavākiya maraṉum ōraṟivuṭiya; piṟavum akkiḷip piṟappu uḷḷaṉa e-ṟu.
'kiḷippiṟap' peṉpatu kiḷiyum piṟappumeṉṟavāṟu. kiḷi yeṉpaṉa, puṟakkāḻum akakkāḻumiṉṟip putaluṅ koṭiyum pōlvaṉa. piṟappeṉpaṉa, makkaḷāṉum vilaṅkāṉum īṉṟa kuḻavi ōraṟiviṉa ākiya paruvamum, eññāṉṟum ōraṟiviṉavēyākiya eṉpil puḻuvumeṉa ivi. ivi vēṟu piṟappeṉak koḷka.
maṟṟu ivaṟṟukku aṟivilli piṟaveṉiṉ, payilat toṭuṅkāṟ pularumākaliṉ ōraṟivuṭiyaveṉa vaḻakku nōkkik kūṟiṉāṉ, itu vaḻakku nūlātaliṉ. aḵtēl, ivi uṇarcciyāyiṉ iṉpatuṉpaṅ koḷḷu mō veṉiṉ, ataṟku maṉamiṉmiyiṉ atu kaṭāvaṉṟeṉpatu.	(28)

īraṟivu uṭiyaṉa
584.	nantum muraḷum īraṟi viṉavē
	piṟavum uḷavē akkiḷip piṟappē.
itu, muṟiyāṉē īruṇarvuṭiyaṉa vuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nantum muraḷum īraṟivākiya ūṟṟuṇarvum nāvuṇarvu muṭiyaṉa. piṟavum akkiḷip piṟappu uḷa e-ṟu.
iri cuvikōṭalum piṟitoṉṟu tākkiyavaḻi aṟitalu muṭimiyiṉ meyyuṇarvoṭu nāvuṇarvu muṭiyaṉa veṉṟavāṟu. ivaṟṟukkuk kiḷiyeṉpaṉa kiḷiñcilum muṟṟilum (maṭṭic cuṇṇāmpu) mutalākiya kaṭalvāḻ cātiyum piṟavumeṉak koḷka. piṟappeṉpaṉa muṟkūṟiyavāṟē koḷḷappaṭum.	(29)

mūvaṟivu uṭiyaṉa
585.	citalum eṟumpum mūvaṟi viṉavē
	piṟavum uḷavē akkiḷip piṟappē.
itu, mūvaṟiviṉa kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) citalum eṟumpum ūṟṟuṇarvum nāvuṇarvum mūkkuṇarvu muṭiya; avaṟṟuk kiḷiyum piṟappum avvāṟē mūvaṟivuṭiya e-ṟu.
ivi uṟṟuṇarntu mīṭalum nāccuvi kōṭalum neyyuḷvaḻi mōntaṟitalumeṉa mūṉṟaṟiviṉiyuṭiyavāṟu kaṇṭukoḷka. ivaṟṟuk kuk kaṇṇuñ ceviyumiṉmi eṟṟāl aṟitumeṉiṉ,- oṉṟu tākkiya vaḻiyaṉṟi aṟiyāmiyiṟ kaṇṇilaveṉpataṟitum, urappiyavaḻi ōṭāmi yiṟ ceviyilaveṉpataṟitum. ivaṟṟiṉ kiḷiyeṉpaṉa īyaṉmūtāy (akam. 14) pōlvaṉa. piṟappeṉpaṉa, muṟkūṟiyavāṟē makkaṭ kuḻaviyum vilaṅkiṉ kuḻaviyum immūṉṟuṇarvākiya paruvattaṉavum aṭṭi mutalākiyavumellāṅ koḷka.	(30)

nāṉkaṟivu uṭiyaṉa
586.	naṇṭun tumpiyum nāṉkaṟi viṉavē
	piṟavum uḷavē akkiḷip piṟappē.
naṇṭun tumpiyum nāṉkaṟiviṉaveṉavum, annālaṟiviṉiyuṭiya kiḷiyum piṟappum vēṟuḷaveṉpatūuṅ kūṟiyavāṟu.
(i-ḷ.) naṇṭiṟkun tumpikkuñ ceviyuṇarvoḻittu oḻinta nāṉku uṇarvum uḷa; avaṟṟuk kiḷiyum piṟappum piṟavum uḷa e-ṟu.
meyyuṭimiyiṉ ūṟṟuṇarvum, irikōṭaliṉ nāvuṇarvum, nāṟṟaṅkōṭaliṉ mūkkuṇarvum, kaṇṇuṭimiyiṟ kaṇṇuṇarvu muṭiyavāyiṉa. naṇṭiṟku mūkkuṇṭōveṉiṉ, aḵtu āciriyaṉ kūṟalāṉ uṇṭeṉpatu peṟṟām. ivaṟṟukkuk kiḷiyeṉpaṉa vaṇṭun tēṉīyuṅ kuḷaviyum mutalāyiṉa. piṟappeṉpaṉa nāṉkaṟivuṭiya piṟa cātikaḷeṉa muṟkūṟiyavāṟē koḷka.	(31)

aiyaṟivu uṭiyaṉa
587.	māvum mākkaḷum aiyaṟi viṉavē
	piṟavum uḷavē akkiḷip piṟappē.
iḵtu, aiyaṟivuṭiyaṉa kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aiyaṟivuṭiyaṉa vilaṅkum avi pōlvaṉa orucār māṉiṭaṅkaḷumām. akkiḷippiṟappup piṟavum uḷa e-ṟu.
māveṉpaṉa - nāṟkālvilaṅku. mākkaḷeṉappaṭuvār - maṉa vuṇarcciyillātār. kiḷiyeṉpaṉa - eṇkāl varuṭiyuṅ kuraṅkum pōlvaṉa. eṇkālavāyiṉum māveṉappaṭutaliṉ varuṭi kiḷi yāyiṟṟu. kuraṅku nāṟkālavākaliṟ kiḷiyāyiṟṟu. piṟappeṉpaṉa - kiḷiyum pāmpum mutalāyiṉa.
maṟṟup pāmpiṟkuc ceviyuṅ kaṇṇum oṉṟēyākik kaṭcevi yeṉappaṭumākaliṉ aiyaṟivilli piṟaveṉiṉ, - poṟiyeṉpaṉa vaṭivu nōkkiṉa allavākaliṉ oṉṟē iraṇṭuṇarviṟkum poṟiyāmeṉpatu. kiḷiyeṉpatu paṟaviyākaliṉ ataṉi vēṟōtukaveṉiṉ, muṉṉiya vaṟṟiṟkum paṟaviyeṉṟōtiya tilaṉākalāṉ avvaccūttiraṅkaḷāṉē ellām aṭaṅkumeṉpatu.
maṟṟup pullum maraṉum mutalāka ivviraṇṭu piṟappeṭuttōti oḻintaṉavaṟṟiyellām piṟavumuḷaveṉap puṟaṉaṭuttateṉṉi yeṉiṉ,- avi variyaṟiyilavākaliṉ aṅṅaṉaṅkūṟiṉāṉ; allatū um, makkaḷum puḷḷum vilaṅkum mutalāyiṉa ōraṟiviṉaveṉṟum īraṟi viṉaveṉṟum eṇṇi variyaṟukkappaṭāmiyālum avvāṟu kūṟiṉā ṉeṉpatu.	(32)

āṟaṟivu uṭiyaṉa
588. 	makkaḷ tāmē āṟaṟi vuyirē
	piṟavum uḷavē akkiḷip piṟappē.
iḵtu, āṟaṟivuyir kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) makkaḷeṉappaṭuvōr aimpoṟiyuṇarvēyaṉṟi maṉa meṉpa tōr aṟivum uṭiyar; akkiḷip piṟappu vēṟum uḷa e-ṟu.
muppattiraṇṭu avayavattāṉ aḷaviṟpaṭṭu aṟivoṭu puṇarnta āṭūu makaṭūu makkaḷeṉappaṭupa. avvāṟuuṇarviluṅ kuṟivu paṭṭārik kuṟintavaki aṟintu muṟkūṟiya cūttiraṅkaḷāṉē avvap piṟappiṉuṭ cērttikkoḷḷa vittāṉeṉpatu. avi, ūmuñ ceviṭuṅ kuruṭum pōlvaṉa. kiḷiyeṉappaṭuvār - tēvarun tāṉavarum mutalāyiṉār. piṟappeṉṟataṉāṟ, kuraṅku mutalākiya vilaṅkiṉuḷ aṟivuṭiyaṉa veṉappaṭum maṉavuṇarvuṭiyaṉa uḷavāyiṉ aviyum īṇṭu āṟaṟivuyirāyaṭaṅkumeṉpatu. 'tāmē'yeṉap pirittuk kūṟiṉami yāṉ nallaṟivuṭiyareṉṟaṟkuc ciṟantāreṉpatuṅ koḷka.	(33)

kaḷiṟu eṉṟa peyarkku uriyaṉa
589	vēḻakku urittē vitantukaḷiṟu eṉṟal
	kēḻal kaṇṇum kaṭivari yiṉṟē.
'makkaṭāmē āṟaṟivuyi'reṉap pirittuk kūṟiṉamiyāṉ āṇpāl atikārappaṭṭatukaṇṭu maṟṟi vilaṅkiṉuḷ āṇpāṟkuriyaṉa kūṟiya toṭaṅkiyavāṟu. niṟuttamuṟiyāṟ kūṟātu kaḷiṟṟiṉi muṟkūṟiṉāṉ apporuḷ vilaṅkiṉuṭ ciṟantamiyāṉeṉpatu. ēṟum ēṟṟiyum payiṉṟa varaviṉavākaliṉ mutaṟcūttirattuḷ (556) muṟkūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu.
(i-ḷ.) yāṉikku vitantu kaḷiṟeṉṟa lurittu; kēḻaṟkaṇṇuñ ciṟupāṉmi varum e-ṟu.
'vitan'teṉṟa vitappiṉāṟ kaḷiṟeṉpatu cātippeyar pōlavum niṟkumeṉpatu; aḵtāvatu, yāṉiyeṉṉuñ cātip peyariṉik kaḷiṟeṉṉum peyarvantu kuṟippittāṟ
"kaṭuṅkaḷiṟ ṟoruttal "	(kali. 2)
eṉṟum ākumeṉpatu.
" iralimā ṉēṟu"
eṉpatum ataṉāṟ koḷka.
paṉṟikkum avvāṟu varuvaṉavuḷavēṟ koḷka.
" kēḻaṟ paṉṟi"	(puṟam . 152)
eṉpataṉik kaḷiṟṟuppaṉṟiyeṉṟuñ collupa.	(34)
oruttal eṉṟa peyarkku uriyaṉa
590.	pulvāy puliuḻi mariyē kavari
	colliya karāmōṭu oruttal oṉṟum.
591.	vārkōṭṭu yāṉiyum paṉṟiyum aṉṉa.
592.	ēṟpuṭittu eṉpa verumik kaṇṇum.
ivi, uriyiyipu nōkki uṭaṉeḻutappaṭṭaṉa.
(i-ḷ.) ivi oṉpatupeyarum oruttaleṉṉum peyarukku oṉṟum e-ṟu.
ivaṟṟip perumpāṉmi ciṟupāṉmi paṟṟi mūṉṟu cūttirattāṉ ōtiṉāṉeṉpatu. utāraṇam,
" kāṭumīk kūṟuṅ kōṭēn toruttal"	(akam . 65)
eṉa yāṉi oruttaleṉṟāyiṟṟu. piṟavum vantavaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka.	(35 - 37)
ēṟu eṉṟa peyarkku uriyaṉa
593.	paṉṟi pulvāy uḻiyē kavari
	yeṉṟivi nāṉkum ēṟeṉaṟku uriya.
594.	erumiyum mariyum peṟṟamum aṉṉa.
595.	kaṭalvāḻ cuṟavum ēṟeṉap paṭumē.
immūṉṟu cūttirattāṉ ōtiya eṭṭuccātiyiṉ āṇpālum ēṟeṉappaṭum e-ṟu.
"kāṟṟuccuva ṭoṟṟik kaṭipuṉaṅ kavaru
mēṟṟiḷam paṉṟiyi ṉiruḷi verūum"
eṉavum,
"veruḷēṟu payiru māṅkaṇ"	(akam. 121)
eṉavum,
"porimalarn taṉṉa poṟiya maṭamāṉ
tirimarup pēṟoṭu tēraṟērk kōṭa"	(kali . 13)
eṉavum,
"ēṟṟiṉaṅ kavari yeriyeṉa veruvap
pūtta vilavattup poṅka rēṟi"
eṉavum,
"ēṟṟerumi neñcam vaṭimpi ṉiṭantiṭṭu"	(kali. 103)
eṉavum,
"varimaraṟ pāvi mariyēṟu kaṟikkum "
eṉavum,
"pulampayi rarunta vaṇṇalēṟu "	(kuṟun. 344)
eṉavum,
" cuṟavē ṟeḻutiya mōtiran toṭṭāḷ "	(kali. 84)
eṉavum varum. piṟavum aṉṉa.	(38 - 40)
pōttu eṉṟa peyarkku uriyaṉa
596.	peṟṟam erumi pulimari pulvāy
	maṟṟivi yellām pōtteṉap paṭumē.
597.	nīrvāḻ cātiyuḷ ḷaṟupiṟappu uriya
598.	mayilum eḻālum payilat tōṉṟum.
(i-ḷ.) pōtteṉṉum peyar ip patiṉmūṉṟu cātiyiṉ āṇpāṟku muriyatu  e-ṟu.
'maṟṟivi yellā' meṉṟataṉāṟ paṉṟiyum ōntiyum mutalāyiṉa vuṅ koḷḷappaṭum. nīrvāḻcātiyuḷ aṟupiṟappeṉpaṉa 1cuṟāvum 2mutaliyum 3iṭaṅkaruṅ 4karāmum 5varālum 6vāḷiyumeṉa ivi.
'payilat  tōṉṟu' meṉṟataṉāṉē nāri mutaliyaṉavuṅ koḷka. maṟṟivi payilattōṉṟumeṉiṟ cūttiram vēṟu ceytateṉṉi? mutaṟcūttirattuḷ eṇṇuka piṟaveṉiṉ, ivi paṟaviyuṭ payilat tōṉṟumākaliṉ vēṟōtiṉā ṉeṉka.
' eṟipōttu,' ' uḻupōttu.' ' erumip pōttu'
eṉavum,
" pulippōt taṉṉa pullaṇaṟ kāḷi"	(perumpāṇ. 138)
eṉavum,
' marippōttu'
eṉavum,
" kavittali mutupōttu kāli ṉoṟṟi
. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .
teṟittunaṭi marapiṟṟaṉ maṟikkuniḻa lākum "	(kuṟun. 213)
eṉavum varum.
'ellā' meṉṟataṉāṟ paṉṟippōtteṉavum varum.
" mutalip pōttu muḻumī ṉārum"	(aiṅkuṟu. 5)
eṉavum,
" pakuvāy varāaṟ palvari yirumpōttu"	(akam . 36)
eṉavum,
" vāḷi veṇpōt tuṇīiya"	(akam . 276)
eṉavum, nīrvāḻcātiyuṭ cila varum.
" mayiṟpōt tūrnta vayiṟpaṭi neṭuvēḷ"
eṉavum,
" pōttoṭu vaḻaṅkā mayilu meḻālum "
eṉavum vantavāṟu.
'payila' eṉṟataṉāṉ,
" nāri niripōt tayiri yārum"	(kuṟun . 166)
eṉavum, oḻintaṉavum ivvāṟē kaṇṭukoḷka. maṟṟu mutaliyum iṭaṅkaruṅ karāmun tammiṉ vēṟeṉappaṭumō veṉiṉ,
" karāaṅ kalitta kuṇṭukaṇ ṇakaḻi
yiṭaṅkaruṅ kuṭṭat tuṭaṉṟok kōṭi
yāmaṅ koḷpavar cuṭarniḻaṟ katūuṅ
kaṭumuraṇ mutaliya neṭunī rilañci"	(puṟam . 37)
eṉa vēṟeṉak kūṟappaṭṭaṉa veṉpatu.
iṉic, cempōtteṉpatum īṇṭuk koḷḷāmō veṉiṉ, atu peṇpāṟkum peyarākaliṉ oru peyarē; paṇpukoḷ peyaraṉ ṟeṉpatuṇarka.	(41 - 43)


irali kali eṉṟa peyarkku uriyaṉa
599.	iraliyum kaliyum pulvāyk kuriya.
600.	kalieṉ kāṭci uḻikkum urittē.
601.	niliyiṟṟu appeyar mucuviṉ kaṇṇum.
ivi mūṉṟu cūttiramum eṇṇiya mūṉṟu cātikkum iraliyuṅ kaliyumeṉṉum āṇpāṟ peyar iṉṉavā ṟuriya veṉkiṉṟaṉa.
(i-ḷ.) iraliyuṅ kaliyumeṉpaṉa pulvāykkuriya; avaṟṟuṭ kaliyeṉpatu uḻikkumurittu; akkaliyeṉpatu mucuviṟku varuṅkāl uḻikkuppōlac ciṟuvaraviṟṟaṉṟi varum e-ṟu.
' pulvā  yirali neṟṟi yaṉṉa'	(puṟam . 374)
eṉavum,
" kavittali mutukali kāli ṉoṟṟi"	(kuṟun . 213)
eṉavum,
" kaḷḷiyaṅ kāṭṭa puḷḷiyam poṟikkali"	(akam . 97)
eṉavum,
"mipaṭ ṭaṉṉa māmuka mucukkali"
eṉavum varum.
mucuviṟku nilipeṟṟateṉavē attuṇi nilipēṟiṉṟik kuraṅkiṟku varuvaṉavuṅ koḷka. atu,
" karuṅkaṭ ṭākkali perumpiṟi tuṟṟeṉa"	(kuṟun . 69)
eṉa varumāṟṟāṉ aṟika.	(44 - 46)


yāṭṭiṉ āṇpāṟpeyarkaḷ
602.	mōttiyum takarum utaḷum apparum
	yātta eṉpa yāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇē.
(i-ḷ.) ik kūṟappaṭṭa nāṉku peyarum yāṭṭiṟkuriya e-ṟu.
avi,
" veḷyāṭṭu mōtti"
eṉavum,
" takarmarup pēyppac cuṟṟupu curinta'	(akam . 101)
eṉavum,
" utaḷa
neṭuntāmpu toṭutta kuṟuntaṟi muṉṟil"	(perumpāṇ. 151, 2)
eṉavum varum.
appareṉpatu ikkālattu vīḻntatu pōlum. yātta veṉṟataṉāṟ kaṭāyeṉpatum yāṭṭiṟkup peyarākak koḷka. atu,
" nilikkōṭṭu veḷḷi naralcevik kaṭāay"	(akam. 155)
eṉa varum.
iṉik kuraṅkiṉi appareṉṟaluṅ koḷka.	(47)


cēvaṟpeyarkku uriyaṉa
603.	cēval peyarkkoṭi ciṟakoṭu civaṇum
	māyirun tūvi mayilalaṅ kaṭiyē.
(i-ḷ.) paṟappaṉavaṟṟuḷ āṇpāṟkellāñ cēvaṟpeyar urittu; avaṟṟuḷ mayiṟkāyiṉ aḵtākātu e-ṟu.
"kāmar cēva lēmañ ceppa"	(akam. 103)
eṉavum,
"vaḷikkaṭ cēval vāḷātu maṭiyiṉ"	(akam. 122)
eṉavum, 
"tokucen neṟṟik kaṇaṅkoḷ cēval"	(kuṟun. 107)
eṉavum,
"kāṉak kōḻik kavarkuraṟ cēval"	(kuṟun. 242. puṟam. 395. malipaṭu. 510)
eṉavum,
"uḷḷūrk kurīit tuḷḷunaṭic cēval"	(kuṟun. 85)
eṉavum varum. piṟavum aṉṉa.
'māyirun tūvi mayil' eṉṟataṉāṉ avi tōkiyuṭiyavākip peṇpāl pōluñ cāyalavākalāṉ āṇpāṟṟaṉmi ilaveṉpatu koḷka. eṉavē, cevvēḷ ūrnta mayiṟkāyiṉ atu nēravumpaṭu meṉpatu.	(48)

ēṟṟikku uriyaṉa

604.	āṟṟaloṭu puṇarnta vāṇpāṟku ellām
	ēṟṟik kiḷavi uritteṉa moḻipa.
(i-ḷ.) ēṟṟi yeṉṉuñ col āṟṟaloṭu kūṭiya āṇpāṟkellām urittu e-ṟu.
avi 
"kuṟuṅki yirumpulik kōḷva lēṟṟi"	(aiṅkuṟu. 216. naṟ. 36. kuṟun. 141)
eṉavum,
"cennā yēṟṟi kammeṉa vīrppa"	(akam. 111)
eṉavum,
"koṭuṅkāl mutalik kōḷva lēṟṟi"	(kuṟun.324)
eṉavum varum.
ik karuttiṉāṉē,
"piṇarmōṭṭu nantiṉ pēḻvā yēṟṟi"	(akam. 246)
eṉavum,
"puṉṟāḷ veḷḷeli mōvā yēṟṟi"	(akam. 133)
eṉavuṅ kūṟiṉār. aviyum appeyarāṉē vaḻaṅkutal āṟṟaluṭiya vākat tōṉṟum. 'ellā' meṉṟataṉāṟ ciṟupāṉmi āṟṟal illātaṉa vuṅ koḷka. atu, "iṭukāṭṭu ḷēṟṟip paṉi" (nālaṭi. 96) eṉpatu pōṉṟu varuvaṉa. piṟavum aṉṉa.	(49)

ciṅkanōkkiṟ puṟaṉaṭi
605.	āṇpāl ellām āṇeṉaṟ kuriya
	peṇpāl ellām peṇṇeṉaṟ kuriya
	kāṇpa aviyavi appā lāṉa.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya āṇpāṟ peyarkkum iṉivarum peṇpāṟ peyarkkum puṟaṉaṭi.
(i-ḷ.) āṇeṉṉuñ col ellāc cātiyuḷḷum āṇpāṟku urittu; peṇṇeṉṉuñcol ellāccātiyuḷḷum peṇpāṟku urittu; vaḻakkiṉuḷ avvāṟu kāṇappaṭum avi e-ṟu.
avi āṇyāṉi, peṇyāṉi, āṇkuraṅku, peṇkuraṅku; āṇkuruvi, peṇkuruvi eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa. ivi kāṇappaṭum eṉavē, ittuṇi viḷaṅkavārātu ciṟuvaraviṉāṉ varuvaṉavumuḷa irupālu mallātaṉaveṉpatu. āṇali peṇṇali eṉavum, āṇpaṉi peṇ paṉi eṉavum varum. piṟavum aṉṉa.	(50)

piṭi eṉṟa peyar
606.	piṭiyeṉ peṇpeyar yāṉi mēṟṟē.
itu muṟiyāṉē mūṉṟām eṇṇu muṟimikkaṇiṉṟa peṇmippeyar kūṟiya toṭaṅki āṇpāliṟ kaḷiṟu muṟkūṟiyavāṟu pōlap piṭiyiṉi muṟkūṟiyatu.
(i-ḷ.) piṭiyeṉṉum peyar yāṉikkaṇṇatu e-ṟu.
"piṭipaṭi murukkiya perumarap pūcal"	(akam. 8)
eṉa varum. 'peya'reṉṟateṉṉiyeṉiṉ, avi toṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu aṟivittaṟkeṉpatu.	(51)

peṭṭikku uriyaṉa
607.	oṭṭakam kutiri kaḻuti mariyivi
	peṭṭi eṉṉum peyarkkoṭikku uriya.
(i-ḷ.) oṭṭakamuṅ kutiriyuṅ kaḻutiyum mariyāvum peṭṭi yeṉṉum peyarpeṟum e-ṟu.
oṭṭakappeṭṭi kutirippeṭṭi kaḻutippeṭṭi mariyāṉpeṭṭi eṉa varum.	(52)


ituvumatu
608.	puḷḷum uriya appeyark keṉpa.
(i-ḷ.) ellāppuḷḷum peṭṭi yeṉṉum peyarāṉ vaḻaṅkutaṟkuriya e-ṟu.
avi, kōḻippeṭṭi mayiṟpeṭṭi yeṉa varum; piṟavum aṉṉa.	(53)

pēṭi, peṭikku uriyaṉa
609.	pēṭiyum peṭiyum nāṭiṉ oṉṟum.
(i-ḷ.) pēṭiyum peṭiyumeṉṉum iraṇṭum muṉṉar niṉṟa puḷḷiṟku oṉṟum e-ṟu.
avi kuyiṟpēṭi aṉṉappeṭi eṉa varum. 'nāṭiṉ' eṉṟata ṉāṟ peṭṭiyeṉpatu vaḻakkiṉuṭ payilāteṉpatu.	(54)


aḷakukku uriyaṉa
610.	kōḻi kūki āyiraṇṭu allavi
	cūḻuṅ kāli aḷakeṉal amiyā.	(55)
611.	appeyark kiḻami mayiṟkum urittē.
(i-ḷ.) kōḻiyuṅ kūkiyum mayilumeṉpaṉavaṟṟukku aḷakeṉ ṉum peyar uriyatu e-ṟu.
"maṉivāḻ aḷakiṉ vāṭṭoṭum peṟukuvir"	(perumpāṇ. 256)
eṉpatu, kōḻi. piṟavum aṉṉa.	(56)

piṇikku uriyaṉa
612.	pulvāy navvi uḻiyē kavari
	colvāy nāṭiṉ piṇiyeṉap paṭumē.
(i-ḷ.) piṇiyeṉṉum peṇpeyarkkuriyaṉa ivi nāṉkum e-ṟu.
"alaṅkal vāṉkaḻi yutirneṉ ṉōkkik
kalipiṇi viḷikkuṅ kāṉat tāṅkaṇ,"	(akam. 129)
eṉavum,
"ciṟutali navvip peruṅkaṇ māppiṇi"	(puṟam. 2)
eṉavum.
"ciṟutalip piṇiyiṟ ṟīrnta neṟikōṭṭu
irali māṉ"	(kuṟun. 183)
eṉavum,
"kavari māṉpiṇi narantaṅ kaṉavum"	(patiṟ. 11)
eṉavum varum.
"colvāy nāṭiṟ piṇiyeṉap paṭumē"
eṉpatu piṇiyeṉṉuñ coṟporuḷiṉi uṇmi nōkkiṟ piriyātu piṇiyum piṟa cātikkuñ cēvaṟkuñ cellumāyiṉum, marapunōkkap piṇiyeṉṟataṟkuc ciṟappuṭiyaṉa ivi eṉṟavāṟu.	(57)

piṇavukku uriyaṉa
613.	paṉṟi pulvāy nāyeṉa mūṉṟum
	oṉṟiya eṉpa piṇavueṉ peyarkkoṭi.
(i-ḷ.) ivi mūṉṟu cātiyum piṇaveṉṉum peyarkkuriyaṉa 
e-ṟu.
"neṭuntāṭ centiṉik keḻunta kēḻal
kuṟuntāṭ piṇavoṭu kuṟukal cellātu"
eṉavum,
"kuṟiyiṟik kurampik kuṟatti yōmpiya
maṭanaṭip piṇavoṭu kavarkōṭ ṭirali"
eṉavum,
"nāyppiṇa voṭuṅkiya kiḻanari yēṟṟi"
eṉavum,
"meṉpuṉiṟ ṟampiṇavu pacitteṉap pacuṅkaṭ
cennā yēṟṟi kēḻa ṟākka"	(akam. 21)
eṉavum varum.
'oṉṟiya' eṉṟataṉāṉ,
"īruyirp piṇaviṉ vayavuppaci kaḷiiya"	(akam. 72)
eṉap pulikkuṅ koḷka. piṟavum aṉṉa.	(58)


piṇavalukku uriyaṉa
614.	piṇaval eṉiṉum avaṟṟiṉ mēṟṟē.
(i-ḷ.) piṇavaleṉpatūum ammūṉṟaṟkum urittu e-ṟu.
"iriyaṟ piṇava ṟīṇṭaliṉ"	(akam. 21)
eṉpatu paṉṟi.
"nāṉmulip piṇaval coliyak kāṉoḻintu"	(akam. 248)
eṉpatuvum atu. oḻintaṉavum aṉṉa.	(59)


ā - eṉpataṟku uriyaṉa
615.	peṟṟamu merumiyum mariyum āvē.
itu, mēl 'antañ cāṉṟa' (558) veṉṉum ilēciṉāṉē āveṉpatu taḻīikkoṇṭamiyiṉ aḵtu iṉṉuḻi allatu ākātu eṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) āveṉṉum peyar peṟṟamum mariyum erumiyum peṟum e-ṟu.
avi,
"puṉiṟṟāp pāynteṉak kalaṅki"	(akam. 56)
eṉavum,
"cēṟṟunili muṉiiya ceṅkaṭ kārāṉ"	(akam. 46. kuṟun. 261)
eṉavum,
"mariyā maralkavara māri vaṟappa"	(kali. 6)
eṉavum varum.	(60)

makkaṭku uriyaṉa
616.	peṇṇum piṇāvum makkaṭ kuriya.
(i-ḷ.) peṇṇum piṇāvum uṇartiṇip peṇmikkuriya e-ṟu.
peṇṇeṉpatu,
"peṇpāl ellām peṇṇeṉaṟ kuriya"	(tol. mara. 50)
eṉṟamiyiṉ, īṇṭuk kūṟutal mikiyām piṟaveṉiṉ, aṟṟaṉṟu; accūttiram aḵṟiṇiyatikāramākaliṉ, īṇṭu vitantōtiṉāṉeṉpatu. oṉṟeṉamuṭitta (665) leṉpataṉāṉ, uyartiṇi āṇeṉpatum īṇṭē koḷḷappaṭum.
"peṇkō ḷoḻukkaṅ kaṇkoḷa nōkki"	(akam. 112)
eṉavum,
"īṉpiṇa voḻiyap pōki"	(perumpāṇ. 90)
eṉavum varum.
"āṇkaṭa ṉuṭimiyiṟ pāṇkaṭa ṉāṟṟiya"	(puṟam. 201)
eṉa āṇeṉpatu uyartiṇikkaṇ vantatu. piṟavum aṉṉa. piṇā veṉṉum ākāraviṟuti vaṉkaṇamaṉmiyiṟ kuṟiyataṉ iṟutic ciṉi keṭṭu ukarampeṟātu niṉṟatu. 'makkaṭkuriya' veṉavē, uriyavaṉṟit
"tuṟukal viṭaraḷip piṇavuppaci kūrnteṉa"	(akam. 146)
eṉap puli mutaliyaṉavaṟṟiṟkuṅ koḷka.	(61)

nāku eṉpataṟku uriyaṉa
617.	erumiyum mariyum peṟṟamum nākē.
618.	nīrvāḻ cātiyuḷ nantum nākē.
(i-ḷ.) innāṉkaṟkum nākeṉum peyar urittu e-ṟu.
"erumi nallāṉ karunāku peṟūum"	(perumpāṇ. 165) 
eṉavum,
"uṭaṉili vēṭkiyiṉ maṭanāku taḻīi
yūrvayiṟ peyarum poḻutiṉ"	(akam. 64)
eṉavum,
"nākiḷa vaḷiyoṭu pakaṉmaṇam pukūum"	(puṟam. 266)
eṉavum varum.	(62, 63)	

yāṭṭiṉ peṇpāṟ peyarkaḷ
619.	mūṭuṅ kaṭamiyum yāṭala peṟā.
(i-ḷ.) ivviru peyarum yāṭṭiṟkēyuriya peṇmip peyar e-ṟu.
ivi ippoḻutu vaḻakkiṉuḷ ariya.	(64)

pāṭṭi eṉṟa peyar
620.	pāṭṭi yeṉpatu paṉṟiyum nāyum.
621.	nariyum aṟṟē nāṭiṉar koḷiṉē.
(i-ḷ.) pāṭṭiyeṉṟu collappaṭuvaṉa paṉṟiyum nāyum, nariyum e-ṟu.
piṟavum aṉṉa.	(65, 66)
manti eṉṟa peyar
622.	kuraṅkum mucuvum ūkamum manti.
(i-ḷ.) immūṉṟu cātip peṇpālum mantiyeṉṉum peyar peṟum e-ṟu.
"kimmi yuyyāk kāmar manti"	(kuṟun. 69)
eṉavum,
"karumuka manti cempi ṉēṟṟi"
eṉavum varum.
ūkattukkum iḵtokkum.	(67)

maruvi vanta muṭipu
623.	kuraṅkiṉ ēṟṟiṉik kaṭuvaṉ eṉṟalum
	marampayil kūkiyik kōṭṭāṉ eṉṟaluñ
	cevvāyk kiḷiyit tatti eṉṟalum
	vevvāy verukiṉip pūci eṉṟaluṅ
	kutiriyuḷ āṇiṉic cēval eṉṟalu
	miruḷniṟap paṉṟiyi yēṉam eṉṟalu
	merumiyuḷ āṇiṉik kaṇṭi eṉṟalu
	muṭiya vanta avvaḻakku uṇmiyiṉ
	kaṭiyal ākā kaṭaṉaṟin tōrkkē.
itu. marīivantu muṭinta marapuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āṇkuraṅkiṉik kaṭuvaṉeṉṟalum, marappotumpiṉuḷ vāḻuṅ kūkiyik kōṭṭāṉeṉṟalum, cevvāyk kiḷiyit tattiyeṉṟalum, verukiṉip pūciyeṉṟalum, āṇkutiriyic cēvaleṉṟalum, iruṇiṟap paṉṟiyi ēṉameṉṟalum, erumiyēṟ ṟiṉik kaṇṭiyeṉṟalum, avvāṟu muṭinta vaḻakkuṇmiyiṟ kaṭiyappaṭā kaṭappāṭu aṟintōrkku e-ṟu.
"kaṭuvaṉu maṟiyumak koṭiyō ṉiyē"	(kuṟun. 26)
iḵtu āṇkuraṅku, itaṉik 'kaṭiyalākā'teṉappaṭṭa iḻuk keṉṉiyeṉiṉ, makkaṭkum verukiṟkum akkālattup payiṉṟaṉa pōlumātaliṉ. kūkiyik kōṭṭāṉeṉṟalum vaḻakkākalāṉ amiyum; 'marampayil kūki' yeṉṟa teṉṉiyeṉiṉ, marakkōṭu viṭāmiyiṟ kōṭṭāṉeṉṉum peyarpeṟṟateṉṟaṟku. tatti yeṉpatu peruṅkiḷiyātaliṟ ciṟukiḷikkum appeyar koṭutta lamiyu meṉṟavāṟu. 'cevvāyk kiḷi' yeṉṟataṉic ciṟukiḷimēṟ koḷka.
'vevvāy veru' keṉṟataṉāṟ, paṭappi vēliyum putalum paṟṟi viṭakkiṟku vēṟṟuyirkoḷḷum verukiṉi, 'illuṟi pūciyiṉ' peyarkoṭuttuc collalum (puṟam. 117, 326) amiyumeṉṟavāṟu.
kutiriyic cēvaleṉṟal ikkālattaritāyiṟṟu. atuvuñ ciṟakoṭu civaṇātāyiṉum ataṉik kaṭuvicipaṟṟip paṟappatupōlac collutal amiyumeṉpatu karuttu.
erumiyēṟṟiṉik kaṇṭi yeṉpapōlum. atu kāṇalāyiṟ ṟilli. ataṉi, ilakkaṇivakiyāṉuṭiya peyareṉṟalumām; itupoḻu tiṉṟeṉpatu.
'kaṭaṉaṟintō' reṉṟataṉāṉ, vaḻakkiṉuñ ceyyuḷiṉum avi vantamiyiṟ kaṭappāṭaṟivōrkkuk kaṭiyalākāteṉṟavāṟu. iṉṉum ipparikārattāṉē kōḻiyi vāraṇameṉṟalum verukiṉi viṭi yeṉṟalum pōlvaṉa palavuṅ koḷka.
avi,
"kāṉa vāraṇa mīṉuṅ
kāṭāki viḷiyu nāṭuṭi yōrē."	(puṟam. 52)
eṉavum,
"vāraṇak koṭiyoṭu vayiṟpaṭa niṟīi"	(muruku. 219)
eṉavum,
"verukku viṭiyaṉṉa veruṇōkkuk kayantali."	(puṟam. 324)
eṉavum varum.	(68)

ituvumatu
624.	peṇṇum āṇum piḷḷiyum aviyē.
ituvumatu. peṇ āṇeṉpaṉa irutiṇippeṇmikkum āṇmikkum potuveṉpaṉa muṟkūṟiṉāṉ; mēl oṉṟaṟkuriya peyar marīi vantu piṟitoṉṟaṟkāyavaḻiyuṅ kaṭiyalākāteṉa niṉṟa atikārattāṉ iviyum avvāṟē tiriyiṉuṅ kaṭiyalākāteṉṟavāṟu.
(i-ḷ.) peṇṇum āṇum piḷḷiyumeṉa vāḷātu colliya vaḻi uyartiṇikkēṟṟaṉa marīivanta marapu e-ṟu.
maka, kuḻaviyeṉpaṉavōveṉiṉ, avi attuṇip payiṉṟila uyartiṇikkeṉpatu; eṭuttōtiya mūṉṟum āyiṉa. vāḷātē 'peṇvanta' teṉṟavaḻi aḵṟiṇipporuḷeṉpatu uṇaralākātu; 'peṇkuraṅku vantatu' eṉa vitantē kūṟalvēṇṭumeṉpatu. 'peṇ piṟantatu' 'āṇ piṟantatu' 'piḷḷi piṟantatu,' eṉa aṭiyaṭātu col liyavaḻi uyartiṇikkēyām. aḵṟiṇikkāyiṉ aṟṟaṉṟeṉpatu.			(69)


antaṇarkku uriyaṉa
625.	nūlē karakam mukkōl maṇiyē
	āyuṅ kāli antaṇarkku uriya.
iḵtu, uyartiṇi nāṉkucātiyum paṟṟiya marapu uṇarttuvāṉ, muṟiyāṉē antaṇarkkuriya marapupaṭṭuvaruṅ kalappi vēṟupāṭu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) munnūluṅ kuṇṭikiyum mukkōlum yāmimaṇiyum pōlvaṉa antaṇarkku uriya e-ṟu.
'āyuṅkāli' yeṉṟataṉāṟ kuṭiyuñ ceruppum mutalāyiṉavum oppaṉa aṟintukoḷka.
u-m :
"eṟittaru katirtāṅki yēntiya kuṭinīḻa
luṟittāḻnta karakamu muricāṉṟa mukkōlum"	(kali. 9)
eṉavum,
"taṇṭoṭu piṭitta tāḻkamaṇ ṭalattup
paṭiva vuṇṭip pārppaṉa makaṉē"	(kuṟun. 156)
eṉavum varum.
iṉṉum 'āyuṅkāli' yeṉṟataṉāṉ, orukōluṭiyār iruvaruḷar. avar tuṟavaṟattu niṉṟārākaliṉ ulakiyaliṉ ārāyappaṭā reṉpatu. mukkōluṭiyār iruvaruṭ picci koḷvāṉum piṟāṇ ṭiruntu taṉatuṇpāṉum ulakiyaliṉ nīṅkāmiyiṉ avariyē varin tōtiṉāṉeṉpatu.
maṟṟu aracarkkum vaṇikarkkum uriya nūliṉi īṇṭu varintō tiṉateṉṉiyeṉiṉ,  orukōluṭiyāṉ nūl kaḷivāṉākaliṉ avaṉuñ ciṟupāṉmi antaṇaṉeṉappaṭumeṉpatu kōṭaṟkuṅ karaka mum maṇiyum uṭiya ṉeṉṟaṟkumeṉpatu. nūliṉi muṟkūṟiṉāṉ piṟappu muṟiyāṉuñ ciṟappu muṟiyāṉumeṉpatu. iṉik kuṭumiyuṅ kuciyumpōlvaṉa kūṟiṟṟilaṉ, atu muṉṉarum piṉṉarum varutalāṉum, maṉṉarum vaṇikarum peṟutalāṉumeṉpatu.	(70)

aracarkku uriyaṉa
626.	paṭiyum koṭiyum kuṭiyum muracum
		naṭinavil puraviyum kaḷiṟum tērum
		tārum muṭiyum nērvaṉa piṟavum
		terivukoḷ ceṅkōl aracark kuriya.
itu, muṟiyāṉē aracarkkuriyaṉa kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) koṭippaṭiyuṅ koṭiyuṅ kuṭiyum muracuṅ kutiriyum yāṉiyun tērun tārum muṭiyum poruntuvaṉa piṟavum aracarkkuriya e-ṟu.
"piṟavum' eṉṟataṉāṟ kavariyum ariyaṇiyum araṇmutalāyiṉa vuṅ koḷka; 'terivukoḷ ceṅkōl araca' reṉṟataṉāṉē ceṅkōl koḷappaṭṭatu.
'tāre'ṉavē pōrppūvun tārppūvum aṭaṅkiṉa. 'paṭi' yeṉpuḻi 'naṭinavil puraviyuṅ kaḷiṟun tērum' aṭaṅkāvō veṉiṉ,aṭaṅkum. avinōkkik kūṟiṉāṉallaṉ;  avi paṭṭañ cāttiya vātal nōkkik kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. aḵtēl, tērkūṟiyateṉṉiyeṉiṉ,atuvum avipōla aracarkkē uriya teṉṟuḷateṉṟaṟkum, atu pūṇṭa kutiriyum avarkkē yuriyaveṉṟuḷa veṉṟaṟkuṅ kūṟiṉāṉeṉ patu. ikkaruttiṉāṟpōlum naṭinavil puraviyeṉac ciṟappittu ataṉi muṟkūṟiyateṉpatu.
ellāvaṟṟiṉuñ ciṟantatātalāṉ muṭipiṟ kūṟappaṭṭatu. 'terivu koḷ ceṅkōl' aracareṉpataṉāṉ aracarellān tannāṭṭu naṉṟun tītum ārāyntu ataṟkut takka taṇṭañ ceytaṟku urimiyum atu veṉak koḷka.	(71)

puṟaṉaṭi
627.	anta ṇāḷarkku uriyavum aracarkku
		oṉṟiya varūum poruḷumā ruḷavē.
itu, mēlataṟkoru puṟaṉaṭi.
(i-ḷ.) antaṇāḷarkku uriyaveṉa mēl (625) ōtap paṭṭaṉa vaṟṟuḷ munnūlum maṇiyumpōlvaṉa aracacātikkuriyavākiyum varum e-ṟu.
'poruḷumāruḷa' veṉṟataṉāṉ, antaṇāḷark kuriyaṉavaṟṟuḷ vēḷvik kalappi yoṉṟivarutal. perumpāṉmiyeṉa uṇarka. īṇṭu avaṟṟi vitantōtiṉāṉ, oḻinta, pulaṉeṟivaḻakkiṉuṭ payilāmiyi ṉeṉpatu.
'aṟuvakip paṭṭa pārppaṉap pakkamum
aivaki marapiṉ aracar pakkamum
irumūṉṟu marapiṉ ēṉōr pakkamum.'	(tol. puṟat. 20)
paṟṟi vākipporuḷ piṟattaliṉ avaṟṟip puṟattiṇiyiyaluṭ kūṟiṉāṉāyiṉum, īṇṭut taḻīikkoḷḷappaṭum, marapuvakiyāṉeṉpatu; eṉpōlaveṉiṉ,
'a i u am mūṉṟuñ cuṭṭu.' 	(tol. eḻut. nūṉ. 31)
eṉa nūṉmarapiṉuḷ ōtiya mūvakiccuṭṭiṉi iṭiccolōttiṉuḷ ōtāṉāyiṉāṉ, atu pōlaveṉpatu.
"koṉṟukaḷam vēṭṭa ñāṉṟi"	(akam. 36) 
eṉavum,
"aṭukaḷam vēṭṭa vaṭupōrc ceḻiya.'	(puṟam. 26)
eṉavum aracarkku vēḷvi kūṟiṉavāṟu. piṟavum aṉṉa.	(72)

aiyam aṟuttal
628.	paricilpā ṭāṇṭiṇit tuṟikkiḻa mippeyar
		neṭuntaki cemmal eṉṟuivi piṟavum
		poruntac colluta lavarkkuurittu aṉṟē.
iḵtu,  aiyam aṟuttatu; antaṇāḷarkkuriyaṉa aracarkku muriyaṉa uḷaveṉak kēṭṭa māṇākkaṉ 'aracarkkuriyaṉavum antaṇark kuriyakol'leṉṟu aiyuṟāmaṟ kāttamiyiṉ,  iḵtu, eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuvitiyeṉiṉum amiyum; eṉṉi? īṇṭu ōtappaṭṭavi aracarkkuriyaveṉpatu koḷḷavittamiyiṉ.
(i-ḷ.) paricil - paricilkaṭāniliyum, paricilviṭiyum pōlvaṉa; pāṭāṇṭiṇittuṟikkiḻamippeyar - pāṭāṇṭiṇikkuriya kikkiḷipporuḷ paṟṟiyuṅ koṭittoḻil paṟṟiyum peṟum peyar; avi kāḷi iḷiyōṉ eṉpaṉa pōlvaṉa. aviyum, neṭuntaki cemmaleṉpaṉa mutalāyiṉavum, ivipōlvaṉa piṟavum puṉinturi vakiyāṟ colliṉallatu cātivakiyāṟ kūṟutal antaṇarkkurittaṉṟu e-ṟu.
paricilkaṭāniliyum paricilviṭiyum pōlvaṉa kūṟiyuṅ, kikkiḷipporuḷ kūṟiyuṅ, koṭittoḻil kūṟiyum, avaṟṟuk kēṟpa eṭuttōtiya peyarkūṟiyum, antaṇarit taṉmi vakiyāṟ ceyyuḷ ceyyappeṟāveṉpatu karuttu.
puṉinturi vakiyāṉ aviyāmāṟu:
"eṇnāṇap palavēṭṭu
maṇnāṇap pukaḻparappiyum."	(puṟam. 166)
eṉa varum.
koṭuttaṟṟoḻil vēḷvikkālattateṉa variyaṟuttaliṟ poruntak kūṟutal avarkkurittaṉṟeṉṟāṉeṉpatu.
pāṭāṇṭiṇit tuṟippeyareṉṉātu, kiḻamippeyareṉṟa teṉṉiyeṉiṉ, avi, aintiṇippeyarāki varuṅkālum avark kuriyavalla veṉṟaṟku. eṉavē, aracarkkāyiṉ iviyellām uriyaveṉpa vāyiṟṟu.
maṟṟup pāṭāṇṭiṇikkuriyavallaveṉa maṟṟittiṇik kiḻamippeyar uriyavām piṟaveṉiṉ, -aḵtu,
'iṭiyiru vakiyōr allatu nāṭiṟ
paṭivaki peṟāar.'	(tol. mara. 76)
eṉa mēṟkūṟi vilakkumeṉpatu.	(73)


nāṟpālārkkum potuvāṉa ceyti
629.	ūrum peyarum uṭittoḻil karuviyum
		yāruñ cārtti aviyavi peṟumē.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya antaṇarkkum aracarkkum uriyaṉavaṟṟōṭu oḻinta cātiyōrkkum oppaṉa uṭaṉkūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nāṉku cātiyārum piṟanta ūrum, avartam peyarum, avarcātikku uritteṉṟaṟkēṟṟa karuviyum, yāruñ cārttappaṭṭu avi avi peṟupa e-ṟu.
immūṉṟum variyaṟuttuc collappaṭā, ellāc cātiyārk kum oppaccellumeṉpatu karuttu; eṟṟukku? ivi cātipaṟṟi vēṟupaṭāp poruḷākaliṉ. ūrum peyarumeṉpaṉa: uṟiyūr ēṇiccēri muṭamōci, peruṅkuṉṟūrp peruṅkucikaṉ, kaṭiyalū ruruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉ eṉpaṉa antaṇarkkuriyaṉa: uṟiyūrc cōḻaṉ, maturip pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉpaṉa aracarkkuriyaṉa; kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattuk kārikkaṇṇaṉ, maturi aṟuvivāṇikaṉ iḷavēṭṭaṉ eṉpaṉa vaṇikarkkuriyaṉa ; amparkiḻāṉ nākaṉ, vallaṅ kiḻāṉ māṟaṉ eṉpaṉa vēḷāḷarkkuriyaṉa.
iṉi, 'uṭittoḻiṟ karuvi'yeṉpaṉa, antaṇāḷarkkuc curuviyuñ camitikuṟikkuṅ karuviyum mutalāyiṉa; aracarkkuk kuṭiyum kūḻum amiccum naṭpum mutalāyiṉa; vaṇikarkku nāvāyum maṇiyum maruntum mutalāyiṉa; vēḷāḷarkku nāñciluñ cakaṭamum mutalāyiṉa. piṟavum aṉṉa. aviyellām avaravar ceyyuṭkuriyaveṉpatu.	(74)


nālvarkkum talimik kuṇaccol
630.	 talimik kuṇaccollun tattamakku uriya
		nilimikku ēṟpa nikaḻttuka veṉpa.
ituvumatu.
(i-ḷ.) annāṉkucātiyār talimikkuṇampaṭac colluñ collum avaravarkkuriya nilimikkēṟpa nikaḻttavumpaṭum e-ṟu.
antaṇar talimikkuṇaṅ kūṟuṅkāṟ piramaṉoṭu kūṟiyum, aracari māyaṉoṭu kūṟiyum, vaṇikari nitiyiṉkiḻavaṉoṭu kūṟiyum, vēḷāṇmāntari varuṇaṉoṭu kūṟiyun, talimik kuṇaccol nikaḻttappaṭum. aviyellām avaravar ceyyuḷuṭ kaṇṭu koḷka.(75)


paṭi peṟuvōr
631.	iṭiiru vakiyōr allatu nāṭiṉ
		paṭivaki peṟāar eṉmaṉār pulavar.
	mēl nāṉku varuṇattāriyun uṭaṉ kūṟivantāṉ, itu mutalum kaṭiyum oḻittu iṭiniṉṟa iruvaruṇattārkkum āvatoru puṟaṉaṭi kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aracarum vaṇikarumallatu paṭippakuti peṟār e-ṟu.
paṭippakuti yeṉpaṉa, vēlum vāḷum villum mutalāyiṉa. 'nāṭi'ṉeṉpataṉāṉ, orucār antaṇarum paṭikkuriyā reṉpatu koḷka. avar iyamataṅkiyārun turōṇaṉum kirupaṉum mutalāyiṉā reṉak koḷka. vēḷāṇmāntarkkum iḵtokkum. ivi vikāra meṉavum eṭuttōtiya varuṇaṅkaṭkē iḵtiyalpeṉavuṅ koḷka.	(76)

viciyaṉ marapu
632.	viciyaṉ peṟumē vāṇika vāḻkki.
itu, vaṇikar marapu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vaṇikarkkut toḻilākiya vāṇikavāḻkki uḷḷuṟi yākac ceyyuḷ ceytal perumpāṉmiyumām (e- ṟu).
aviyum avar ceyyuḷiṟ kāṇappaṭumāṟu aṟintukoḷka.	(77)


ituvumatu
633.	meyteri vakiyiṉ eṇvaki uṇaviṉ
	ceytiyum variyār appā lāṉa.
iṉṉum vaṇikarkkē uriya toḻil kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) poruḷ terinta vakiyāṉ eṇvakik kūlamuñ ceyyil viḷittalum avarkaṭaṉ e-ṟu.
aviyum avar paṇṭattōṭu upakārappaṭumākaliṉ variyap paṭāteṉṟāṉeṉpatu.
eṇvakiyuṇaveṉpaṉa 1payaṟum 2uḻuntuṅ 3kaṭukuṅ 4kaṭaliyum 5eḷḷuṅ 6koḷḷum 7avariyun 8tuvariyumām.	(78)


ituvumatu
634.	kaṇṇiyum tārum eṇṇiṉa rāṇṭē.
ituvumatu.
(i-ḷ.) iviyum vaṇikar tiṟattaṉa e-ṟu.
kaṇṇiyeṉpatu, cūṭumpū. tāreṉpatu, orukuṭip piṟantārkkurit teṉa variyaṟukkappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. eṇṇappaṭumeṉavē, avaravarkkuriya vāṟṟāṟ palavākivarum. avi vantavaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka.	(79)


vēḷāḷar marapu
635.	vēḷāṇ māntarkku uḻutūṇ allatu
	illeṉa moḻipa piṟavaki nikaḻcci.
itu, vēḷāṇmāntarkkuk kūṟappaṭun toḻil kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) vēḷāṇmāntar palavakippaṭṭa toḻilarēṉum uḻuntoḻilē perumpāṉmittākalāṉ ataṉiyē ciṟappittuc collutaṉ marapu e-ṟu.
' uḻutuṇṭu vāḻvār.'	(kuṟaḷ . 1033)
