eṉpatu, itaṉ poruḷāyiṟṟu. maṟṟup pārppiyaṉ mutalākiya nālvakit toḻilum vākiyuṭ kūṟiṉamiyiṉ iccūttiramum mēlic cūttiraṅkaḷum mikiyām piṟaveṉiṉ, aṟṟaṉṟu; pārppiyalum araciyalum vāṇikattoḻilumākip potuppaṭaniṉṟa ōtalum vēṭṭalum ītalum ivarkku otta ciṟappiṉavākalāṉum, avaruḷ vaṇikarkkum oḻinta vēḷāḷarkkum otta ceytiyaṉavākiya uḻavut toḻilum nirikāttalum vāṇikamumeṉpaṉa avaṟṟiṉ otta ciṟappiṉa vaṉṟi avaṟṟuḷḷum orōvoṉṟu orōvaruṇattārkku uriyatāmāka lāṉum īṇṭu avi vitantukūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu. nirikāvalum uḻavut toḻilum vaṇikarkkum vēḷāḷarkkun taṭumāṟutalpōlātu, vāṇika vāḻkki vēḷāṇ māntarkkuc ciṟuvaraviṟṟeṉavum, uḻutuṇṭal vaṇikarkkuc ciṟuvaraviṟṟeṉavum, eṇvakikkūlattoṭu paṭṭatē peruvaraviṟṟeṉavuṅ kūṟiṉāṉ iccūttiraṅkaḷāṉeṉpatu. itaṉatu payam : pulaṉeṟivaḻakkiṉuḷ ivarkku ivi ciṟanta marapeṉṟalāyiṟṟu.(80)


potup puṟaṉaṭi
636.	vēntuviṭu toḻiliṉ paṭiyum kaṇṇiyum
	vāyntaṉar eṉpa vavarpeṟum poruḷē.
itu, nāṉkām varuṇattārkkoru puṟaṉaṭi.
(i-ḷ.) vēntar koṭuppiṉ vēḷāṇmāntarkkup paṭikkalamuṅ kaṇṇiyum peṟumporuḷākac collappaṭum e-ṟu.
'vēntu viṭutoḻi'leṉpatu, vēntaṉāṟ koṭukkappaṭum taṇṭattalimi yākiya ciṟappuk kāraṇattā ṉeṉṟavāṟu; akattiṇi yiyaluḷ,
" uyarntōrkku uriya vōtti ṉāṉa."
eṉpuḻik kūṟappaṭṭateṉiṉ, vēḷāḷari yoḻittu oḻintōri nōkkiṟṟu accūttirameṉpatu. mēṟkūṟiyavaṟṟiyum marapupaṟṟi īṇṭu variyaṟi kūṟukiṉṟavāṟeṉak koḷka. aviyum avaravar pāṭṭukka ḷuṭ kaṇṭukoḷka.	(81)


ituvumatu
637.	anta ṇāḷarkku aracuvari viṉṟē.
iḵtu, ellāvaṟṟiluñ ciṟuvaraviṟṟāki aracarkkuriya toḻil antaṇarkkuriyavākaliṉ īṇṭup pōtantu kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) aracar ilvaḻi antaṇarē avvaraciyal pūṇṭoḻukalum variyappaṭātu e-ṟu.
makkaḷit tiṉṟa maṉṉavarkkup piṉṉi maṟiyōrāṉ aracu tōṟṟappaṭṭāṟpōlak koḷka.	(82)


ituvumatu
638.	villum vēlum kaḻalum kaṇṇiyun
	tārum māliyum tērum vāḷum
	maṉpeṟu marapiṉ ēṉōrkkum uriya.
itu, muṭiyuṭivēntarallāk kuṟunila maṉṉarkkuriyaṉa kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ivveṇṇappaṭṭaṉa vellāṅ kuṟunilamaṉṉarkkum uriyaṉa e-ṟu.
'maṉpeṟu marapiṉ ēṉō' reṉappaṭuvār aracupeṟumarapiṟ kuṟunilamaṉṉar eṉak koḷka. avi perumpāṇāṟṟuḷḷum piṟavaṟ ṟuḷḷuṅ kāṇappaṭum.	(83)


ituvumatu
639.	aṉṉar āyiṉum iḻintōrkku illi.
iḵtu, eytiya tikantupaṭāmik kāttatu.
(i-ḷ.) maṉṉavar pōluñ celvattārākiya iḻikulattōr nāṭāṇṭārāyiṉum avarkku ivi kūṟalamiyātu (e- ṟu).
" maṉpeṟu marapi ṉēṉōr.'	(tol.mara. 83)
eṉavē, aracar viciyaraṉṟik kīḻ amiyāvāyiṟṟu,	(84)


pullum maraṉum
640.	puṟakkā ḻaṉavē pulleṉa moḻipa
	akakkā ḻaṉavē maraṉeṉa moḻipa.
itu, makkaḷi vaḻaṅkumāṟṟukkaṇ marapukūṟi iṉi ōraṟivuyirkku iṉṉavāṟu collutaṉ marapeṉpa tuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) puṟattuk kāḻppuṭiyaṉavaṟṟip pulleṉavum akattuk kāḻppuṭiyaṉavaṟṟi marameṉavuñ collupa e-ṟu.
' puṟakkāḻaṉa'veṉavē,alvaḻi veḷiṟeṉpataṟiyappaṭum, avi. paṉiyun teṅkuṅ kamukum mutalāyiṉa pulleṉappaṭum. iruppiyum puḷiyum āccāvum mutalāyiṉa marameṉappaṭum. iṅṅaṉam variyaṟi kūṟip payantateṉṉi? puṟattum akattuṅ koṭi mutalāyiṉa kāḻppiṉṟi yum akiṉmaraṉ pōlvaṉa iṭiyiṭi poypaṭṭum pullum maraṉum varuvaṉa uḷavāleṉiṉ, iraṇṭiṭattum ēkāram pirittuk kūṟiṉami yāṉum ' eṉamoḻipa' eṉṟu iruvaḻiyuñ ciṟappittu vitantamiyāṉuñ ciṟupāṉmi aviyum pullum maraṉumeṉa aṭaṅkumeṉpatu.
" irumpaṉam pulliṉ pacuntōṭṭuk kuṭampi"
eṉavum, 
" yāaṅ koṉṟa marañcuṭ ṭiyaviṉ."	(kuṟun. 198)
eṉavum varum.
" nilāvi ṉilaṅku maṇaṉmali maṟukiṟ
pulālañ cērip pulvēy kurampi."	(akam. 200)
eṉap puṟakkāḻum akakkāḻum illaṉa pulleṉappaṭṭaṉa veṉpatu. atu paṉiyōliyumākaliṟ puṟakkāḻuṭiya paṉiyumāmeṉpatu. uti marak kiḷaviyumāmeṉpatu ciṟupāṉmi. piṟavum aṉṉa.	(85)


pulliṉ uṟuppukkaḷ
641.	tōṭē maṭalē ōli yeṉṟā
	ēṭē itaḻē pāḷi yeṉṟā
	īrkkē kuliyeṉa nērntaṉa piṟavum
	pulloṭu varumeṉac colliṉar pulavar.
itu, muṟiyāṉē puṟakkāḻaṉavaṟṟuṟuppiṉic collum marapuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) eṇṇappaṭṭa vāypāṭum piṟavum pulluṟuppiṉic collum vāypāṭu e-ṟu.
'piṟavu' meṉṟataṉāṟ kurumpi nuṅku nukumpu pōntiyeṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattaṉavuṅ koḷka.
"irumpaṉi veṇṭōṭu milintō ṉallaṉ"	(puṟam. 45)
eṉavum,
"māveṉa maṭalu mūrpa"	(kuṟun. 17)
eṉavum?
"tukaṭapu kāṭci yaviyattā rōli"	(kali. 94)
eṉavum varum. oḻintaṉavuṅ koḷka.
"vaṇṭōṭṭu nelliṉ varukatir vaṇaṅka"	(neṭunal. 22)
eṉpatum atu.
'nērntaṉa piṟavum' eṉpatu kēṭṭāri uṭampaṭuvippaṉa piṟavumeṉṟavāṟu.
"vaṇkōṭ peṇṇi vaḷarnta nuṅkiṉ"	(ciṟupāṇ. 27)
eṉavum,
"paṉinukum paṉṉa ciṉimutir varāloṭu"	(puṟam. 249)
eṉavum varum. piṟavum aṉṉa.	(86)


marattiṉ uṟuppukkaḷ
642.	iliyē taḷirē muṟiyē tōṭē
	ciṉiyē kuḻiyē pūvē arumpē
	naṉiuḷ ḷuṟutta vaṉiyavi yellām
	maraṉoṭu varūuṅ kiḷavi yeṉpa..
iḵtu, akakkāḻaṉavaṟṟuṟuppiṉic collum marapuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) eṇṇappaṭṭaṉavum piṟavumellām marattuṟuppiṉic collum vāypāṭu e-ṟu.
'aṉiyavi yellām' eṉṟa puṟanaṭiyāṉē pullun taḻiyum poṅkarum mutalāyiṉa koḷka. avi
"teyva maṭiyiṟ ṟēkkili kuvii"	(perumpāṇ. 104)
eṉavum,
"muṟimē yākki"	(malipaṭu. 313)
eṉavum,
"yāa voṇṭaḷi rarakku vitirttaṉṉa"	(akam. 166)
eṉavum,
"tōṭutōy malirniri yāṭi yōrē"	(akam. 166)
eṉavum,
"mēkkeḻum peruñciṉi yirunta tōki"	(kuṟun. 26)
eṉavum,
"koykuḻi yakikāñcit tuṟiyaṇi nallūra"	(kali. 74)
eṉavum,
"naṉitta cerunti"	(akam. 150)
eṉavum,
"aṇṭar makaḷir taṇṭaḻi yuṭīiyar
marañcela mititta māal pōla"	(akam. 59)
eṉṟāṟpōla varuvaṉavumellāṅ koḷka.
mēlic cūttirattup
"pulloṭu varumeṉac colliṉar"	(tol. 641)
eṉavum,
iccūttirattu
"maraṉoṭu varūuṅ kiḷavi"	(tol. 642)
yeṉavum mikipaṭak kūṟiyateṉṉiyeṉiṉ,- ammikiyāṉē eṭuttōtiya puṟakkāḻaṉavum akakkāḻaṉavumaṉṟi avaṟṟoṭu taḻīik koḷḷappaṭuvaṉavumellām ivaṟṟuk kuriya veṉpatu koḷka. avi, ūkampulluñ cīḻakampullum pañcāy mutaliyaṉavum, puḻaṟkāl āmpal mutaliyaṉavum pulleṉap paṭṭaṭaṅki, yavaṟṟiṉ peyarum peṟumeṉpatu. piṭāvuṅ, kāyāmutaliya putalum, pirampu mutalākiya koṭiyum marameṉap paṭṭavaṟṟu uṟuppiṉ peyar peṟumeṉpatu. avi ūkantōṭu cīḻakantōṭu eṉavum, piṭavili kāyāmpū mullippū eṉavum varum. piṟavum aṉṉa.
maṟṟup piṟappu muṟiyāṉ taḷir muṟkūṟātu ili muṟ kūṟiya teṉṉiyeṉiṉ,- pulliṉuḷ orucāraṉa iliyeṉavum pūveṉavum paṭumeṉa atikāraṅkōṭaṟkeṉka. avi,
"īttili vēynta veyppuṟak kurampi"	(perumpāṇ. 88) 
eṉavum,
'āmpalili, tāmariyili'
eṉavum,
"āmpaṟpū, tāmarippū"
eṉavum varum. piṟavum aṉṉa koḷka.
iṉṉum ivvilēcāṉē, pulliṟkuriyaṉa marattiṟku varuvaṉavuṅ koḷka. avi,
"īṉṟavaḷ titalipō līrpeyyun taḷiroṭum"	(kali. 32)
eṉa īrkkeṉpatu māvilimēl vantatu. piṟavum aṉṉa. 	(87)

iraṇṭaṟkum potuvuṟuppukkaḷ
643.	kāyē paḻamē tōlē cetiḷē
	vīḻōṭu eṉṟāṅku aviyu aṉṉa.
itu mēṟkūṟiya iraṇṭaṟkum potuvuṟuppuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iviyum avvirutiṟattō ṭokkum e-ṟu.
u-m:
teṅkaṅkāy kamukaṅkāy eṉavum, vēppaṅkāy marutaṅkāy eṉavuṅ, kāyeṉpatu avviraṇṭaṟkum vantatu. paḻameṉpa taṟkum iḵtokkum. paṉantōl vēppantōl, paṉañcetiḷ vēppañ cetiḷ, tāḻivīḻ ittivīḻ eṉa iviyum irupāṟkumuriyavāyiṉa.
maṟṟu iviyellām variyaṟiyiṉṟic cellumāyiṟ puṟakkāḻaṉa akakkāḻaṉaveṉa variyaṟuttup payantateṉṉiyeṉiṉ,- avviru pakutiyuḷ aṭaṅkak kūṟiya vellām pulleṉavum marameṉavum paṭāveṉṟaṟkum avaṟṟuṟuppu ōtiyavāṟṟāṟ pulliṉkaṇṇum marattiṉ kaṇṇum peruvaraviṉavā meṉṟaṟku meṉpatu.	(88


marapu tiriyāmikkuk kāraṇam
644.	nilantī nīrvaḷi vicumpōṭu aintuṅ
	kalanta mayakkam ulakam ātaliṉ
	irutiṇi aimpāl iyaṉeṟi vaḻāamit
	tirivil colloṭu taḻāal vēṇṭum.
iḵtu, ulakilellām marapiṟṟiriyāmiyiṉ ataṉ ciṟappuṇartti ataṟkuk kāraṇamuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nilaṉun tīyum nīruṅ kāṟṟum ākāyamumeṉṉum aimperum pūtamuṅ kalanta kalaviyallatu ulakameṉpatu piṟitillāmiyiṉ, avaṟṟic collumāṟu collātu, irutiṇipporuḷeṉa vēṟupaṭuttum aimpāleṉa vēṟupaṭuttum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟa vaḻakkellām marapil tiriyāta colloṭu taḻīi varalvēṇṭum e-ṟu.
itaṉ karuttu;
"nilamvalitu, tīveytu, nīr taṇṇeṉṟatu,
vaḷiyeṟintatu, vicumpuakaliyatu,"
eṉa aḵṟiṇi vaḻakkiṉavāyiṉum, ivi kalanta vaḻiyum avvāy pāṭṭāṉ vaḻaṅkātu, uyartiṇi vāypāṭu vēṟākavum, aḵṟiṇi vāypāṭu vēṟākavum avitammuṭ pakutiyākiya aintupāṟcollum vevvēṟākavum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟa vaḻakkiṟkuk kāraṇam marapallatu piṟitilli yeṉṟavāṟu. eṉavē, marapiṉi valiyuṟuttavāṟu.
maṟṟu, nilam nīr tī vaḷi ākāyameṉa oṉṟu oṉṟaṉuḷ aṭaṅku muṟiyāṟ kūṟutal ceyyātu mayaṅkak kūṟiyateṉṉiyeṉiṉ,- avi kalakkuṅkāl orōporuḷiṉkaṇṇum ammuṟiyāṉē niṟkuṅko leṉṟu karutiṉuṅ karutaṟka, mayaṅkiniṟkumeṉṟaṟku avvāṟu kūṟiṉā ṉeṉpatu.	(89)


marapu tiriyātu varutaṟ ciṟappu
645.	marapunili tirital ceyyuṭku illi
	marapuvaḻip paṭṭa colli ṉāṉa.
itu, marapiṉiyē vaṟpuṟīiyatu.
(i-ḷ.) vaḻakkiṉakattu varalāṟṟumuṟimi piṟaḻātu varutalē takkatu, maṟṟuc ceyyuḷceyta cāṉṟōr ataṟkēṟṟa vakiyāṟ ceyyappeṟuvarākaliṉ e-ṟu.
avarkku immarapu vēṇṭuvataṉṟukoleṉṟu karutiṉuṅ karutaṟka,
"marapētāṉum,
nāṟcol iyalāṉ yāppuvaḻip paṭṭaṉṟu"	(tol. cey. 80)
eṉṟataṉāṉ. marapuvaḻippaṭuṅkāleṉpāṉ
"marapu vaḻippaṭṭa colli ṉāṉa"
eṉṟāṉeṉpatu. āṇṭuk kūṟiya marapu ulakiyalākalāṉ avvulakiyal avvāṟātaṟkuk kāraṇam īṇṭuk kūṟic ceyyuṭkum atuvē kāraṇa meṉak kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu. itaṉatu payaṉ: 'ceññāyi' ṟeṉpatu vaḻakkaṉṟāyiṉum uṇminōkki ataṉic ceyyuḷceyyuñ cāṉṟōr (puṟam. 38) avvāṟu vaḻaṅkiṉāṟpōla, uyartiṇip poruḷiyum aimperumpūtaṅkaḷ aḵṟiṇiyāṉ vaḻaṅkutaluṇmi nōkki avvāṟuñ collappeṟupakoleṉṟu aiyuṟṟāṉi aiyam aṟutta vāṟu. aḵṟiṇip pāṟkum iḵtokkum. allatūum iṉṉaceyyuṭku iṉṉaporu ḷuritteṉavum, iṉippakutiyāṟ peyarpeṟumeṉavum, marapu paṟṟiyē collappaṭumeṉṟaṟkum, iṉi 'niṟimoḻi māntar maṟimoḻi' pōlvaṉa cila miṟikkavi pāṭiṉāruḷareṉpatē paṟṟi allātārum avvāṟu ceytal marapaṉṟeṉṟaṟkum itu kūṟiṉā ṉeṉpatu. avi: cakkarañ, cuḻikuḷaṅ, kōmūttiriki, ēkapātam, eḻukūṟṟirukki, mālimāṟṟu eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa. ivi mantiravaki yāṉaṉṟi vāḷātu makkaḷic ceyyuḷ ceyvārkku akaṉintiṇikkum marapaṉṟeṉpatu karuttu. allātār ivaṟṟi ellārkkuñ ceytaṟ kuriyaveṉa iḻiyak karuti aṉṉa vakiyāṉ vēṟu cila peytukoṇṭu avaṟṟiṟkum ilakkaṇam collupa. avi ittuṇiyeṉṟu variyaṟukkalākā; eṉṉi? "oṟṟi iraṭṭi putti vittāra" meṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa palavuṅ kaṭṭikkoṇṭu avaṟṟāṉē ceyyuḷ ceyyiṉuṅ kaṭiyalākāmiyiṉ, avaṟṟiṟku variyaṟi vakiyāṉ ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟalākā veṉpatu.
"aiyitaṉ kiyu ḷiraṇṭoḻitte ṉimpāṉmēṟ
peytār pirivuritta lilliyāl"
eṉavuṅ,
"kōṭāp pukaḻmāṟaṉ kūṭa laṉiyāḷi
yāṭā vaṭakiṉuḷuṅ kāṇēṉ."	(tiṇi. nūṟ. 4)
eṉavuñ colluvār colluvaṉavaṟṟukkellām variyaṟiyiṉmi yiṉ, avaṟṟukku ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟār, paṇṇattippāṟ (492) paṭuppiṉalla teṉpatu.	(90)

marapu tiriyiṉ varum iḻukku
646.	marapunili tiriyiṉ piṟitupiṟitu ākum.
itu, mēṟpōṉa vaḻakkiṟkuñ ceyyuṭkum varukiṉṟa ilakkaṇat tiṟkum potu.
(i-ḷ.) marapiṉi nili tirittuc collupaveṉiṉ, ulakattuc collellām poruḷiḻantu piṟitupiṟitākum e-ṟu.
cevippulaṉāya ōcikēṭṭuk kaṭpulaṉāya poruḷuṇarvatel lām marapu paṟṟāka allatu maṟṟilliyeṉṟavāṟu.	(91)

vaḻakkāvatu iḵtu eṉal
647.	vaḻakkeṉap paṭuvatu uyarntōr mēṟṟē
	nikaḻcci yavarkaṭṭu āka lāṉa.
itu, vaḻakkiṉuḷ marapiṉip piḻittuk kūṟuvaṉavum uḷaveṉpa tuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vaḻakkeṉṟu collappaṭuvatu uyarntōr vaḻaṅkiya vaḻakkē; eṉṉi? ulakattu nikaḻcciyellām avariyē nōkkiṉami yiṉ e-ṟu.
avariyē nōkkutaleṉpatu avarāṇiyāṉ ulaka nikaḻcci celkiṉṟateṉṟavāṟu. eṉavē, uyarntōreṉap paṭuvār (509) antaṇarum avarpōlum aṟivuṭiyōrumāyiṉāreṉpatu.	(92)

nūliṉ iruvaki
648.	marapunili tiriyā māṭciya vāki
	yuripaṭu nūltām iruvaki yiyala
	mutalum vaḻiyumeṉa nutaliya neṟiyiṉa.
itu, mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa marapu vaḻakkiṟkēyaṉṟi ilakkaṇañ ceyvārkkum veṇṭumeṉavum avvilakkaṇam iṉippakutit teṉavuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) marapuniliyiṉ tiriyāmi tamakkuk kuṇaṉāka uṭiyavāki ellārāṉum urikkappaṭum nūl iraṇṭilakkaṇattavākum, mutaṉūleṉa vum, vaḻinūleṉavuṅ karutik koḷḷum aṭippāṭṭāṉ e-ṟu.
"marapunili tiriyiṉ piṟitupiṟitu ākum."	(tol. mara. 91)
eṉpataṉi itaṟkumēl eytuvittāṉaṉṟē, ataṉāṉ marapu nili tiriyāmiyē tamakkut takutiyāvateṉavum ivviruvāṟṟāṉum oṉṟaṉāṟ ceytalē marapeṉavuṅ kūṟiyavāṟu.
'nutaliyaneṟi' (648) yeṉṟateṉṉiyeṉiṉ, iṉṉatē mutaṉūl iṉṉatē vaḻinūleṉpatōr yāppuṟavilli; oru nūl paṟṟi oruvaṉ vaḻinūl ceytavaḻi av vaḻinūl paṟṟip piṉṉorukālattu orunūl piṟantatāyiṉ atu vaḻinūleṉap paṭṭu muṉṉi vaḻinūlē mutaṉū leṉappaṭumeṉṟaṟkeṉpatu. itaṉic cārpunūleṉṉāmō veṉiṉ,- vaḻinūluñ cārpunūlumākaliṉ aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟāṉāyiṉāṉ. eṟṟukku? cārpunūliṉip paṟṟi orunūl piṟantavaḻic cārpiṉ cārpeṉak kūṟal vēṇṭuvatākalāṉum ,ataṉic cārntu tōṉṟiṟ cārpeṉṉuñ colli mummuṟi colliyum ataṉpiṉ tōṉṟiya nūṟku nāṉmuṟi colliyum eṇṇikantōṭutalāṉumeṉpatu.
maṟṟu iraṇṭāmeṇṇumuṟimikkaṇiṉṟa nūliṉiyē vaḻinūleṉak koṇṭu mūṉṟāvatu tōṉṟiya nūlē cārpu nūlākavum ataṉpiṉṉar nūl ceyyappeṟāreṉavum koḷḷāmōveṉiṉ, mutaṉūliṟ kiṭanta poruḷi ōr upakārappaṭa vaḻinūl ceyta āciriyaṉ ceytakkāṟ cārpunūleṉap piṉṉar ōr āciriyaṉ nūl ceytataṉāṟ payantateṉṉiyeṉka. eṉṟārkku, vaḻinūlum piṟkālattaritāmāyiṉ ataṉiyum eḷitākac ceytalaṉṟē itaṉāṟ payantateṉiṉ, atutāṉum piṉṉorukālattu aritāmāyiṉ ataṉiyum eḷitākac ceyvā ṉallaṉōveṉṟu maṟukka. ivvārāycci vēṇṭā veṉṟaṟkaṉṟē ivviruvakiyāṉum varutalē marapeṉpāṉāyiṟṟeṉ ṟoḻika.
oṉṟaṉvaḻiyē yaṉṟiyun tāntām aṟintavāṟṟāṉum nūlceyyap peṟārōveṉiṉ, atu marapaṉṟu; atunōkkiyaṉṟē,
"marapunili tiriyiṉ piṟitupiṟitu ākum"	(646)
eṉṟa viti nūliṟkum eytuvittup pukuntateṉpatu. eṉṉi? piṟitu piṟitākumāṟeṉiṉ, - oru poruṭkaṇṇē māṟupaṭṭa ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiṉ avviraṇṭum ataṟkilakkaṇamākātu; eṉpōla? māṇikka maṇiyiṉic cevvaṇṇam mutalāyiṉa cila ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiya nūl kiṭappak karuvaṇṇa mutalāyiṉavum ataṟkilakkaṇameṉṟu oruvaṉ etirnūleṉpatoru nūl piṟkālattuc ceyyumāyiṉ, aḵtu ataṉ ilakkaṇameṉap paṭātākalāṉeṉpatu.
maṟṟuk kālantōṟum vaḻakku vēṟupaṭutaliṉ vaḻakkunūlum vēṟupaṭa amiyumpiṟa eṉiṉ,-
"vaḻakkeṉap paṭuvatu uyarntōr mēṟṟē
nikaḻcci yavarkaṭṭu āka lāṉa."	(tol. mara. 92)
eṉṟamiyāṉum,  aṅṅaṉam kālantōṟum ōrōrnūl ceyyiṉ vaḻakkallatu eññāṉṟum avvilakkaṇattāṟ payamiṉṟākiyē cellumākalāṉum atu poruntāteṉpatu.
allatūum, muṟkālattu vaḻaṅkivantataṉi vaḻuveṉṟu kaḷipavāyiṉaṉṟē, piṟkālattup piṟanta vaḻakku ilakkaṇameṉat taḻīikkoḷvateṉka. innūl ilakkaṇattiṉi ivvōttiṉ iṟutikkaṇ vittāṉ 'vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷu'meṉṟu vitantu pukunta iraṇṭilakkaṇamum muṭittallatu, avaṟṟik kūṟum ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟalākāmiyiṉeṉpatu. ikkaruttaṟiyātār ceyyuḷiyaliṉi oṉpatām ōtteṉpa.
allatūum innūlilakkaṇam vaḻakkiṟkuñ ceyyuṭkumēyaṉṟi, aṅṅaṉamākap poruṇmēvum paṇṭam mutalāyavaṟṟiṟku ilakkaṇañ ceyyuṅkālum niyāyañceyyiṉun, tamiḻnūlataṟkilakkaṇam evvāṟ ṟāṉum ituvē yeṉṟaṟkum īṇṭuk kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu, eṉavē, piṟapāṭi nūlkaḷāyiṉ immarapu vēṇṭuvataṉṟāyiṟṟu. maṟṟu mutaṉūliṉi iṉṉateṉpatu tuṇintu uriyārōveṉiṉ, -  atuvaṉṟē iṉik kūṟukiṉṟateṉpatu.	(93)

mutalnūl āmāṟu
649.	viṉiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkiya vaṟiviṉ
	muṉivaṉ kaṇṭatu mutaṉū lākum.
mēl oṉṟaṟku mutaṉūlākiyum oṉṟaṟku vaḻinūlākiyum vantu taṭumāṟumeṉṟāṉ, iṉi avvāṟaṉṟi oṉṟē mutaṉūlākaluñ ciṟupāṉmi uṇṭeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) ceyviṉiyiṉ payaṉ tuvvātu meyyuṇarvuṭiyaṉākiya muṉṉōṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭatē orutaliyāka mutaṉūlāvatu e-ṟu.
eṉavē, mēliccūttirattu mutaṉūlum vaḻinūlumeṉappaṭṭaṉa mutaluñ ciṉiyum pōlat taṭumāṟumeṉpatūum ituvāyiṉ taṭumāṟātu eññāṉṟum mutaṉūlāmeṉpatūum peṟṟām.
viṉiyeṉpaṉa iruviṉi; iṉ nīkkattukkaṇ vantatu. 'viḷaṅkiya aṟi' veṉpatu. muḻutum uṇarum uṇarcci. 'aṟivi'ṉeṉa niṉṟa iṉ cāriyi. iṉṉa aṟiviṉoṭu kūṭiya muṉivaṉ aṟiviṉ muṉiva ṉeṉappaṭum. muṉṉōṉi muṉivaṉeṉpatu orucol viḻukkāṭām. muṉṉeṉpataṉi muṉiyeṉpavākaliṉ.
maṟṟu 'viṉiyi'ṉeṉṟa vēṟṟumi niṟpataṉ niliyum nīṅkuvataṉ nīkkamuṅ kūṟumākaliṉ orukālattu viṉiyiṉkaṇiṉṟu orukālattu nīṅkiṉāṉpōlak kūṟiyateṉṉiyeṉiṉ, aṟṟaṉṟu; viṉippayan toṭaraṟpāṟṟuḷḷam ilaṉeṉpataṉṟi avarkkuc cila ceykiyuḷa veṉpatu pallōrkkum uṭampāṭākaliṉ, avviṉikkaṇiṉṟē, pōkamum pāvamum meyyuṇarvu paṟṟit teṟappaṭṭa vittuppōlap, piṟappil peṟṟiyaṉāki nīṅkumākaliṉ avvāṟu kūṟal amiyumeṉpatu. eṉṉi? 'kuṟṟaṅ keṭuttu muṟṟa uṇarntōruṅ,' 'keṭuppatoru kuṟṟamiṉṟi muḻutuṇarntōrum ellām piṟarkkuṟutiyākiya ākamañ ceyyiṉun tuṟakkam mutalākiya payaṉṟuyppārallarākaliṉ.
iṉi muṉivaṉāṟ ceyyappaṭuvatoru nūlilliyeṉpār avaṉvaḻit tōṉṟiya nalluṇarvuṭiyār avaṉpāṟ poruḷ kēṭṭu mutaṉūl ceytāreṉavum, ammuṉivaṉ muṉṉar ākamattup piṟanta toru moḻiyip paṟṟi aṉittup poruḷuṅ kaṇṭu piṉṉar avaṟṟavaṟṟukku nūlceytār avareṉavum, avvākamattiṉiyē piṟkālattārum oḻukkam vēṟupaṭuntōṟum vēṟupaṭuttu vaḻinūluñ cārpunūlumeṉap palavum ceytāreṉavuṅ kūṟupa. avi evvāṟṟāṉum muṟṟa uṇarntōr ceyta nūlaṉmiyiṉ avitēṟappaṭā; allatūum avi tamiḻnūlaṉmiyiṉ īṇṭu ārāycciyila veṉpatu.
maṟṟu, mēliccūttirattu nutaliya neṟiyāṉē mutalum vaḻiyumā meṉavē, ellārkkum mutalvaṉāṉ ceytatu mutaṉūlēyāmeṉpatu peṟutumākaliṉ, īṇṭu iccūttiraṅ kūṟiyateṉṉiyeṉiṉ, tāmē talivarāvārum attalivari vaḻippaṭṭut talivarāyiṉārum palarākaliṉ, tāmē talivarāyiṉār nūl ceyyiṉ mutaṉūlāva teṉiṉ, aṟṟaṉṟu: tāmē talivarāyiṉār muṟkālattut tamiḻnūl ceytila rākaliṉ, talivar vaḻiniṉṟu talivaṉākiya akattiyaṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa tum mutaṉūleṉpatu aṟivittaṟkum, piṟkālattup perumāṉaṭikaḷ kaḷaviyal ceytāṅkuc ceyyiṉum piṟkālattāṉum mutaṉūlāvateṉ patu aṟivittaṟkum, aṅṅaṉam 'viṉiyi ṉīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟiviṉāṉ' mutaṉūl ceytāṉeṉpatu aṟivittaṟkum itu kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu. eṉavē, akattiyamē muṟkālattu mutaṉūleṉpatūum, ataṉ vaḻittākiya tolkāppiyam ataṉ vaḻinūleṉpatūum peṟṟām.
eṉṟārkku muntunūleṉappaṭṭaṉa muṟkālattu vīḻntaṉaveṉak kūṟit tolkāppiyar akattiyattoṭu piṟaḻavum avaṟṟuvaḻi nūl ceytāreṉṟakkāl iḻukkeṉṉiyeṉiṉ, - atu vēta vaḻakkoṭu māṟukoḷvār ikkālattuc colliṉum, iṟanta kālattup piṟa pācāṇṭikaḷum mūṉṟuvakic caṅkattu nāṉku varuṇattoṭu paṭṭa cāṉṟōrum atu kūṟāreṉpatu. eṉṉi? kaṭiccaṅkattāruṭ kaḷaviyaṟ poruḷ kaṇṭa kaṇakkāyaṉārmakaṉār nakkīrar "iṭiccaṅkattārkkuṅ kaṭiccaṅkattārkkum nūlāyiṟṟut tolkāppiyam" eṉṟārākalāṉum, piṟkālattārkku uriyeḻutiṉōrum atu kūṟik karipōkkiṉārāka lāṉum, avar pulavut tuṟanta nōṉpuṭiyārākalāṟ poykūṟārāka lāṉumeṉpatu. iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟākkāl ituvum marapuvaḻu veṉṟu añci akattiyarvaḻit tōṉṟiya āciriyarellāruḷḷun tolkāppiyaṉārē talivareṉpatu ellā āciriyaruṅ kūṟupavōveṉpatu. eṅṅaṉamō veṉiṉ,
"kūṟiya kuṉṟiṉum mutaṉūl kūṭṭit
tōmiṉ ṟuṇartal tolkāp piyaṉṟaṉ
āṇiyiṟ ṟamiḻaṟin tōrkkuk kaṭaṉē."
itu palkāppiyap puṟaṉaṭic cūttiram.
"vīṅkukaṭa luṭutta viyaṉkaṇ ñālattut
tāṅkā nallicit tamiḻkkuviḷak kākeṉa
vāṉō rēttum vāymoḻip palpuka 
ḻāṉāp perumi yakattiya ṉeṉṉu
maruntava muṉiva ṉākkiya mutaṉūl
poruntak kaṟṟup puritapa vuṇarntōr
nallici niṟutta tolkāp piyaṉum"
eṉpataṉāṉ akattiyar ceyta akaktiyattiṉi mutaṉūleṉavum, avar vāṉōr ēttum vāymoḻip palpukaḻ āṉāpperumi yuṭiyā reṉavum, avarāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa mutaṉūl poruntak kaṟṟup puritapa uṇarntōruḷ talivarāyiṉār tolkāppiyaṉāreṉavum, paṉṉiru paṭalattup puṉinturivakiyāṟ pāyirac cūttirattuḷ urikkap paṭṭatu. iṉip, paṉṉiru paṭalam mutaṉūlāka vaḻinūl ceyta veṇpā māli aiyaṉāritaṉārum itu kūṟiṉār; eṉṉi?
"maṉṉiya ciṟappiṉ vāṉōr vēṇṭat
teṉmali yirunta cīrcāl muṉivaraṉ
ṟaṉpāṟ ṟaṇṭamiḻ tāviṉ ṟuṇarnta
tuṉṉaruñ cīrttit tolkāp piyaṉmutaṟ
paṉṉiru pulavarum pāṅkuṟap pakarnta."
eṉap pāyirañ ceytaṟku uṭaṉpaṭṭamiyiṉeṉpatu.
ivaṟṟāṉellām akattiyamē muṟkālattu mutaṉūleṉpatūun, tolkāppiyam ataṉ vaḻinūleṉpatūum, atutāṉum paṉampāraṉār,
"vaṭavēṅkaṭan teṉkumari"	(tol. pāyiram)
eṉak kumariyāṟṟiṉi elliyākak kūṟip pāyirañceytamiyiṟ cakarar vēḷvikkutiri nāṭit toṭṭa kaṭalakattup paṭṭuk kumariyāṟum paṉināṭṭoṭu keṭuvataṟku muṉṉiyateṉpatūum, avvaḻakkunūl paṟṟiyallatu īṇṭu mūṉṟuvakic caṅkattāruñ ceyyuḷ ceytilareṉpa tūum,  āciriyarum avar pōlvārum avarvaḻi āciriyaruñ ceyyuḷceyta cāṉṟōruñ collātaṉa collappaṭāveṉpatūum, avaruṭampaṭātaṉa col uḷaveṉṟu etirnūleṉa oruvaṉ piṟkālattu nūlceyyumāyiṉ tamiḻvaḻakkamākiya marapiṉōṭun tamiḻnūlōṭum māṟupaṭa nūlceytāṉākumeṉpatūum, iṉit tamiḻnūluḷḷun tamatu matattukkēṟpaṉa mutaṉūl uḷaveṉṟu ikkālattuc ceytukāṭṭiṉum avi muṟkālattu ilaveṉpatu muṟkūṟivanta vakiyāṉ aṟiyappaṭu meṉpatūum, pāṭṭun tokiyum allātaṉa cila nāṭṭikkoṇṭu maṟṟu aviyuñ cāṉṟōrceyyuḷāyiṉa vaḻuvil vaḻakkameṉpār uḷarāyiṉ ikkālattuḷḷum orucārārk kallatu avar cāṉṟō reṉappaṭā reṉpatūum, iṅṅaṉaṅ kaṭṭaḷi ceyyavē kālantōṟum vēṟupaṭa vanta aḻivaḻakkum iḻiciṉar vaḻakkum mutalāyiṉavaṟṟuk kellām nūlceyyiṉ ilakkaṇamellām ellippaṭātu ikantōṭumeṉpatūum, iṟantakālattu nūlellām piṟanta piṟanta vaḻakkup paṟṟik kuṉṟakkūṟaleṉṉuṅ kuṟṟantaṅkumeṉpatūum, oṉṟākap puṟaṉaṭuttu oru cūttiramē ceytupōta amiyumaṉṟi oḻinta cūttiraṅka ḷellām mikiyāmeṉpatūumellām paṭumeṉpatu.	(94)

vaḻinūl āmāṟu
650.	vaḻiyeṉap paṭuvatu ataṉvaḻit tākum.
itu, vaḻinūlāmā ṟuṇarttukiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) nutaliya neṟiyāṉ vaḻinūleṉṟaṟkuc ciṟappuṭiyatu ammutaṉūlvaḻip piṟanta vaḻinūl e-ṟu.
atu tolkāppiyam. maṟṟup palkāppiyam mutaliyaṉavō veṉiṉ, - avi vaḻinūlē; tolkāppiyattiṉvaḻit tōṉṟiṉa veṉpatu; eṉṉi?
"kūṟiya kuṉṟiṉum mutaṉūl kūṭṭit
tōmiṉṟu uṇartal tolkāp piyaṉṟaṉ
āṇiyiṉ tamiḻaṟin tōrkkuk kaṭaṉē."
eṉpavākalāṉum, ivvāciriyar palkāppiyar palkāyaṉār mutalāyi ṉāri avvāṟu kūṟārākalāṉumeṉpatu. eṉṟārkkut, tolkāppiyaṅ kiṭappap palkāppiyaṉār mutaliyōr nūlceyta teṟṟukkeṉiṉ, avarum avarceyta eḻuttuñ collum poruḷumellāñ ceytilar. ceyyuḷilakkaṇam akattiyattup parantukiṭantataṉi ivvāciriyar curuṅkacceytaliṉ aruminōkkip pakuttuk kūṟiṉārākalāṉum, avar tantirattukkēṟpa mutaṉūloṭu porunta nūlceytārākalāṉum amiyumeṉpatu. piṟkālattuk kākkippāṭiṉiyārun tolkāppiya roṭu poruntavē nūlceytāreṉpatu. maṟṟu,
"vaṭatici maruṅkiṉ vaṭukuvaram pākat
teṉṟici yuḷḷiṭ ṭeñciya mūṉṟum
varimaruḷ puṇariyoṭu kariporutu kiṭanta
nāṭṭiyal vaḻakkam nāṉmiyiṉ kaṭikkaṇ
yāppiṉa tilakkaṇa maṟikuvaṉ muṟiyē."
eṉat teṟkuk kumariyaṉṟik kaṭalelliyākiyakālattuc ciṟu kākkippāṭiṉiyār ceyta nūliṉiyum ataṉ vaḻinūleṉṟumō veṉiṉ,- īṇṭuk kūṟiya poruḷellān taḻuvumāṟṟāṟ ceytārāyiṉ, atu vaḻinūlātaṟku iḻukkeṉṉiyeṉka. iṉic, cila kuṉṟak kūṟiṉār tokuttu nūlceytārākali ṉeṉpārum uḷar. oḻiporuḷavāyiṉatu tokiyeṉappaṭāmiyiṉ. aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟutal vaḻinūlilakkaṇamaṉ ṟeṉpatu.	(95)

vaḻinūl vaki
651.	vaḻiyiṉ neṟiyē nālvakittu ākum.
itu, vaḻinūl iṉittu vakitteṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) mēl, vaḻinūleṉappaṭṭatu nāṉkuvakiyāṟ ceyyap paṭum e-ṟu.
itaṉpayaṉ: oru nūluḷ oṉṟallatu pala vārāveṉavum, ataṉāṉē nāṉkuvakiya vaḻinūleṉavum, mutaṉūlāyiṉ oṉṟēyā meṉavuṅ kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu.
aviyāvaṉa mēṟ kūṟupa.	(96)

ituvumatu
652.	tokuttal viritta ṟokiviri moḻipeyarttu
	atarppaṭa yāttalōṭu aṉimara piṉavē.
innāṉku vakiyuṅ kāraṇamāka mēṟkūṟiya nālvaki vaḻinūlum peṟalām e-ṟu.
tokuttaleṉpatu mutaṉūluḷ virintataṉic cilvāḻ nāṭ ciṟṟaṟiviṉ mākkaṭku aṟiyat tokuttukkūṟal; virittaleṉpatu mutaṉūlil tokuttuk kūṟappaṭṭu viḷaṅkātu niṉṟataṉi viḷaṅku māṟṟāṉ virittukkūṟal. tokiviriyeṉpatu avviruvāṟum paṟṟit tokuttu muṉṉiṟīi anniṟutta muṟiyāṉē piṉ virittuk kūṟutal. moḻipeyartteṉpatu piṟapāṭiyāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa poruḷiṉit tamiḻnūlākac ceyvatu. atuvun tamiḻnūluḷ vaḻinūṟku marapāmeṉṟa vāṟu. atarppaṭaveṉpatu, neṟippaṭaveṉṟavāṟu. neṟippaṭutaleṉpatu, avvāṟu moḻipeyarttuc ceyyuṅkāl atu kiṭantavāṟṟāṉē ceyyappaṭum; tokuttum virittun tokiviriyākavuñ ceytataṉāṟ payamillit tamiḻarkkum āriyarkkumeṉpatu. moḻipeyartteṉavē, poruḷ piṟaḻāmi peṟṟām. vaḻakkunūluḷḷum moḻipeyarttu yākkappaṭuvaṉa uḷavōveṉiṉ, atu vēṇṭumē? vētapporuṇmi yum ākamapporuṇmiyum niyāyanūṟ poruṇmiyum paṟṟit tamiḻppaṭukkuṅkāl, avaṟṟiṟkum ituvē ilakkaṇameṉṟaṟku moḻipeyarttaliyum ivaṟṟukkaṭ kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu.
iṉip, parantupaṭṭa poruṇmiyavākiya māpurāṇam, pūta purāṇameṉpaṉa cilvāḻnāṭ ciṟṟaṟiviṉ mākkaṭku upakārappaṭāmi yiṉ, tokuttuc ceyyappaṭṭu vaḻakkunūlākiya tolkāppiyam iṭiccaṅkam mutalāka iṉṟukāṟum uḷatāyiṟṟeṉak koḷka.	(97)

nūl ilakkaṇam
653.	otta cūttiram urippiṉ kāṇṭiki
	meyppaṭak kiḷanta vakiya tāki
	īriṅ kuṟṟamum iṉṟi nēritiṉ
	muppat tiruvaki uttiyoṭu puṇariṉ
	nūleṉa moḻipa nuṇaṅkumoḻip pulavar.
iḵtu, ellā nūṟkum āvatōr ilakkaṇamuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) otta cūttirattiṉi urinaṭāttal vēṇṭiṉa vaḻip piṟanta kāṇṭikiyum, akkāṇṭikiyāṉum viḷaṅkāta kālattu ataṉiyum viḷaṅkakkūṟum urivikaṟpattatumākip, pattuvakik kuṟṟamumiṉṟi, nuṇporuḷavākiya muppattiruvaki uttiyoṭu poruntivariṉ, ataṉi nūleṉṟu collupa nuṇṇitiṉ uṇarnturikkum pulavar e-ṟu.
'otta cūttira' meṉṟataṉāṉ nūliṉ vēṟākiya iruvakip pāyiramuñ cūttirattōṭu otta ilakkaṇattaveṉpatu koḷka. 'urippi'ṉeṉṟataṉāṉ,
" uṇṇiṉ ṟakaṉṟa uriyoṭu porunti "	(tol. cey. 166)
varutali nūlilakkaṇameṉac ceyyuḷiyaluṭ kūṟippōntāṉ; aṅṅaṉam vēṟākip poruntivarumeṉappaṭṭa uriyiṉṟic cūttirattāṉē poruḷ nikaḻnta kālamum uṇṭeṉpatām.
itaṉatu payam : uriyuṅ kāṇṭikiyumiṉṟic cūttirattāṉē coṟpaṭuporuḷ urittalumuṇṭu; aḵtu avaṟṟi yoḻiya ākātu ikkālat teṉṟalāyiṟṟu.
'urippi'ṉeṉṉum viṉiyeccaṅ 'kiḷatta'leṉpataṉoṭu muṭiyum. 'vaki' yeṉṟataṉāṉ uriyeṉappaṭṭatutāṉum ak kāṇṭikiyiṉ meyppaṭak kiḷantatē yākaliṉ avviraṇṭuñ ceyyuḷiyaluṭ kūṟivanta urivikaṟpamē yeṉpatuṇarttiyavāṟu. aḵtēṟ kāṇṭiki yeṉṟāṟpōla itaṟkup peyar vēṟu kūṟāṉōveṉiṉ,-vēṇṭiyār vēṇṭiyavāṟu,
" maṟutalik kaṭāa māṟṟamum"	(tol. mara. 104)
elliyiṉṟi ikantuvaruvaṉavellām uriyuḷ eḻutappaṭāmiyiṉ, atuvuṅ kāṇṭikip pakutiyēyeṉpatu aṟivittaṟku vēṟupeyar kūṟātu 'meyppaṭak kiḷanta vaki'yeṉṟē pōyiṉāṉeṉpatu.
maṟṟu illataṟkum ilakkaṇañ colluvār pōla 'īriṅ kuṟṟamumiṉṟi' yeṉṟateṉṉiyeṉiṉ,-avaṟṟi etir maṟuttukkoḷ ḷum pattuvakik kuṇamuṅ kōṭaṟkut tantu kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu. avi,
" etirmaṟuttu uṇariṉ tiṟattavum aviyē"	(tol. mara. 109)
eṉpuḻic collutum. allatūum ivi kūṟiyavataṉāṉē, piṟitoru poruḷ koḷḷappaṭum iṭaṅkaḷum uḷa; avi nōkki aṅṅaṉaṅ koḷḷātaṉavun tokuttu uṭaṉkūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu. aviyum avaṟṟu viriccūttirattuṭ kūṟutum.
" īriṅkuṟṟamu'meṉṟa ummiyi eccappaṭuttup piṟavuṅ kuṟṟam uḷaveṉpatuṅ koḷka. avi mutaṉūloṭu māṟukōṭalum yāppiṉuṭ cititalum pōlvaṉa. avi muṉṉarc collutum.
'nēriti'ṉeṉṟataṉāṉ mutalvaṉ ceyta nūlāyiṉum vaḻi nūlāyiṉum av vaḻinūliṉ muḻuvatūun terintuṇarum nuṇṇuṇarvi ṉārkkē muppattiruvaki uttiyum pulaṉāvateṉak koḷka. aviyum muṉṉarc collutum. 'vakiyatāki'yeṉpatu 'puṇari'ṉeṉpataṉoṭu muṭintatu. 'puṇari'ṉeṉpatu 'moḻipa' veṉpataṉoṭu muṭintatu.
otta cūttirattiṉi urinaṭāttal vēṇṭiṟ kāṇṭikip poruḷiṉi viḷaṅkaccollum urivikaṟpattatākiyeṉavē 'cūttiramuṅ 2kāṇṭikiyum 3uriyumeṉa mūṉṟum avaṟṟoṭu 4kuṟṟamiṉmi yum 5uttivakiyuṅ kūṭṭa nūlilakkaṇam aintāyiṉa.
itaṉatu payam : ivaṟṟatu viriccūttiraṅkaḷāṉ uyttuṇarvāṉ eṭuttōtit tokuttuk kāṭṭalāyiṟṟu.
maṟṟuk kuṟṟattiṉi iṭivittu uttivakiyiṉi īṟṟukkaṇ vitta teṉṉiyeṉiṉ, īriṅkuṟṟamumiṉṟi varum ilakkaṇam mutaṉūṟkaṇ illiyeṉpatu 'mutalvaṉ kaṇ' (tol. mara. 106) eṉpuḻic collumākaliṉ, avi pōla mutaṉūṟkaṇ uttivakiyum vārā, ciṟuvaraviṉa veṉṟaṟkeṉpatu.
iṉi, 'otta'veṉṟataṉāṟ potuppāyiramum ciṟappuppāyiramum cūttiramum tammiṉ vēṟeṉṟalum, avviruvakip pāyiravuriyum cūttiravuriyum vēṟeḻutappaṭumeṉṟalum, cūttiravuriyuṭ pāyiravuri mayaṅkivaruvaṉa uḷaveṉṟalum, avviruvaki yurikkum vēṟāyiṉum avvuriceytāṉ peyar kūṟutal mutalākiya pāyiravuri kūṟa amiyumeṉṟaluñ, cūttirantāṉē pāyiramillātavaḻip pāyirampōla nūṉmukattu niṟkumeṉṟalum, aṅṅaṉam niṉṟavaḻip potuppāyiramuñ  ciṟappuppāyiramum urivakittāṟ peyturittalum, avviruvakip pāyiramuñ ceytār iṉṉāreṉṟalumeṉṟu iṉṉōraṉṉa kōṭalāyiṟṟu. iviyellāñ cūttirattōṭu otta ilakkaṇamē yātaliṉ 'otta'veṉṉum māṭṭēṟṟā ṉaṭaṅkiṉa.
avi varumāṟu :
" valampuri muttiṟ kulampuri piṟappum"
eṉṉum potuppāyiramum,
" vaṭavēṅkaṭan teṉkumari"	(tol. pāyiram)
eṉṉuñ ciṟappuppāyiramum, oḻinta cūttiraṅkaḷ pōla nūṟpā vakavalāki nūliṉ vēṟāki iṉṟiyamiyāvāyiṉa. avaṟṟiṟkuc cūttiravuriyoṭu mayaṅkāmal vēṟuriyum āṇṭeḻutappaṭṭaṉa.
' eḻutteṉap paṭupa'	(tol. eḻut.1)
eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṉi niṟīi, eṉpatu cūttirameṉavum, ivvatikāram eṉṉa peyarttōveṉavum, ivvatikāram yātaṉi nutaliyateṉavum, avaṟṟiṟku viṭikūṟalum, ivvatikāram eṉippakutiyāṉ uṇarttiṉā ṉeṉavuṅkūṟi oḻinta ōttiṟkum ivvāṟē cūttiravuriyoṭu pāyiravuri mayaṅkac colliyavāṟum, avaṟṟavaṟṟuṭ  kaṇṭukoḷka.
iṉi, ivvuriceytār yārō(veṉiṉ), eṉṟavaḻi, maturik kaṇakkāyaṉār makaṉār nakkīraṉāreṉa uriyeḻutiṉāṉ peyar kūṟutaluñ cūttirañceytāṉ  peyar kūṟutalōṭu otta ilakkaṇatta tāyiṟṟu.
" aṉpiṉ aintiṇik kaḷaveṉap paṭuvatu
antaṇar arumaṟi maṉṟal eṭṭaṉuḷ
kantaruva vaḻakkam eṉmaṉār pulavar"	(iṟiyaṉār. 1)
eṉpatu pāyiramiṉṟit tāṉē nūṉmukattuniṉṟu iruvakip pāyiravuriyum peytu urikkappaṭṭatu.
'vaṭavēṅkaṭan teṉkumari' eṉṉuñ ciṟappuppāyirañ ceytār paṉampāraṉāreṉavum,
" valampuri  muttiṉ kulampuri piṟappum"
eṉṉum potuppāyirañceytāṉ āttiriyaṉ pērāciriyaṉeṉavum pāyirañceytāṉpeyar kūṟiyavāṟu.
'eṉpatu pāyiram ; eṉṉutaliṟṟō(eṉiṉ)'eṉap pāyiravurikku mukavurivantavāṟu kaṇṭukoḷka. piṟavum aṉṉa.	(98)

aiyam aṟuttal
654.	uriyeṭuttu ataṉmuṉ yāppiṉum cūttiram
	puritapa uṭaṉpaṭak kāṇṭiki puṇarppiṉum
	viṭuttalum vilakkalum uṭiyōr vakiyoṭu
	puritapa nāṭip puṇarkkavum paṭumē.
iḵtu, aiyam aṟuttal nutaliṟṟu ; eṉṉi? mēṟkūṟiya cūttiram uripeṟuṅkāṟ kāṇṭikiyum uriyum vēṟuvēṟu piṟakkak kaṇṭa māṇākkaṉ oḻintaṉavum oṉṟoṉṟaṉi oḻittuvaruṅkoleṉṟu aiyuṟṟāṟkuk kāṇṭikiyum uriyumē kaṇṭāy oṉṟu oṉṟaṉi oḻintuvaruvaṉa veṉṟu aiyam aṟuttamiyiṉ. mēlataṟkē puṟaṉaṭi yeṉavum amiyum.
(i-ḷ.) uriyeṭuttu ataṉmuṉ yāppiṉum - kāṇṭikiyiṉi viḷakkuṅ karuttiṉālum akkaruttiṉṟiyum accūttirattiṟku uritoṭarnteḻutiṉum ; cūttirattiṉi atuveṉṟu cuṭṭiṉāṉ.
' muṟpaṭak kiḷattal ceyyuḷuḷ urittu'	(tol. col. 39)
eṉpataṉā ṉeṉavuṇarka.
cūttiram puri tapa uṭaṉpaṭak kāṇṭiki puṇarppiṉum - accūttirattiṟku iṭiyiṉṟik kāṇṭiki poruntacceyyiṉum 'puritapa' eṉpataṉāṉ mēl iṭipukumeṉappaṭṭa pāyiravuriyiṉṟic ceytal kāṇṭikikkum perumpāṉmiyeṉak koḷka. 'uṭaṉpaṭa' eṉpataṉāṟ kāṇṭikiyallāta mukavurikkaṇṇē muṉṉum piṉṉuṅ kāṇṭikipeytu urittaluṅ koḷka. koḷḷavē, muṟkūṟiya aintum oruṅku varutalum ivvāṟē peṟṟām.
viṭuttalum vilakkalum uṭiyōr vakiyoṭu- kaṭāviṟku viṭikūṟuvārum pōli maṟuppāruñ colluñ coṟpakutiyōṭu ; 'vaki' yeṉṟataṉāṉ avvirupakutiyuṅ kāṇṭikikku varuṅkāṟ kuṟippiṉāṟ koḷḷavarumeṉavum, urikkaṇ varuṅkāṟ kūṟṟiṉāṟ koḷḷavarumeṉavuṅ koḷka. aḵtēṟ kūṟṟiṉāṟ kōṭal,
"maṟutalik kaṭāa māṟṟamum uṭittāy"	(tol.mara. 104) 
eṉa varukiṉṟa cūttirattiṟ peṟutumeṉiṉ, aṟṟaṉṟu ; atu piṟaṉkōḷ paṟṟi maṟutalik kaṭāa māṟṟamuṅ kūṟutaṟkuc colliṉāṉ; iḵtu aṉṉataṉṟi aṟiyātu viṉāvun tuṇiyēyākaliṉ vēṟeṉpatu. viṭuttaleṉavē viṉāviṉi viṭuttal peṟṟām. vilakkaleṉavē aḵtallāp pōliyi vilakkutaleṉpatu peṟṟām.
puri tapa nāṭip puṇarkkavum paṭumē- kāṇṭikiyiraṇṭum uriyiraṇṭumeṉṟu varukiṉṟa nāṉkaṉkaṇṇum iṭiantaramiṉṟip puṇarkkavumpaṭum avvirupakutiyum e-ṟu.
aḵtāvatu, kāṇṭikippakuti iraṇṭaṟkum viṭuttalum vilakkalum uṭiyōr kuṟippiṉāṟ koḷḷavittalum, oḻinta uriyiraṇṭaṉ kaṇṇum avi kūṟṟiṉāṟ koḷḷa vittalumeṉa uṇarka. ummi iṟantatu taḻīiyiṟṟu ; eṉṉi? kāṇṭikiyum uriyum oṉṟu oṉṟaṉi oḻittum varumeṉṟamiyiṉ. oḻiyātuvarutal 'uṭaṉpaṭa'veṉṟataṉāṉ taḻuvappaṭṭatu. ivaṟṟiṟku utāraṇamāmāṟu tattam viriccūttirattuṭ collutum.	(99)

cūttira ilakkaṇam
655.	mēṟkiḷantu eṭutta yāppiṉuṭ poruḷoṭu
	cilvaki yeḻuttiṉ ceyyuṭ ṭākic
	colluṅ kāli yuriyakat taṭakki 
	nuṇmiyoṭu puṇarnta voṇmit tākit
	tuḷakka lākāt tuṇimi eyti
	yaḷakka lākā varumporuṭ ṭākip
	palvaki yāṉum payaṉṟeripu uṭiyatu
	cūttirattu iyalpeṉa yāttaṉar pulavar.
itu, niṟutta muṟiyāṉē cūttiramāmā ṟuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mēṟ kiḷantu eṭutta yāppiṉ uṭporuḷoṭu.........
" avaṟṟuṭ cūttiran tāṉē
āṭi niḻaliṉ aṟiyat tōṉṟi
nāṭutal iṉṟip poruḷnaṉi viḷaṅka
yāppiṉuḷ  tōṉṟa yāttamip patuvē"	(tol. cey. 169)
eṉa mēṟ ceyyuḷiyaluḷ vitantōtiya ilakkaṇamuṭittāy,
eṉavē, vaḻuvamikkiṉṟa kiḷaviyākkattu mutaṟkaṇṇē viṉiyiyaluṭ kūṟappaṭum ilakkaṇattiṉi āṇṭu ōr upakārappaṭak kūṟiyatupōla, aṭivariyiṉmikku āṇṭuk kūṟiyatallatu, ic collappaṭṭa ilakkaṇam, īṇṭu iṉik kūṟukiṉṟa ilakkaṇattoṭu kūṭi marapeṉavē paṭum eṉṟāṉām. āṇṭup potuvakiyāṟ kūṟiya nūliṟkum iḵtokkum ; eṉṉi ? pala cūttiran toṭarntu nūlāki īṇṭē marapu kūṟappaṭutaliṉ. uriyāyiṉ, aṉṉataṉṟi nālvakiyuri yuḷ ēṟpatu pakuti vēṟupaṭum marapu kūṟukiṉṟamiyiṉ, atu ceyyuḷiya luṭ kūṟiyavāṟu pōlātu, vēṟeṉavē paṭum īṇṭeṉpatu.
iṉi, muṟkūṟiya pāyira ilakkaṇattiṉi iṉṟiyamiyātu cūttirameṉṟaṟkum ituvē ōttākak koḷka; eṉṉi? mēṟpuṟattu niṉṟu 'mēṟkiḷan teṭutta yāppeṉa'vēpaṭumākali ṉeṉpatu.
itaṉatu payam : pāyira ilakkaṇaṅkaḷoṭu poruntac cūttirañ ceyta leṉpatāyiṟṟu : iḵtu oppakkūṟa (665) leṉṉum uttivaki.
'mēṟkiḷanteṭutta' ilakkaṇamāvatu, āṭiniḻaṟ pōl poru ḷāṉun tērtal vēṇṭāmaṟ poruṭpeṟṟi uṇarkiṉṟāṅkuc cūttirattoṭaruḷ ilakkiyamiṉṟāyiṉum ataṉi itukēṭṭāṉ kāṇumāṟṟāṟ ceytalāyiṟṟu.
iṉi, mēṟkiḷanteṭutta pāyira ilakkaṇam cūttirattoṭu porun tuṅkāṟ potuppāyira ilakkaṇam poruntā, ciṟappuppāyira ilak kaṇam eṭṭumē poruntuvaṉaveṉak koḷka. aḵteṉṉi peṟumā ṟeṉiṉ, mēṟkiḷanta yāppeṉṉātu eṭutta yāppeṉṟataṉāṉē oru nūṟkuc ciṟappu vakiyāṉ iṉṟiyamiyātāki eṭuttuk koḷḷappaṭuvatu
' vaṭavēṅkaṭan teṉkumari'	(tol. pāyiram)
eṉṟāṟpōlum ciṟappuppāyiramākalāṉum, potuppāyiram ellā nūṉmukattum urikkappaṭumeṉṟaṟkum aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟappaṭṭatu ;
cilvaki eḻuttiṉ ceyyuṭṭāki- āṭiniḻaliṉ aṟiyac ceyyuṅ kāl, atupōla oruvaḻip poruḷaṭakki oruvaḻi veḷḷiṭikiṭappac ceyyappaṭātu, poruṭku vēṇṭuñ cila collāṟ ceyyuñ ceykittāki; ceyyuḷeṉṟāṉ, aṟuvakic ceyyuḷiṉ vēṟupaṭṭa poruṭpāṭṭiṟṟākiya aṭivarippāṭṭiṉuṭ ciṟappuṭiya āciriyat tāṉum veṇpāvāṉum ceyyavumpaṭuñ cūttirameṉṟaṟkeṉpatu ; eṉṉi? maṇṭilappāṭṭiṉ uṭampoṭu puṇarttuc cūttirañ ceytamiyāṉuñ,
" ciṉmeṉ moḻiyiṟ ṟāya paṉuval"
veṇpāṭṭākiyum varumātalāṉu meṉpatu.
colluṅ kāli uriyakattu aṭakki- poruḷāṉum pōli yāṉuñ colluvār colluṅkāl avvuriyellān taṉṉakat taṭakki;
nuṇmiyoṭu puṇarnta oṇmittāki- parupporuṭṭākiya pāyirampōlātu nuṇporuṭṭākiya poruḷ kēṭṭārkku veḷḷi taṉṟi uḷḷuṭittāki;
tuḷakkalākāt tuṇimi eyti- muṉṉum piṉṉuṅ kiṭanta cūttiraṅkaḷāṉē taṉṉuṭporuḷ iṉṟiyamiyātatākaleyti ; eṉavē, oṉṟoṉṟaṉi iṉṟiyamiyāta uṟuppup pōlac ceyyal vēṇṭu meṉṟavā ṟāyiṟṟu.
aḷakkalākā arumporuṭṭu āki - aḷattaṟkariya perum poruṭṭāki ;
palvakiyāṉum payaṉ teripuṭiyatu - palavāṟṟāṉum poruḷ viḷaṅka varuvatu :
cūttirattu iyalpeṉa yāttaṉar pulavar - iviyellām cūttirat tilakkaṇameṉṟu mutaṉūlceyta āciriyarāṟ collappaṭṭaṉa e-ṟu.
iḵtiyalpeṉavē ivaṟṟiṟ ciṟiya vēṟupaṭṭaṉa vikārameṉṟā ṉām.
"ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷi yiṟuti
yakaramum uyirum varuvaḻi yiyaṟki"	(tol. eḻut. toki. 21)
eṉṟavaḻi, yakaramum uyirum varuvaḻiyeṉavē, itu nilimoḻit toḻileṉpatu, āṭiniḻaliṉ aṟiyat tōṉṟiṟṟu.
iṉi, nilimoḻittoḻil vēṟu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ,
" mayaṅkā marapiṉ eḻuttumuṟi kāṭṭi"	(tol. pāyiram)
eṉap pāyira ilakkaṇattuṭaṉ poruntuvatāyiṟṟu.
iṉi, eṇvakiyāṟ parantupaṭṭa puḷḷiyīṟṟuc colliṉi yellām 'ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷi'yeṉa aṭakkiṉamiyiṟ cilvaki yeḻuttiṉ ceyyuḷu  māyiṟṟu. 'yakaramum uyirum varuvaḻi iyaṟki'yeṉa varumoḻipparappellām aṭakkiṉamiyiṉ ataṟkum iḵtokkum. itaṉippaṟṟi yeḻunta poypporuḷum meypporuḷum palavāka liṉ uriyakat taṭakkalum uṭittāyiṟṟu.
tokimarapiṉuḷḷum uyirmayaṅkiyaliṉuḷḷum puḷḷi mayaṅkiya liṉuḷḷuṅ kuṟṟiyalukarappuṇariyaliṉuḷḷum parantu paṭṭa poruḷiṉi nuḻintu vāṅkik koḷḷa vittamiyiṉ nuṇmiyoṭu puṇarntatūu māyiṟṟu. iccūttiram poruḷurittavaḻiyum veḷḷitaṉṟi uḷ ḷuṭittākaliṉ nuṇmiyuṭitteṉavum paṭṭatu.
" kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉart taṉṉuru piraṭṭalum "	(tol. eḻuttu. toki. 18)
eṉpatūum
" ñakāri yoṟṟiya toḻiṟpeyar muṉṉar"	(tol. eḻuttu. puḷḷi. 1)
eṉpatūum mutalāyavaṟṟiṟku iḵtu iṉṟiyamiyātatākalum itaṟku avi iṉṟiyamiyāvākalum uṭimiyiṉ, tuḷakkalākāttuṇimi yeytiyatūumāyiṟṟu. irupattunāṉkīṟum irupattiraṇṭu mutalumpaṟṟi eḻunta moḻikaḷellām vēṟṟumikkaṇṇum alvaḻikkaṇṇum irumoḻit toḻilum oruvakiyāṉ tokuttuk kūṟiṉamiyiṉ aḷakkalākā arumporuṭṭātalum peṟṟām. iṅṅaṉam varutal iyalpeṉavē ciṟiya vēṟupaṭṭuvaruvaṉa uḷavāyiṉ aviyum amiyu meṉṟāṉām. avi,
" annā lintum mūṉṟutali yiṭṭa
muṉṉuṟak kiḷanta uyartiṇi yavvē"	(tol. col. viṉi. 11)
eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉavum piṟa ilēcuc collum iyalpiṉṟi vikārameṉap paṭum.	(100)

kāṇṭikiyuri
656.	paḻippil cūttiram paṭṭa paṇpiṉ
	karappiṉṟi muṭivatu kāṇṭiki yākum.
itu, muṟiyāṉē kāṇṭiki yāmāṟuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟamillāta cūttiram taṉatuṭpaṭṭa ilakkaṇattuḷ oṉṟuṅ karavātu muṭiyacceyvatu kāṇṭikiyām. e-ṟu.
'paḻippil cūttira'meṉṟataṉāṟ, kāṇṭiki ceyyattakāteṉṟu ikaḻccippaṭap parantaṉa uḷavāyiṉ maṟuttuc ceyka veṉpatām. itaṉi varukiṉṟa kāṇṭikikkum akalavurikkum ēṟpittuk koḷka.
u-m :
" eḻutteṉṟu ciṟappittuc collappaṭuvaṉa akaramuta, ṉakara viṟuvāy muppaḵtu eṉṟu colluvār. āciriyar, cārntu varalilakka ṇatta mūṉṟu mallāta iṭattu"	(tol. eḻuttu. cū : 1) eṉavarum. piṟavum aṉṉa. iṉi maṟuttuc ceyyuṅkāl,
" veṟiyaṟi ciṟappiṉ vevvāy vēlaṉ
veṟiyāṭṭu ayarnta kāntaḷum"	(tol. puṟat. 5)
eṉpatu teyvacciṟappiṉi aṟiyuñ ciṟappoṭu vevvāyuṭiya vēlaṉatu veṟiyāṭṭiṉi āṭiya kāntaḷumeṉṟāṟpōla varuvaṉa palavuṅ koḷka. iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟi utāraṇaṅkāṭṭal vēṇṭāmiyi uṇarntu urinaṭanta kālamum uṭiyavākum muṟkālattu nūlka ḷeṉpatu karuttu. aḵtēl, utāraṇattoṭuvaruṅ kāṇṭikiyilavō veṉiṉ,  atu varukiṉṟa cūttirattuṭ collutum. karappillateṉṉātu muṭivateṉṟāṉ, ataṉāṟ parantupaṭṭa cūttirattiṉi aṅṅaṉam maṟuttuc ceyyātu tokuttuk kāṇṭikiyāṉ urikkuṅkāl uṭporuḷellām viḷaṅkāmaṟ karantuceytalum uṇṭeṉpatu koḷka; atu,
" veṟiyaṟi ciṟappiṉ vevvāy vēlaṉ
veṟiyāṭṭu ayarnta kāntaḷum"	
eṉpatu mutalāka ik kūṟappaṭṭa irupattoru tuṟiyuṅ karantiyeṉap paṭumeṉṟāṟpōla māṭṭeṉṉuñ ceyyuḷuṟuppiṉit tōṟṟuvāy ceytuviṭutal. piṟavum aṉṉa.	(101)

ituvumatu

657.	viṭṭakalvu iṉṟi virivoṭu poruntic
	cuṭṭiya cūttiram muṭittaṟ poruṭṭā
	ētu naṭiyiṉum eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉum
	mēvāṅku aminta meynneṟittu atuvē.
itu, mēlataṟkoru puṟaṉaṭi ; eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappu vitiyumām; eṉṉi? kāṇṭikiyi mēlataṉōṭu iruvakiya eṉṟamiyiṉ.
(i-ḷ.) viṭṭakalviṉṟi virivoṭu porunti - coṟpaṭuporuḷ colluṅkāl toṭarmoḻikaḷit tokinili payaṉili mutalāyavaṟṟāṟ pakuttukkūṟātu, pakuttukkūṟum urivikaṟpam pōṉṟu maṟṟap poruḷ tōṉṟi; viṭuttaleṉpatu, kaṇṇaḻivu; akkaṇṇaḻi vāṉ akaṉṟupaṭutal viṭṭakal veṉappaṭṭatu. aṅṅaṉam akaṉṟupaṭuta liṉṟi yeṉavē, 'iḵtu iṉṉa vēṟṟumittoṭar, iḵtu iṉṉa payaṉilittoṭa'reṉṟāṟpōlac coṉṉikaḻcciyuṭittāyiṉ aḵtu uriyeṉappaṭumeṉṟāṉām.
cuṭṭiya cūttiram muṭittaṟ poruṭṭā- itu niyamaccūttiram atikāraccūttira meṉṟāṟpōla mutaṟkaṭ colliya cūttirappeyariṉi avvāṟāki muṭinta muṭipu collutal poruṭṭāka; ētu naṭiyiṉum - niyamac cūttirameṉṟāṉum piṟitoṉṟeṉṟāṉum mēṟkoṇṭakkāl iṉṉatu kāraṇattiṉeṉak kāraṇaṅ kūṟum vaḻakkiṉāṉum; eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉum- aṅṅaṉaṅ kāraṇaṅ kūṟiyakkāl ataṟkēṟpatu oṉṟāyiṉum utāraṇamāyiṉum vaḻinūṟkāyiṉ mutaṉūl eṭuttuk kāṭṭutalāyiṉum oṉṟupaṟṟiyum; mēvāṅkaminta meyn neṟittu atuvē- cūttirañ ceyta āciriyaṉ vēṇṭiyatē colliviṭum poruḷilakkaṇattiṟṟu mēṟ kūṟivarukiṉṟa kāṇṭiki e-ṟu.
itu, 'meynneṟi' yeṉpatu cūttirattuc colliṟ karutiya poruḷilakkaṇam. ataṉi avaṉ vēṇṭiyāṅkuc colli muṭippi ṉallatu, cūttirattuc coṟkaṇ vanta collārāycciyum eḻut tārāycciyum aṟiyavēṇṭuvaṉa vellāñ collāreṉpatu karuttu. itaṉi īṟṟukkaṇ vittataṉāṉē varukiṉṟa uriyum iṅṅaṉam viṭṭakalviṉṟi varutaluṭitteṉak koḷka.
u-m :
' iyaṟkip poruḷi yiṟṟeṉak kiḷattal'	(tol. col. kiḷavi. 19)
eṉpatu taṉṟaṉmiyāṉ nikaḻporuḷi iṉṉataṉmitteṉṟu colluka veṉṟa vāṟu. atu 'nilam valitu, nīr taṇṇitu, tī veytu, vaḷi yuḷarum'eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa. itaṟkuk karuttōtutal pāyiravakiyāṟ peṟutumeṉpatu,
'urippiṟ kāṇṭiki'	(tol. mara. 98)
eṉpuḻik kūṟivantām.
maṟṟu, 'uyartiṇiyeṉṟu collupa āciriyar makkaṭ cuṭ ṭiṉkaṇ, aḵṟiṇiyeṉṟu collupa āciriyar avarala piṟavaṟ ṟukkaṇ, avvirutiṇip poruḷmēlum icikkuñ col liṉi'(tol. col: 1) yeṉṟakkālum,
'vēṟṟumi yeṉappaṭṭa poruḷ ēḻu' eṉṟakkāṟum utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭumāṟeṉṉi? avaṟṟavaṟṟup piṉ vēṟu cūttiraṅkaḷāṉ,
'āṭūu vaṟicoṉ makaṭūu vaṟicoṟ
pallō raṟiyuñ colloṭu civaṇi"	(tol. col. 2)
eṉavum,
" oṉṟaṟi collē palavaṟi colleṉṟu"	(tol. col. 3)
eṉavum,
" avitām, peyari yo ṭu ku
iṉ atu kaṇ"	(tol. col. vēṟ. 3)
eṉavum utāraṇaṅ kūṟumāleṉiṉ, - avi tuḷakkalākāt tuṇimi yeyti niṉṟamiyiṉ avvāṟu utāraṇam āṇṭuk kāṭṭākkālum amiyum, avaṟṟip piṟkūṟumākaliṉeṉpatu. allatūum etiratu nōkki uyartiṇiccol mūṉṟum aḵṟiṇic col iraṇṭuṅ kāṭṭiyakkālum iḻukkaṉṟeṉpatu. oḻintavaṟṟiṟkum iḵtokkum.
	(102)

uri ilakkaṇam
658.	cūttirat tuṭporuḷ aṉṟiyum yāppuṟa
	iṉṟi yamiyātu iyipavi ellām
	oṉṟa urippatu uriyeṉap paṭumē.
itu mēl,
" otta cūttira murippiṟ kāṇṭiki
meyppaṭak kiḷanta vakiya tāki"	(tol. mara. 98)
eṉa niṟuttamuṟiyāṉē kāṇṭikiyiṉi meyppaṭak kiḷanta vaki yuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mēl,
" paḻippil cūttiram paṭṭa paṇpiṉ"	(tol. mara. 101)
varutalum, atuvē ētuvum eṭuttukkāṭṭum uṭittāki varutalumeṉa iruvakiyum uṭitteṉṟāṉ. iṉic cūttirattuṭ poruḷaṉṟiyum orutaliyāka ataṟku iṉṟiyamiyātu poruntuvaṉavellām ataṉoṭu kūṭṭic collutal uriyeṉappaṭum e-ṟu.
avi,
"iyaṟkip poruḷi yiṟṟeṉak kiḷattal."	(tol. col. kiḷavi. 19)
eṉṟavaḻi, eṭuttōttiṉṟi 'nilam valitāyiṟ'ṟeṉṉum vaḻuvamiti kōṭalum,
'otta cūttiram'	(tol. 653)
eṉṟavaḻip pāyiram otta cūttirameṉṟu kōṭalum iṉṉōraṉṉa koḷka.
ellāmeṉṟataṉāṟ cūttirapporuḷēyaṉṟi eḻuttuñ collum  paṟṟi ārāyum pakutiyuṭiyatātal vēṇṭum avvuri yeṉpatu koḷka.
iṉi, mēṟ kāṇṭikikku ōtiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷuḷ itaṟkēṟpaṉa vellām atikārattāṟ koḷḷappaṭum. avi ētu naṭiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭuñ cūttirañ cuṭṭutalumeṉṟu iṉṉōraṉṉa koḷka. iviyellān taḻuvutaṟkup pōlum,
" iṉṟi yamiyā tiyipavi yellām"
eṉṟu eṭuttōtuvāṉāyiṟ ṟeṉpatu.	(103)

urikku eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti
659.	maṟutalik kaṭāa māṟṟamum uṭittāyt
	taṉṉū lāṉum muṭinta nūlāṉum
	aiyamum maruṭkiyum cevvitiṉ nīkkit
	teṟṟeṉa oruporuḷ oṟṟumi koḷīit
	tuṇivoṭu niṟṟal eṉmaṉār pulavar.
ituvuṅ kāṇṭikipōla uriyum iruvakitteṉpatu aṟiyumāṟṟāṉ eytiyataṉmēṟ ciṟappuviti yuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) maṟutalik kaṭāa māṟṟamum uṭittāy- maṟutali māṟṟattiṉi iṭiceṟittuk kaṭāvutalum ataṟku maṟumāṟṟamākiya viṭi kūṟutalum uṭittāy; taṉ nūlāṉum- uriyeḻutuvāṉāṟ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟa uri taṉakku mutaṉūlākiya cūttirattāṉum; itaṉāṉē uriyiṉṟic cūttiramē nūleṉappaṭuvatūumāyiṟṟu; muṭinta nūlāṉum - ataṉ mutaṉūlāṉum; muṭintanūleṉpatu iṇinūleṉpārum uḷar; iṇivaṉa kūṟat tāṉ kūṟāṉāyiṉ atu kuṉṟak kūṟaleṉa maṟukka. aṅṅaṉaṅ koḷḷum poruṇmiyuḷavāyiṉ avi,
" cūttirat tuṭporu ḷaṉṟiyum."
eṉpataṉāṉ aṭaṅkum.
maṟṟu, mutaṉūlāṟ kūṟiya poruḷ cila kuṉṟakkūṟiṉāṉ pōla muṭinta nūlāṟ koḷka eṉṟa teṉṉiyeṉiṉ, aṟṟaṉṟu; ipporuṇmi muṭinta nūliṉum uṇṭeṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭappaṭu meṉṟāṉeṉpatu; itu, mēl
" ētu naṭiyiṉu meṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉum."	(tol. mara. 102)
eṉṟavaḻip peṟutumāyiṉum mutaṉūlallatu eṭuttukkāṭṭappeṟāa reṉṟaṟku īṇṭu varintu kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu; eṉavē, iṇinūlum avaṟṟiṉ vaḻinūlum eṭuttuk kāṭṭuṅkāl taṉakku mutalvarāyiṉāri nāṭṭi avar karuttē paṟṟip piṟar ceytāreṉiṉallatu piṟarmēl talimi niṟīi avar karuttup paṟṟi ivar ceytāreṉak kūṟār. aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiṉ avar nūṟkē uriyeḻutuvā ṉallaṉōveṉa maṟukka.
iṉi,
" viṉiyi ṉīṅki viḷaṅkiya vaṟiviṉ muṉiva"	(tol. mara. 94)
ṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa mutaṉūṟkāyiṉ muṭintanūl eṭuttuk kāṭṭuta leṉṉum ivvilakkaṇamiṉṟeṉpatu koḷka, oru taliyaṉmi yeṉṉum uttivakipaṟṟi yeṉpatu.
aiyamum maruṭkiyuñ cevvitiṉ nīkki- aiyavuṇarvum poy yuṇarvuñ cemporuḷiṉāṉ nīkki; teṟṟeṉa oru poruḷ oṟṟumi koḷīi- accemporuḷ karuviyākak kēṭpāṉ uṇarvu maruṭki nīkki meyyuṇarntu teṟṟeṉavum iraṭṭuṟutaṉīkki oṟṟumi koḷuttiyum; tuṇivoṭu niṟṟal eṉmaṉār pulavar- kavarpaṭac collātu oruporuḷ tuṇinturittumāṟutalum atikārattāṉ niṉṟa uriyilakkaṇam e-ṟu.
maṟutaliyum māṟṟamumeṉpatu kaṭā viṭi; aiyamum maruṭki yum, orupuṭi yoppumiyuṭiya pōliyum ataṟku oṉṟum iyipil lāta poypporuḷumeṉappaṭum.
" piṟitu piṟitēṟṟalu muruputoka varutalum"	(tol. col. vēṟ. mara. 21)
eṉṟavaḻip piṟiteṉṟu orukāṟ colla āṟāmurupeṉavum piṉṉoru kāṟ piṟiteṉa oḻinta urupellān taḻuvumeṉavuñ collutal pōliyeṉappaṭum; eṉṉi? avvāṟāvataṉōṭu ēḻāmurupum piṟavurupēṟkum vaḻakkuḷvaḻi oṉṟaṉik koṇṭamiyiṉ, aḵtu orupuṭi yoppumi yuṭittāki apporuḷaṉṟeṉavum paṭātu maṟṟoṉṟiṉik kōṭal poruḷeṉat tuṇiyavum paṭātu aiyavuṇarvu ceytamiyiṉ atu pōliyāyiṟṟu.
iṉip,
" piṟitupiṟi tēṟṟalum uruputoka varutalum."
eṉṉum iraṇṭummiyum poruḷilaveṉṟu piṟitu piṟitēṟṟal uruputoka varuvataṟkaṇṇeṉṟu poruḷkūṟutal pōlvaṉa cūttirat tiṉ karuttaṟiyātu poyyi meyyeṉṟu mayaṅkiya maruṭki yeṉappaṭum. ivviraṇṭaṉiyum nīkki uṇmi uṇarttutal uri yeṉappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. niṟṟaleṉpatu niṟka avvuri yeṉṟavāṟu. taṉ nūlāṉum mutaṉūlāṉum aiyamum maruṭkiyum nīṅkuṅkāl avaṟṟuṭ kiṭanta cemporuḷāṉē nīkkappaṭumeṉpatu ' iraṇṭu kaṇṇāṉuṅ kūrmiyāṟ pārttā'ṉeṉpatupōlak koḷka.	(104)
mutaṉūloṭu māṟukōṭal vaḻinūṟkuc citivātal
660.	collap paṭṭaṉa vellā māṇpum
	maṟutali yāyiṉu maṟṟatu citivē.
itu, vaḻinūṟkē āvatoru vēṟupāṭuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mēṟkūṟiya cūttiramuṅ kāṇṭikiyum uriyumeṉṉum mūṉṟaṟkuñ collappaṭṭa ilakkaṇamellāñ citiyātu māṭci mippaṭiṉum, mutaṉūloṭu māṟukoḷḷiṉ avaṟṟāṉ ellāñ citintateṉavēpaṭum annūl e-ṟu.
' maṟutaliyāyiṉu'meṉṟa ummi etiratu taḻīiyiṟṟu; mēṟkūṟum pattuvakik kuṟṟamē (653) yaṉṟi vaḻinūṟku ituvuṅ kuṟṟamāmeṉṟami yiṉ. ' īriṅ kuṟṟamu miṉṟi' eṉpataṉi nōkka iṟantatu taḻīiyiṟṟumām. itaṉāṉē, piṟkālattu nūlceyvār nūlilakkaṇam piṟaḻāmaṟ ceyyiṉum muṟkālattu nūloṭu poruḷ māṟupaṭac ceyyiṉ atu marapaṉṟu vaḻinūṟkeṉa vilakkiyavāṟāyiṟṟu. ākavē, mutaṉūṟkāyiṉ ivvārāycciyiṉṟeṉpatu karuttu; eṉṉi? mutalvaṉūl māṟupaṭuva taṟku ataṉ muṉṉiyatoru nūl iṉmiyiṉeṉpatu. allatūum 'maṟṟatu' eṉṟu orumi kūṟiṉamiyāṉum itu vaḻinūṟkē vilakkiṟ ṟeṉpatu koḷka. maṟṟumēl,
" mutalum vaḻiyumeṉa nutaliya neṟiyiṉa."	(tol. mara. 93)
eṉavē, mutaṉūliṉ vaḻittu vaḻinūleṉpatūum maṟutaliyāyiṟ citiveṉpatūum peṟutumātaliṉ iccūttiram mikiyām piṟa veṉiṉ,- aṟṟaṉṟu; mutal vaḻi yeṉpaṉa muṉṉum piṉṉum kāṭṭiṉaṉṟi māṟupaṭāmik kūṟalvēṇṭumeṉpatūum peṟutumāyiṉ atu kūṟalvēṇṭumeṉpatu. peṟāmākaliṉ itu kūṟalvēṇṭumeṉpatu. allākkāl,
" muṉṉōr nūliṉ muṭipuoruṅ kovvāmip
piṉṉōṉ vēṇṭum vikaṟpaṅ kūṟuvāṉ"	
cellumeṉpatu.	(105)

mutalnūliṉ ciṟappu
661.	citivila veṉpa mutalvaṉ kaṇṇē.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya vaḻinūṟkup pōla mutaṉūṟkē āvatoru vēṟupā ṭuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mēṟ potuvakiyāṟ kūṟappaṭṭa nūlilakkaṇam aintaṉuḷ īriṅkuṟṟamumiṉṟiyeṉa muṉṉē ōtiya ilakkaṇam oṉṟu; mutaṉūṟ kāyiṉ ammarapiṉa ilakkaṇam vēṇṭuvataṉṟu e-ṟu.
citivilakkaṇattic citiveṉṟu ōtiṉāṉ ākupeyarāṉeṉa uṇarka. maṟṟu,
" viṉiyi ṉīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟiviṉ muṉivaṟku."	(649)
citivilaveṉpatu aṟivāṉē uṇarvaleṉiṉ, - eṭuttōttuk kaḷintu uyttuṇartal payamiṉṟeṉpatu. allatūum mutaṉūṟku muṉṉiyatōr nūliṉi ilakkiyamākap peṟiṉaṉṟē mutalvaṉṟāṉ nūlilakkaṇañ ceyvatu?
maṟṟu aṉṉatoru nūliṉmiyiṉ avaṉ ceyyāta nulilakka ṇam yāṉ ceytēṉeṉavum, avaṟṟuḷ citivē mutaṉūṟkaṇ illi yeṉavum, oḻinta nāṉkum mutalvaṉūṟkaṇṇē uḷavākaliṉ avaṟṟi ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiṉēṉ eṉavum, iṉikkuṟṟaṅkaḷum piṟṟōṉṟuṅ koleṉṟu añci iṅṅaṉam avaṟṟi variyaṟuttu yāṉ pātukāk kiṉṟēṉeṉavum, aṅṅaṉam pātukāttu ivvilakka ṇaṅkaḷ ōtiṟṟum avaṉmutaṉūlē ilakkiyamākap peṟṟeṉavuṅ, 'kūṟiyatu kūṟal (663) pōlvaṉa vēṟoru poruḷ viḷakkumāyiṟ kuṟṟamaṉ'ṟeṉpataṉiyum yāṉē kūṟiyataṉṟi mutalvaṉāyiṟ cilvaki yeḻuttiṉ ceyyuṭṭākavē avaṟṟi vēṟuvēṟu vitittup parantupaṭac ceyyumeṉavum, ivi yellām aṟivittaṟkuc,
" citivila veṉpa mutalvaṉkaṇ"	(661)
eṉṟāṉeṉpatu.
maṟṟu, mutalvaṉ yāppē kūṟumāyiṉ annūṟkut tantiravuttiyum vēṇṭuvataṉṟām piṟaveṉiṉ,-aṅṅaṉamē mutaṉūṟkāyiṉ muppattiruvakiyutti vārātu ciṟupāṉmiyāṉ varumeṉṟaṟkaṉṟē,
"īriṅ kuṟṟamu miṉṟi nēritiṉ,
muppat tiruvaki yuttiyoṭu puṇarntatu."	(653)
eṉa īṇṭu vilakkappaṭṭa kuṟṟattiṉ piṉṉar avvuttiyi vitta karutteṉpatu āṇṭuṅ kūṟiṉāmāyiṟ ṟeṉpatu.
maṟṟu, mutaṉūliṉāṉ nūl ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟāṉōveṉiṉ,  kūṟiṉāṉē yaṉṟō? tāṉ oruvakiyāṉ nūlceytu maṟṟu atuvē nūlilakkaṇameṉap piṟkālattārkku aṟiyavittamiyiṉeṉpatu. maṟṟuc citivilaveṉpār, yārōveṉiṉ, - nikaḻkālattāciriyarum etirkālattāciriyarumeṉa uṇarka.	(106)

vaḻinūṟkuk kuṟṟam
662.	mutalvaḻi āyiṉum yāppiṉuḷ citiyum
	vallōṉ puṇarā vāram pōṉṟē.
itu, mutaṉūloṭu maṟutalippaṭutal vaḻinūṟkuk kuṟṟameṉ kiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) mutalvaḻi yāyiṉum yāppiṉuṭcitiyum -atikārattāṉē mutaṉūliṉik kuṟṟamiṉṟi varuvateṉṟu īṇṭu ilakkaṇaṅkūṟal vēṇṭuvataṉṟeṉṟāṉ. iṉi ammutaṉūliṉi māṟupaṭāmal āṇ ṭōtiya poruṇmi kūṟumāyiṉum nālvakiyāppiṉuḷ mutaṉiṉṟa mūṉṟum paṟṟi vaḻinūṟkuk kuṟṟampiṟakkum ; maṟṟu moḻipeyartta loḻiyak koḷḷumāṟeṉṉiyeṉiṉ, 'yāppiṉuṭ citiyu'meṉṟu iṭamum iṭattiyal poruḷumāka ōtiṉāṉākaliṉ aḵtoḻikkappaṭum. maṟṟu aḵtoḻikkumā ṟeṉṉiyeṉiṉ, -  tamiḻnāṭṭu vaḻakkiṟku mutaṉūlākiya akattiyattuṇ moḻipeyarttuc ceyyavēṇṭum poruḷilavākalāṉum, piṟapāṭikkum potuvāyiṉa poruḷavāyiṉ aviyun tamiḻvaḻakku nōkkiyē ilakkaṇañ ceyyappaṭu mākalāṉum, avarkkum moḻipeyarttal vēṇṭuvataṉṟākaliṉ atuvē oḻikkap paṭum. ikkaruttiṉāṉaṉṟē,
"vaḻiyi ṉeṟiyē nālvakit tākum"	(tol. mara. 96)
eṉa vaḻinūliyē virittu nālvaki yāppiṟkum urimi kūṟiyateṉpatu. aṅṅaṉam mutaṉūṟku moḻipeyarttaliṉmiyiṉ yāppoṉṟum paṟṟic citivupiṟavātu, oḻintaṉapaṟṟi vaḻinūṟkuc citivuṇṭāmeṉpatu karuttu.
vallōṉ puṇarā vāram pōṉṟē - vāram puṇarppāṉ vēṟoruva ṉāyavaḻi mutaṟkūṟu puṇarntāṟ puṇarntavāṟṟoṭu poruntac ceyyātavāṟu pōla e-ṟu.
vārameṉpatu kūṟu; eṉṉi? oru pāṭṭiṉip piṟkūṟu colluvāri,
"vāram pāṭun tōriya makaḷirum"	(cilap.  ūrkāṇ. 14.)
eṉpavākaliṉ.
yāppiṉuṭ cititaleṉpatu, mutaṉūlum vaḻakkunūlāyiṉ iḻintōr vaḻakkum vaḻakkaṉṟōveṉak kūṭṭi virittu yāttuc ceytalum, aḻāṉ puḻāṉ (tol. eḻut. 193) eṉpaṉa pōlvaṉa ikkālattilaveṉṟu kaḷintu tokuttu yāttuc ceytalun, toki viriyum mayaṅkumāṟṟāṉ virintatu tokuttaleṉṉum nūṟpuṇarp piṉit tokiviriyeṉum yāppeṉak kūṟiyatallātavaḻit tokuttu yāttē ceytalum immūvaki yāppiṉoṭu meyttiṟaṅ kūṟuvaleṉa moḻi peyarttali mayakkik kūṟutalumellām yāppiṉuṭ citivē yām. atu paṇṇum pāṇiyum mutalāyiṉa oppiṉum vallōṉ puṇartta iṉṉiciyataṉ nīrmippaṭak kāṭṭātu, vāram puṇarppāṉ puṟanīra tākiya icipaṭap puṇarttal pōlvatāyiṟṟu.
maṟṟu iḻintōr vaḻakkiṉip piṟkālattu uyarntōrun takuti paṟṟi vaḻaṅkupavākalāṉ aviyum amiyāvōveṉiṉ, avi cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkutavā; kaṟṟuṇarntāruṅ kaṟṟuṇarātārum maṟṟu avi kēṭṭē maṉamakiḻvārip paṟṟi nikaḻntaṉavāyiṉum, oṟṟumip paṭṭu oruvaki nillā. peṟṟa kālantōṟum piṟitupiṟitākik kaṭṭaḷip paṭuttu nūlceyvārkkuṅ kiyikantu, variyaṟi yiṉṟāmākaliṉ aḵtu ilakkaṇameṉappaṭāteṉpatu.	(107)

pattuvakik kuṟṟam
663.	citiveṉap paṭupavi vaciyaṟa nāṭiṟ
	kūṟiyatu kūṟaṉ māṟukoḷak kūṟal
	kuṉṟak kūṟal mikipaṭak kūṟal
	poruḷila moḻital mayaṅkak kūṟal
	kēṭpōrkku iṉṉā yāppiṟ ṟātal
	paḻitta moḻiyāṉ iḻukkaṅ kūṟal
	taṉṉāṉ oruporuḷ karutik kūṟal
	eṉṉa vakiyiṉum maṉaṅkōḷ iṉmi
	yaṉṉa piṟavum avaṟṟuviri yākum.
itu, niṟutta muṟiyāṉē īriṅ kuṟṟamum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) citivu eṉappaṭupavi vaciyaṟa nāṭiṉ - kuṟṟameṉṟaṟkuc ciṟappuṭiyaṉavaṟṟik kuṟṟantīra ārāyiṉ: ivaṟṟi eṉappaṭupa veṉpateṉ, kuṟṟamātaṟkuc ciṟantilavām piṟaveṉiṉ, ivvāciriyar eññāṉṟuṅ kuṟṟattiṟku ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟārākalāṉ ilakkaṇattoṭu kūṟuntuṇip payampaṭutalum oruvāṟṟāṟ ciṟappeṉavēpaṭumeṉpatu. allatūum, oḻinta ceyyuṭkāyiṉ iviyaṉittum ākāveṉpatu īṇṭē taḻāal vēṇṭumākalāṉuñ, ceyyuṭkāyiṉ avi amiyāc ciṟappu uṭimiyāṉum, itu vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷumēyaṉṟi avaṟṟiṉ vēṟupaṭac ceyyappaṭum nūlilakkaṇamākalāṉum, eḻuvaki vaḻuppōla amivaṉavē kūṟātu nūluḷ varappeṟātaṉavum varintu kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu. amivaṉavaṟ ṟiṉañcārtti, oḻintaṉavuṅ kūṟiṉāṉ; allākkāl, aviyē kuṟṟamāki oḻintaṉa pukutappeṟuvāṉ cellumeṉṟañciyeṉpatu.
'vaciyaṟa nāṭiṉ' eṉṟataṉāṉ iṅṅaṉaṅ kuṟṟameṉṟu variyap paṭṭaṉavaṟṟik koṇṭu pukuntu maṟṟoru poruḷ koḷḷiṉ avi vaciyaṟṟaṉavāmeṉpatu. avi 1kūṟiyatu kūṟaṉ, 2māṟukoḷakkūṟal, 3mikipaṭakkūṟal, 4poruḷila moḻitaṉ 5mayaṅkakkūṟal eṉṉum aintumāyiṉa.
1kūṟiyatukūṟal muṉṉorukāṟ colliya poruḷ piṉṉu morukāṟ collutal. atu,
"uṭkuvarat tōṉṟu mīrēḻ tuṟittu"	(tol. puṟat. 1)
eṉavum, 
"vanta vīrēḻ vakiyiṟ ṟākum"	(tol. puṟat. 3)
eṉavum irukāṟcolli orukāṟ payaṉkoṇṭavāṟu.
2māṟukoḷakkūṟal
"marappeyark kiḷavik kammē cāriyi"	(tol. eḻut. kuṟ. 10)
eṉṟavaḻip pulliṉiyum marameṉa aṭakkutal. ikkaruttu nōkki(yē) pōlum āṇṭu,
"oṉṟeṉa muṭittal taṉṉiṉa muṭittal"
eṉpataṉāṟ pulliṉiyum marameṉṟu taḻīiyiṉatu akkarutteṉpatu.
3kuṉṟakkūṟal collappukunta poruḷiṉi ācaṟak kūṟātu oḻiyappōtal; iḵtoru payaṉpaṭāk kuṟṟam.
4mikipaṭakkūṟal cilvaki yeḻuttiṉ ceyyuṭṭākac ceyyātu cūttirattuṭ cilacol mikiyākac ceytal pōlvaṉa; avi,
"āyī  riyala puṇarnilic cuṭṭu"	(tol. eḻut. puṇa. 5)
eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa. iṅṅaṉam mikipaṭac ceyyuṅkāl muṉṉiṉṟa colliṟku oṉṟum iyipillaṉa kūṟalākā. eṉṉi?
"muppāṟ puḷḷiyu meḻuttō raṉṉa"	(tol. eḻut. nūṉ. 2)
eṉṟavaḻi, 'avitām muppāṟ puḷḷiyu'meṉap payaṉili koṇṭu māṟip piṉṉar 'eḻuttōraṉṉa' eṉṟatu āṇṭu iyipillatoru colleṉṟu ilēcupaṭātu tāṉ collukiṉṟa poruṭkum iyipupaṭac colliyē mikippaṭuttal vēṇṭumeṉpatu, eṉṉi?
"eḻutteṉappaṭupa"	(tol. eḻut. 1)
eṉpataṉuḷ mūṉṟumē ciṟappuṭiyaveṉpatu koṇṭāṉāyiṟ piṉṉar ammūṉṟiṉiyuñ ciṟappillā muppatiṉōṭum okkumeṉa uvamikka lākāteṉavum māṭṭēṟṟuc cūttiraṅkaḷāṉellām oppumi peṟuvatallatu ciṟappiṉmi kāṭṭappaṭātākalāṉum, muppaḵtumē ciṟantaṉaveṉṉuṅ karuttiṉaṉāyiṉ mūṉṟuñ ciṟappilaveṉpatu mutaṟ cūttirattuṭ peṟappaṭumākaliṉ piṉṉar ivvilēcu kūṟic ciṟappaḻittal vēṇṭāvākalāṉum, 'palaporuṭ kēṟpiṉallatu kōṭala'ṉṟi aiyuṟaṟṟōṟuñ cūttirañ ceyyāṉākalāṉum,
"eḻuttō raṉṉa"	(tol. eḻuttu. 2)
veṉpatu āṇṭu iyipillatoru colleṉṟu ataṉi āṇṭuppeytu ilēcukoṇṭāṉeṉal ākāteṉpatu.
5poruḷilamoḻital - nūṉutaliya poruḷaṉṟi,
"eṉmaṉār pulavar"
eṉṟāṟ pōla vaḻinūl vāypāṭṭu māttiriyē payaṉākac ceyvaṉa.
6mayaṅkakkūṟal - niṟuttamuṟiyāṉaṉṟi,
"atti ṉakara makaramuṉi yilli"	(tol. eḻut. puṇa. 23)
eṉa mayaṅkak kūṟip piṟitoṉṟu kōṭal;
7kēṭpōrkkiṉṉā yāppiṟṟātal - cūttiracceyyuḷ kēṭpōrkku iṉṉāticippac ceytal.
8paḻittamoḻiyāṉ iḻukkaṅkūṟal - muṭivillāta col lāṉum iḻinta collāṉum eṭuttu muṭippaṉavaṟṟi maṟu muṭivupaṟṟi iḻukkaṅ kūṟalāyiṟṟu.
9taṉṉāṉoruporuḷ karutikkūṟal - muṉṉōrāṟ kūṟavum paṭātu vaḻakkiṉuḷḷatumaṉṟit taṉṉuḷḷē oruporuḷ paṭittuk kūṟutal. taṉṉāṉeṉpatu oru colleṉat tāṉeṉpatoru coṟṟāy meyyiṉuḷ uṇaraniṉṟatu. taṉṉāṟ ṟāṉoru poruḷpaṭittuk kūṟutaṉ mūṉṟāvateṉiṉum iḻukkātu, eṉṉi? nutalikkūṟaleṉṉum payaṉilikkut tāṉeṉṉum peyar veḷippaṭā niṉṟatu veḷip paṭuttukkoḷap peṟutumākaliṉ.
10eṉṉavakiyiṉu maṉaṅkōḷiṉmi - evvāṟṟāṉum poru ḷaṟitaṟku aritākac ceytal. ivināṉkuṅ kuṉṟak kūṟaloṭu kūṭṭa aintum eññāṉṟum payaṉpaṭātaṉavāyiṉa.
aṉṉa piṟavum avaṟṟuviri yākum - avi pōlvaṉa piṟavum īriṅkuṟṟameṉṉappaṭṭa toki eṇṇiṟku (853) avvāṟu tokuttaṟkēṟṟa viriyeṇṇām, e-ṟu.
'virintatu  tokutta'leṉpataṉāṉ, etiratu nōkki āṇṭut tokuttāṉātaliṉ (853) īṇṭu avaṟṟit toki kūṟātu virit teṇṇiṉāṉeṉpatu.
'piṟavum' eṉṟataṉāṉ 'veṟṟeṉat toṭuttaṉ, maṟṟoṉṟu virittal, ceṉṟu tēyntiṟutal, niṉṟu payaṉiṉmi' eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa koḷka. ivi mēl īriṅkuṟṟamumeṉa ummiyāl taḻuviyavaṟṟu viriyāyiṉa. mutaṉūloṭu māṟupaṭutalum yāppiṉuṭ cititalum ivi pōla orōvaḻi vārā; annūliṉ muḻuvatūuṅ koḷḷak kiṭantami yiṉ īṇṭavi puṟaṉaṭiyāl taḻuvappaṭṭavāṟeṉpatu. iviyel lāṅ kuṟṟameṉṟu kaḷiyappaṭuvaṉavāyiṉum, īriṅ kuṟṟamumiṉṟiyeṉa mutaṟcūttirattuḷ ōtippukuntāṉ, allākkāl ivaṟṟuṭ cila nūluṭ pukutumāṟum avaṟṟāṟ payaṉkoḷḷumāṟum iṉmiyiṉeṉpatu. payaṉkoḷḷappaṭuvaṉa ainteṉpatu mēṟkāṭṭiṉām;
iṉi, appatiṉāṉkuṅ kuṟṟamēyāki varumāṟu:
"taṉmi yuvami yuruvakan tīvakam
piṉvaru niliyē muṉṉa vilakkē
vēṟṟup poruḷvippu vēṟṟumi yeṉāa"
eṉavum,
"uruvaka muvami vaḻinili maṭakkē
viricuṭar viḷakkeṉa marīi varuvaṉa"
eṉavuñ cila cūttiraṅkaḷi mutaṉiṟīip piṉṉarum avvāypāṭṭāṉē,
"taṉmi yuvami yuruvakan tīvakam
piṉvaru niliyē muṉṉa vilakkē
vēṟṟup poruḷvippu vēṟṟumi yeṉṟāṅ
keṇvaki yiyala ceyyuṭ kaṇiyeṉa 
miyaṟu pulavar vakutturit taṉarē"
eṉṟāṟpōlap piṉṉarum avvāṟē cūttirañceytal 1kūṟiyatu kūṟalāyp payaṉpaṭātāyiṟṟu. eṉṉi? ivaṟṟatu vēṟupāṭu tokic cūttirattut tuṇitteṇṇiyataṉāṉē peṟṟavaḻip, piṉṉum avvāṟē maṟṟoru cūttirañ ceytataṉāṟ payantatiṉmiyi ṉeṉpatu.
"aṉṟeṉa vorutali tuṇinta kuṟṟam
naṉṟaṟi pulavar nāṭṭutaṟ kuriya"
eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa variyātu kuṟṟameṉṟataṉiyē cāṉṟōrkkāyiṟ kuṟṟamākāteṉṟal 2māṟukoḷakkūṟalām, ceyyuṭkellām aṇi yilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuvāṉ kuṟṟaṅ kūṟumvaḻi varintu kūṟutaliṉ.
ceyyuṭkuriya poruṭpaṭi yellāvaṟṟuḷḷum nallaṉavun tīyaṉavum iviyeṉṟu collap pukuntāṉ avaṟṟuṭ cila colli yoḻitalum, vaḻakkoṭu meypporuḷum ārāyvaleṉṟu pukuntāṉ avaṟṟuḷ vaḻakkiṟku vēṇṭuva kūṟi meypporuḷ ārāycci muḻuvatūuñ collātu nekiḻntu pōtalum, eṭuttukkoṇṭa nūluṭ kāṭṭum ilakkiyaṅkaḷic cūttirattāṉ aṭivari ceyva leṉṟu pukuntu cila aṟuttuc ceytu cilavaṟṟukkuc ceyyātu pōtal pōlvaṉavuṅ 3kuṉṟakkūṟalām. avi muṭinta nūliṟ kaṇṭukoḷka.
"iṭiyiṭi tiriyā tiyaṉeṟi marapi
ṉuṭiya karupporu ḷorōvaḻit tuvaṉṟavu
miyaṟki marapi ṉuripporu ṭōṉṟavum
paṉṉiru kālamum nālvaki yiṭattoṭun
toṉṉeṟi marapiṟ ṟōṉṟiṉar ceyalē."
eṉac cūttirañceytu nāṟpatteṭṭiṉum nāṟpatteṭṭuk kālamun tokka cūttirattāṉ,
"tuṭippaṉa tuṭittuc ceyaṟki pōla
vaḻiyiṭa ṉoḻittuk kālaṅ kūṟuval'
eṉṟu pukuntu, akkālattuḷḷuñ ciṟupoḻutu kūṟātu iṭa ilakkaṇamē kūṟi iṭattiṟkuc curuṅka vēṟu cūttirañ ceytavāṟu pōlātu ataṟku iṉṟiyamiyātaṉa vellāṅ kuṉṟāmaṟ kūṟātu, ceṉṟupaṭṭa parappiṟṟākac cūttirañ ceytal pōlvaṉa 4mikipaṭak kūṟalām. aḵtāvatu iṉṉiḷavēṉi leṉpatu
"taṇṇiḻa laṟalyāṟ ṟaṭikarit tuṟitoṟu
milaṅku mulikka ṇēyppakkōṅ kaviḻntu
vaṇṭutā tūtun taṇṭaḷirk kāviṟ
parumala rutirntu murukukamaḻ parappiṉ
maṇvayiṟu kuḷirkkun taṇṇaṟuṅ kayattu
niḻaliruḷ naṭuva ṇaḻalavir tāmarit
tāḷkaṟit taruntum vāḷeyiṟ ṟiḷamīṉ
puḷḷuka veṟiya veḷḷeṉṟu piṟaḻum
paḷiṅku nekiḻntaṉṉa tuḷaṅkāt teṇṇīr
taḷirkuṭintu teviṭṭiya kuyilkuṉintu kuṭippa
maravan tāḻnta karimarac cāraṟ
ṟēṉāṟu tēṟal vēṉiṟ kaṇṇum
vārār kolleṉa nīrvār kaṇṇoṭu
pulampuṭi makaṭūuk kalaṅkaña reyta
yāṟuṅ kuḷaṉuṅ kāvu māṭi
yōruyir mintaru makaḷiruṅ kaḷippak
kāma viḻavoṭu kaṉṉiyar nōṟpa
nilavuñ cāntum palavayiṟ payaṉpaṭat
tuṉpak kālan tuṭittaṉar peṟūu
miṉpak kāla meṉmaṉār pulavar."
eṉṟāṟpōlak kālam paṟṟik kūṟip peruñcūttirañ ceytavaḻiyum paruvattiṟku variyaṟiyilakkaṇam pōtutaliṉṟi viṭutalāyiṟṟu.
iṉi,
"ammūṉ ṟeṉpa maṉṉic collē."	(tol. col. iṭi. 4)
eṉavum,
"appā leṭṭē yummic collē."	(tol. col. iṭi.7)
eṉavum,
"āyī rintoṭu piṟavu maṉṉa"
eṉavum iṉṉōraṉṉa palavum mikipaṭac ceytār ivvāciriyarākaliṟ piṟkālattu nūlceytārkkum mikipaṭacceytal amiyu meṉpāru muḷar. aṟṟaṉṟu;
"yāvayiṉ variṉun tokiyiṉ ṟiyalā."	(tol. col. iṭi. 42)
eṉappaṭṭa eṇṇākaliṉ tokikūṟiṉamiyāṉum vaḻakkātalāṉum atumutalāka allatu cūttiracceyyuḷuñ ceyyārākalāṉum aṅṅaṉan tokitoṭarākkāl iṭarppaṭṭu vikāravakiyāṉ tokuttaṉaṉā meṉavum avi palacollākalāṉ tokukkappaṭuvaṉavalla veṉavuñ colli maṟukka. allatūum, maṉṉeṉpatu ōr iṭiccol; atu tāṉaṭinta coṟporuḷaṉṟit taṉakku vēṟuporuḷiṉmiyiṟ poruḷpaṟṟi mūṉṟeṉalākātu, palaporuḷorucol eṉappaṭuva taṉṟi; ataṉāṉ ataṉi nōkki mūṉṟeṉṟāṉallaṉ, aḵtu aṭutta colliṉi maṉṉic colleṉṟāṉ, avi mūṉṟaṉṟi ettuṇiyum palavāyiṉum oṉṟāy immūṉṟu pakutippaṭu meṉṟu kōṭaṟ keṉpatu. oḻinta iṭiccollāyiṉum avaṟṟiṟkum iḵtokkum. maṉṉic colleṉpatu vēṟṟumittokiyē yaṉṟip paṇput tokiyākalu muṭittu. maṉṉaṭutta colliṉiyum maṉṉeṉa ākupeyarāṟ kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu.
"vaḻakku vaḻippaṭṭa collīṟu tiriyiṉum
paṭittuk koṇṭa colloṭu civaṇit
tiricol leṉṟē ceppiṉar pulavar
varivaḷip paṇittōṇ maṭanal lōyē."
eṉṟāṟpōla iyipillaṉa kūṟutal 5poruḷilamoḻitalām. ivi oḻinta ceyyuṭkāyiṉ 'taṭaṅkaṇṇāy' (nālaṭi. 216) eṉṟāṟpōl varappeṟumeṉṟaṟku ōtteṉṉiyeṉiṉ,-atu ceyyuḷ ceyvār vēṇṭiyavāṟu ceypavākaliṉ, īṇṭuc 'cilvakiyeḻuttiṉ' (655) ceyyuṭṭākac ceyyunūṟkē itu vilakkiṉamiyiṉ ataṉtiṟattuk kaṭāviṉṟeṉa maṟukka.
6mayaṅkak kūṟaleṉpatu,
"mayaṅkā marapi ṉeḻuttumuṟi kāṭṭuval"	(tol. pāyiram)
eṉṟu pukuntāṟpōla iṉṉatu colluvaleṉṟu pukuntāṉ, meyn nūlum vaḻakkunūlum uṭaṉārāytalum āriyamun tamiḻum pōlvaṉa uṭaṉārāytalum pōlvaṉa.
7kēṭpōrkkiṉṉā yāppāvatu,
"katana pama"
"cañayava"
"uyirmuṉ piṉvalliṉam"
"larayanatā meṉa"
eṉṟāṟpōlac cūttiraccurukkamum moḻikku mutalāmeḻuttum īṟāmeḻuttum potuvakiyāṉ aṭaṅka ōtutaṟ payaṉōkki iṅṅaṉam iṉṉāvōcittākac cūttirañ ceytal pōlvaṉa.
8paḻittamoḻiyā ṉiḻukkaṅkūṟal,
"viḷāme ṉiṟuti paḻamoṭu puṇariṟ
ṟaḷāviyaṟ ṟaṉṟi yiyaṟki yākum"
eṉṟāṟpōla viḷāmeṉpatoru vaḻuccollāṟ cūttirañceytal. oṉṟaṟkoṉṟeṉpataṉi,
"oṉṟiṉuk koṉṟu"
eṉac cūttirañ ceytalum atu.
9taṉṉāṉoruporuḷ karutik kūṟaleṉpatu,
malipaṭukaṭāttiṉi āṉantak kuṟṟameṉap piṟkālattā ṉoruvaṉ oru cūttiraṅ kāṭṭutalum, patamuṭip peṉpatōr ilakkaṇam paṭittuk kōṭalum (naṉṉūl) pōlvaṉa.
10eṉṉavakiyiṉu maṉaṅkōḷiṉmi varumāṟu :
"irutiṇip piṟanta vimpāṟ kiḷavikkum"	(tol. col. peyar. 7)
eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṟkum,
"eppeyar muṉṉarum valleḻuttu varuvaḻi"	(tol. eḻut. 128)
eṉpataṟkum vēṟuporuḷ urippā rurikkumāṟṟāṉ aṟiyappaṭum.
11iṉi maṟṟoṉṟu virittaleṉpatu,
āṟuṟuppum pāviṉamum kūṟuvaleṉṟu pukuntu poruḷārāycci palavuṅ kūṟutal. ituvum iṉṉōraṉṉavum mikipaṭak kūṟalāy aṭaṅkumeṉiṉum amiyum.
12niṉṟupayaṉiṉmi yeṉpatu,
piṟkālattut tōṉṟiya vaḻakkē paṟṟi avaṟṟiṟkuṅ kuṟṟan tīra ilakkaṇaṅ kāṭṭiyavaḻi, atu cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkup payaṉpaṭātu tammōraṉṉōr ceyyuṭkup payaṉpaṭṭoḻiyac ceytal. avi vantavaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka. pāṭṭiyaṉmarapeṉak kāṭṭuvaṉavum avi.
13iṉi veṟṟeṉattoṭuttaleṉpatu,
kēṭpōrkkiṉṉā yāppiṉpāṟ paṭṭu aṭaṅkum.
14ceṉṟutēyntiṟutaleṉpatu,
collappukunta toruporuḷ ellāviṭattuñ collap paṭuvatākavum īṟṟukkaṇ māyntu māṟuvatu. atuvuṅ kuṉṟakkūṟalāy aṭaṅkum. oḻintaṉavum aṉṉa.
maṟṟuk kūṟiyatukūṟaṉ muṉ vittateṉṉiyeṉiṉ, atu nūluṭpōlac ceyyuṭkaṇṇum payaṉpaṭavariṉ amiyumeṉṟaṟ keṉpatu. avi,
"vikalum vikal varakkaṇṭu maḵtuṇarār"	(nālaṭi. 4: 9)
eṉavum,
"vañciyāy vañciyār kō"	(yā. vi. mēṟ.)
eṉavuñ colvakiyāṉ iraṭṭittavāṟu. iṉi yamivaṉavaṟṟiṭiyē kuṉṟakkūṟal vittāṉ avaṟṟuttuṇic ciṟappilaveṉṟu aiyuṟāmi yeṉpatu.
ivvāciriyaṉ yāṇṭum ilakkaṇamēkūṟi ivvilakkaṇattiṟ piṟaḻa varutalik kuṟṟameṉṟu koḷḷa vippiṉaṉṟi illāta kuṟṟaṅkaṭku ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟātāṉ itu kūṟiṉāṉ;
"etirmaṟut tuṇariṉ ṟiṟattavu mavi"	(tol. marapu. 664)
eṉa varukiṉṟa cūttirattāṉ iviyum ilakkaṇattiṟku upakārappaṭutali ṉeṉpatu.	(108)

pattuvakik kuṇam
664.	etirmaṟut tuṇariṉ tiṟattavu maviyē.
itu, mēṟ kuṟṟam pattuṅ kūṟi iṉik kuṇamum pattuḷa veṉkiṉṟatu. mēlellām ivvāciriyaṉ ilakkaṇa vaḻakkiṉiyē vitantōti ataṉiṟ piṟaḻntatu kuṟṟameṉṟu koḷḷavittāṉ; ivvōttiṉuḷ 'īriṅkuṟṟamu'meṉpaṉa cila kuṟṟaṅ kūṟiṉāṉ. itu māṟukoḷakkūṟalāṅ kollōveṉiṉ, aṟṟaṉṟu; iviyum ilakkaṇamē kūṟiṉaṉeṉpāṉ ivaṟṟi yetirmaṟuttuk koḷḷa veṉṟāṉeṉpatu. itaṉatu payaṉ, uḷḷatu collutalēyaṉṟi illatu collutalum nūlilakkaṇameṉṟaṟivittalāyiṟṟu. avi,
"peyarnilik kiḷavi kālan tōṉṟā"	(tol. col. vēṟ. 9)
eṉavum,
"viṉiyeṉap paṭuvatu vēṟṟumi koḷḷātu"	(tol. col. viṉi. 1)
eṉavum varum. eṉavē, 'kūṟiyatu kūṟāmi'yum 'māṟukoḷak kūṟāmiyu'ṅ 'kuṉṟakkūṟāmi'yum pōlvaṉavum pattu uḷa nūlilakkaṇameṉṟāṉām. mēl,
"īriṅ kuṟṟamu miṉṟi"
eṉṟatallatu avaṟṟi nāṭṭikkoṇṭu etirmaṟuttuk kōṭal peṟāmiyiṉ itu kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu.	(109)

muppattīr utti vakikaḷ
665.	otta kāṭci uttivaki virippiṉ
	nutaliya taṟital atikāra muṟiyē
	tokuttuk kūṟal vakuttumeyn niṟuttal
	moḻinta poruḷō ṭoṉṟa vavvayiṉ
	moḻiyā tataṉi muṭṭiṉṟu muṭittal
	vārā tataṉāṉ vantatu muṭittal
	vantatu koṇṭu vārāta tuṇarttal
	muntu moḻintataṉ talitaṭu māṟṟē
	oppak kūṟal orutali moḻital
	taṉkōṭ kūṟal muṟipiṟa ḻāmi
	piṟaṉuṭam paṭṭatu tāṉuṭam paṭutal
	iṟantatu kāttal etiratu pōṟṟal
	moḻivā meṉṟal kūṟiṟ ṟeṉṟal
	tāṉkuṟi yiṭutal orutali yaṉmi
	muṭintatu kāṭṭal āṇi kūṟal
	palporuṭ kēṟpi ṉallatu kōṭal
	tokutta moḻiyāṉ vakuttaṉar kōṭal
	maṟutali citittut taṉṟuṇi purittal
	piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟal aṟiyā tuṭampaṭal
	poruḷiṭi yiṭutal etirporu ḷuṇarttal
	colli ṉeccañ colliyāṅ kuṇarttal
	tantupuṇarn turittal ñāpakaṅ kūṟal
	uyttukkoṇ ṭuṇarttaloṭu meyppaṭa nāṭic
	colliya valla piṟavavaṇ variṉum 
	colliya vakiyāṉ curuṅka nāṭi
	maṉatti ṉeṇṇi mācaṟat terintukoṇṭu
	iṉattiṟ cērtti yuṇarttal vēṇṭum	
	nuṉittaku pulavar kūṟiya nūlē.
itu muṟiyāṉē iṟutikkaṇiṉṟa muppattiruvaki yuttiyuṅ kūṟi maṟṟum innūluḷ atikāram mūṉṟaṟkum vēṇṭum puṟaṉaṭiyuṅ kūṟuta ṉutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) otta kāṭci yuttivaki virippiṉ  muṟkūṟiya kuṟṟaṅkaḷōṭu oppattōṉṟun tōṟṟattiṉi uṭiyavākiya uttik kūṟṟiṉi virittuc colliṉ:
pattuvakik kuṟṟattōṭum ottuvarumeṉavē iviyum nūṟkaṇaṉṟi oḻinta ceyyuṭku varuṅkāl vilakkappaṭutalum, muṟkūṟiya kuṟṟampōla iviyum vēṟu cila poruḷ paṭittalumuṭiyavāyiṉa. 'kāṭciyutti' yeṉṟu ivaṟṟik kūṟiyavataṉāṉ nūluṭkāṇappaṭu meṉṟa aintu kuṟṟattōṭum ottal koḷḷappaṭum, avvannūṟkup payampaṭavarum pakutiyāṉeṉpatu.
utti yeṉpatu nūlceyyuṅkāl iyalpuvakiyākiya vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷum pōlac cevvaṉañcollutal oṇmi yuṭittaṉṟām piṟaveṉiṉ, aṟṟaṉṟu; avvāṟu ceytakkāl,
"nuṇmiyoṭu puṇarnta oṇmittākal vēṇṭum."	(655)
eṉpatu muṉṉarccolliṉāṉ. īṇṭuccevvaṉañ collāta tantiravutti vakiyum avvāṟē oṇmiyuṭiyavāmeṉpatu karuttu; eṉṟārkkuc cevvaṉañ ceytali uttiyeṉṉāṉōveṉiṉ,- atu collāmi muṭintatākaliṉaṉṟē uttiyeṉṉātu ivaṟṟi uttivaki yeṉpāṉāyiṟṟeṉpatu. aḵtēl, ivaṟṟi muṉ tokuttāṉpōla virippiṉeṉṟa teṉṉiyeṉiṉ, muṉṉar etiratunōkki (mara. 98) muppattiraṇṭeṉat tokikūṟip piṉṉar,
"maṉatti ṉeṇṇi mācaṟat terintukoṇṭu
iṉattiṟ cērtti yuṇarttal vēṇṭum,"
eṉkiṉṟāṉākalāṉ, aṅṅaṉam iṉam paṟṟi avaṟṟōṭu aṭaṅkuvaṉa vellām avaṟṟu viriyākumeṉṉuṅ karuttiṉāṟ kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu.
"avaṉiva ṉuvaṉeṉa varūum peyarum
avaḷiva ḷuvaḷeṉa varūum peyarum
avariva ruvareṉa varūum peyarum
yāṉyā nāmeṉa varūum peyarum
yāvaṉ yāvaḷ yāva reṉṉum
āvayiṉ mūṉṟō ṭappati ṉintum
pālaṟi vanta vuyartiṇip peyarē."	(tol. col. 164)
eṉpataṉuḷ,
"āvayiṉ mūṉṟō ṭappati ṉintum"
eṉat tokuttuk kūṟiyavaḻi atu mikipaṭak kūṟalākātu; eṉṉi? ummiyeṇṇākaliṉ tokiyiṉṟiyuñ cilvakiyeḻuttiṟ ceyyuṭṭāka vaḻakkiyalāṉē curuṅkacceyvatōr āṟuḷatāyiṉum appatiṉintumeṉat tokuttuk kūṟal uttivakiyāṉ amiyumākali ṉeṉpatu karuttu. iṅṅaṉan tokikūṟutal veḷḷitaṉṟi oḷḷitākal; avi, patiṉintu peyarumē orunikaraṉa veṉpatu aṟiyalākumeṉpatu.
iṉi,
"meypeṟu marapiṟ ṟoṭivaki tāmē"	(tol. cey. 100)
eṉṉuñ cūttiram pōlvaṉa piṟitoruporuḷ payakkumeṉṟalum accūttirattuṭ kāṭṭappaṭṭatu. ivvāṟṟāṉ ivvuttivaki ellā nūṟkum iṉṟiyamiyāvāyiṉa.
maṟṟu, vakiyeṉappaṭātu cevvaṉañceyyum uttiyāvaṉa yāviyeṉiṉ,
"iyaṟkip poruḷi yiṟṟeṉak kiḷattal"	(tol. col. 19)
eṉṟāṟpōlac colliyoḻivaṉa. piṟavum aṉṉa.
'virippi'ṉeṉṟa viṉiyeccañ 'cērtti yuṇarttal vēṇṭu' meṉṟa muṟṟuviṉikoṇṭu muṭintatu. 'meyppaṭanāṭi' yeṉpaṉa pōlvaṉavaṟṟukkum ituvē muṭipu; ataṟku nūleṉṉum peyar koṭuttu muṭikka. nūliyeṉṟu aikāram virippiṉ, vēṇṭumeṉpatu taṉpālāṉum piṟaṉpālāṉum peṟṟatoru peyarkoṇṭu muṭiyum.
(1) nutaliyataṟital:cūttirattuḷ ōtiya poruḷāṟ collappaṭum payaṉ illatupōlac colli, yataṉāṉē ataṟkēṟṟavakiyāṟ karutiyuṇarappaṭuporuḷ iṉṉateṉṟu koḷḷavittal; atu,
"vēṟuviṉip potucco loruviṉi kiḷavār"	(tol. col. 46)
eṉavum,
"ciṟappi ṉākiya peyarnilik kiḷavikku
miyaṟpeyark kiḷavi muṟpaṭak kiḷavār"	(tol. col. 41)
eṉavum ivi 'vaḻuvaṟka' 'vaḻuvamikka' eṉṉum irupakutiyuḷ vaḻuvamittaṟkeḻuntaṉaveṉpatu karutiṉāṉ āciriyaṉeṉpatu aṟiya vantaṉa. itaṉi mutaṟkaṇvittuppōy iṟutikkaṇṇē uyttuk koṇṭuṇartali vittāṉ; ituvumatupōl uyttuk koṇ ṭuṇartal oruvakiyāṉuṭittāyiṉum ataṉāṟ peṟṟapayaṉ piṟitu moṉṟu uḷatātal vēṟṟumiyuṭimiyiṉeṉpatu. atu muṉṉarc collutum (pak. 378).
iṉi, mūvakit tamiḻvaḻakkamum nutaliyatum, avaṟṟuḷ iyaṟṟamiḻē nutaliyatum, atikāram nutaliyatum, atikārattuḷ ōttu nutaliyatum, ōttiṉuṭ cūttiram nutaliyatumeṉavum ivi nutaliyataṟitaṟ pakutiyāy aṭaṅkumeṉpāṉ,
"maṉatti ṉeṇṇi mācaṟat terintukoṇṭu
iṉattiṟ cērtti yuṇarttal vēṇṭum"
eṉkiṉṟāṉeṉpatu. mēlvarukiṉṟavaṟṟukkumokkum. aḵtēl. itaṉi āṇṭuvittu maṟṟataṉi īṇṭuvippiṉum iḵtokkum piṟaveṉiṉ, - aṟṟaṉṟu; nūliṉvēṟākiya pāyirattuḷḷāyiṉum in nutaliyataṟital varutalum uttivakiyeṉṟaṟku itaṉi muṉvittā ṉeṉpatu.
(2) atikāramuṟimieṉpatu: muṉṉiṉṟa  cūttirap poruṇmi piṉvaruñ cūttirattiṟkum peṟaṟpālaṉa peṟavittal; avi,
"iyaṟkip poruḷi yiṟṟeṉak kiḷattal"	(tol. col. 19)
eṉṟavaḻi iṟṟeṉak kiḷatta lurimipūṇṭataṉṟē, ataṉic, 
"ceyaṟkip poruḷi yākkamoṭu kūṟal"	(tol. col. 20)
eṉpuḻiyuṅ koḷḷa vittalum;
"kuṟṟiya likara niṟṟal vēṇṭum"	(tol. eḻut. 34)
eṉpataṉik,
"kuṟṟiya lukaram vallā ṟūrntu"	(tol. eḻut. 36)
niṟṟal vēṇṭum eṉṟu koḷḷa vittalum pōlvaṉa.
iṉi, vaḻakkiyalum vaḻakkiyalāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa ceyyuḷiyalum paṟṟi eḻunta ilakkaṇam iyaṟṟamiḻeṉappaṭum. acceyyuḷiṉṟi amiyāta iciyilakkaṇam icittamiḻeṉappeyareyti avviyaṟ ṟamiḻp piṉṉar vikkappaṭṭateṉappaṭum; ivviraṇṭaṉvaḻi nikaḻttuṅ kūttilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiya nāṭakat tamiḻ avaṟṟup piṉṉarttā meṉa muṟimi kūṟutalum, iṉi iyaṟṟamiḻuḷḷum eḻuttatikārat toṭu collatikārattiṟkuñ collatikārattoṭu poruḷatikārattiṟ kum iyipu kūṟutalum, atikārattuḷ ōttup palavākaliṉ avi oṉṟaṉpiṉoṉṟu vittaṟku iyipu kūṟutalum, avvāṟē cūttirat tiṟku iyipu kūṟutalumellām atikāramuṟimikku iṉameṉṟu cērttiyuṇarappaṭum. piṟavum aṉṉa.
atikārameṉṟa poruṇmi yeṉṉiyeṉiṉ, muṟimiyeṉa vum, iṭameṉavuṅ, kiḻamiyeṉavuṅ koḷḷappaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ, īṇṭu atikārameṉṟoḻiyātu muṟimiyeṉavuṅ kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, atikāra meṉpatu muṟimip poruṭṭaṉṟi muṉ ōriṭattu niṟuttiyataṉi vaḻimuṟiyāṟ poruḷkōṭal koḷka; eṉṉi? muṉṉum piṉṉum niṉṟa cūttiram iraṇṭaṉiyum ōriṭattaṉavākak karutal vēṇṭum; aṅṅaṉaṅ karutaliyipukoṇṭaṉṟi oṉṟaṉ poruṇmi oṉṟaṟku varuvittal  aritākalāṉum, aṅṅaṉam iṭam paṟṟākkāl oruvaṉ ceyta nūloṭu piṟitorunūṟku iyipu kūṟātavāṟupōla eḻuttatikārat toṭu collatikārattiṭi iyipu kūṟal vēṇṭuvataṉṟāvāṉ cellumātalāṉumeṉpatu. eṉavē, iṭameṉappaṭṭatutāṉē muṟimip poruḷum paṭumāyiṉum iccūttirattuḷ atikārameṉavum muṟimiyeṉavuṅ kūṟiṉamiyiṉ īṇṭu iṭamuṟimi yeṉpatē karuttāyiṟṟu. itaṉāṉē uṭaṉpiṟantāruḷ oruvaṟkuriyatu vaḻit tōṉṟiṉārkkum oruvaḻi uriyavāṟupōla, muṉṉar niṉṟa viti piṉṉar vantataṟkum vēṇṭiyavaḻik koḷḷappaṭumeṉpatu uttivakiyāyiṟṟu.
iṉiyorucārār, iṅṅaṉam varintukoṇṭa iṭattuḷḷē yāṟṟoḻukkup pōlavaṉṟi iṭiyiṭiyum peṟumeṉpatu nōkki arimā nōkkun, tērip pāyttuḷum, paruntu viḻukkāṭum āki varumeṉavuñ collupa; aviyum iṉattiṟ cērtti yuṇarttavēpaṭu meṉpatu.
(3) tokuttuk kūṟal: tokuttiyātta nūluḷḷun tokuttuk kūṟutal;
aviyāvaṉa: eḻuttu muppattumūṉṟu, colliraṇṭu, poruḷiraṇṭu eṉa āciriyaṉṟāṉē toki kūṟutal pōlvaṉa.
"irutiṇi maruṅki ṉimpā laṟiya
vīṟṟiṉiṉ ṟicikkum patiṉō reḻuttum"	(tol. col. 10)
eṉap pala cūttirattu virintaṉa tokuttalum,
"āvayiṉ mūṉṟō ṭappati ṉintum
pālaṟi vanta vuyartiṇip peyar"	(tol. col. 164)
eṉa oru cūttirattu virittut tokuttalum pōlvaṉavum ataṉ pakutiyāy aṭaṅkum.
(4) vakuttu meynniṟuttal: aṅṅaṉan tokuttuk kūṟiyavaḻi eḻuttu muppattumūṉṟeṉṟa tokiyiṉik kuṟṟeḻuttum neṭ ṭeḻuttum uyirum uyirmeyyum valliṉamum melliṉamum iṭi yiṉamumeṉṟāṟ pōlavum, uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇi yeṉṟa toki yiṉi oruvaṉ orutti palar oṉṟu palaveṉṟāṟpōlavum vakuttal :
"uyartiṇik kurimiyu maḵṟiṇik kurimiyu
māyiru tiṇikku mōraṉṉa vurimiyum"	(tol. col. 162)
eṉavum,
"iyaṟco ṟirico ṟiciccol vaṭacoleṉ
ṟaṉittē ceyyu ḷīṭṭac collē"	(tol. col. 387)
eṉavum varuvaṉavum avi. ivaṟṟuḷ irutiṇiyeṉak kūṟippeyar mūṉṟu, viṉi mūṉṟeṉṟalum, iṉi akattiṇi puṟattiṇiyeṉakkūṟi, akampuṟameṉak kōṭalum iṉameṉṟatu, avi kuṟṟeḻuttu neṭṭeḻutteṉa muṟkūṟiyaṉavē maṟṟoru peyar peṟṟāṟ pōlātu colvakiyāṉ mūṉṟāvatoru col vēṟu peṟṟilāmiyiṉeṉpatu. iṅṅaṉaṅ koḷḷākkāl irutiṇip peyarum viṉiyum mummūṉṟeṉal ākātaṉṟōveṉpatu.
"colleṉap paṭupa peyarē viṉiyeṉṟu
āyiraṇ ṭeṉpa vaṟintici ṉōrē"	(tol. col. 160)
eṉṟavaḻi,
"iṭiccoṟ kiḷaviyu muriccoṟ kiḷaviyum
avaṟṟuvaḻi maruṅkiṟ ṟōṉṟu meṉpa"	(tol. col. 161)
eṉa vakuttalum, iṉi ārāynta iyaṟcolloṭu vēṟu mūṉṟu coṟkūṭṭi nāṉkeṉṟalum avvāṟē iṉameṉappaṭum. eṉṉi? oḻinta mūṉṟu collum iyaṟcolliṉ vēṟupaṭṭami kūṟumākali ṉeṉpatu. aḵtēl, eḻuttu muppattumūṉṟeṉak kūṟip piṉṉar irunūṟṟorupattāṟuyir meyk kuppi kōṭalum vēṟṟeḻuttāki varumeṉpatu 'vakuttu meynniṟutta'leṉpataṉāṟ koḷḷāmō veṉiṉ, kōṭumaṉṟē,
"puḷḷi yillā vellā meyyum"	(tol. eḻut. 17)
eṉpuḻi,
"uruvuru vāki yakaramo ṭuyirttalum"
eṉa āciriyaṉ iraṇṭeḻuttiṉ kūṭṭamākac cūttirañ ceyti lāṉāyiṉeṉpatu.
'mey'yeṉṟataṉāṉ vakuttavaṟṟip piṉṉum vakuttu niṟuttal koḷḷappaṭum; eṉṉi? iyaṟcīr patteṉavum āciriyavuriccīrā ṟeṉavum vakuttavaṟṟi,
"iyaṟcī riṟutimuṉ nērava ṇiṟpiṉ
uriccīr veṇpā vāku meṉpa"	(tol. cey. 19)
eṉa iyalacimayakkamākiya nāṉkaṉiyē iyaṟcīreṉavum,
"veṇpā vuriccī rāciriya vuriccīr
iṉpā nēraṭik koruṅkunili yilavē"	(tol. cey. 23)
eṉṟavaḻi, āciriyavuriccīr āṟaṉuḷ iraṇṭaṉi oḻittu uriyaci mayakkamākiya nāṉkaṉiyē āciriyavuriccīreṉavum vakuttu niṉṟamiyi ṉeṉpatu.
'mey'yeṉṟataṉāṉ vakuttavaṟṟip piṉṉum vakuttu niṟuttal koḷḷappaṭum; eṉṉi?
"iyalaci mayakka miyaṟcī rēṉi
uriyaci mayakka māciriya vuriccīr"	(tol. cey. 13)
eṉṟu pakuttōtiyavāṟṟāṉeṉpatu; akkaruttiṉāṉaṉṟē oḻinta iraṇṭaṉiyuṅ,
"kalittaḷi maruṅkiṟ kaṭiyavum paṭāa"	(tol. cey. 24)
eṉa iṟantatutaḻīi urippāṉāyiṟṟeṉpatu.
(5) moḻinta poruḷō ṭoṉṟa vavvayiṉ moḻiyātataṉi muṭṭiṉṟu: muṭittaleṭuttōtiya poruṇmikku ēṟṟa vakiyāṉ apporuṇmikkaṭ collātatoṉṟu koḷḷavittal.
'muṭṭiṉṟi muṭitta'leṉṟataṉāṉ eṭuttōtātatum eṭut tōtiyataṉōṭu okkuñ ciṟappiṟṟeṉṟavāṟām. atu pōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum. maṟṟu itaṉi aruttāpatti yeṉṉāmō veṉiṉ, eṉṉā maṉṟē,
"piṟacī ruḷvaḻit taṉṟaḷi vēṇṭupa"
eṉṉum poruṭṭaṉṟit taṉcīroṭu iyaṟcīr vantu taḷi koḷḷumeṉa moḻiyātoru poruḷkōṭaliṉeṉka.
iṉi,
"raḵkā ṉoṟṟum pakara viṟutiyum"	(tol. col. 7)
eṉṟavaḻi oḻinta nāṉkeḻuttum īṟṟiṉiṟkumeṉṟu kōṭal pōl vaṉavum ataṉ iṉameṉappaṭum. eṉṉi? oṉṟeṉamuṭittal taṉṉiṉa muṭitta'leṉiṉum iḻukkātu. iṉic,
"cīriyi maruṅki ṉōraci yoppiṉ"	(tol. cey. 56)
eṉṟavaḻi, oṉṟātatu iyaṟcīrveṇṭaḷi yeṉṟukōṭal aruttāpatti yāki,
"eṭutta moḻiyiṉañ ceppalu murittu"	(tol. cey. 61)
eṉṟa vaḻakkiyalāṉē vaḻuvaṉṟi aṭaṅkumeṉpatu.
(6) vārātataṉāṉ vantatu muṭittal:oru poruṇmikku vēṇṭum ilakkaṇam nirampa vārātatoru cūttirattāṉē aṅṅaṉam vanta poruṇmikku vēṇṭum muṭipu koḷḷacceytal; atu,
"toṭara liṟuti tammuṟ ṟāmvariṉ
lakaram ṟakaravoṟ ṟākalu murittē"	(tol. eḻut. 214)
eṉa akarattiṟkuk kēṭu vārāta cūttirattāṉē lakaram ṟakaravoṟṟāy varumeṉap pirittut tiripu kūṟiyatē paṟṟāka akaram āṇṭu nillātu keṭumeṉṟu koḷḷavittu ataṉāṉē 'ciṟcila vittip paṟpala koṇṭā'reṉṟu vanta puṇarcci muṭittavāṟu kaṇṭukoḷka.
"ellā meṉṉu miṟuti muṉṉar
vaṟṟeṉ cāriyi muṟṟat tōṉṟum"	(tol. eḻut. 189)
eṉṟavaḻi, ellāmeṉṉum viravuppeyaruḷ uyartiṇi kūṟiṉ vaṟṟuccāriyi peṟāmiyāṉ atu peṟṟuvanta aḵṟiṇik kūṟē muṭittalum atu; piṟavum aṉṉa.
iṉi, iṅṅaṉam muṭipukōṭalaṉṟi, āṇṭuk kuṟiyiṭutalum, āṭciyuṅ kuṟiyīṭum oruṅkunikaḻntatu piṉṉarāṭcikkaṇ vārāmiyum vantavaḻip piṟavaṟṟoṭu kūṟutalum ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum. avi,
"avvaki yoṉpatum viṉiyeñcu kiḷavi"	(tol. col. 230)
eṉa muṭintataṉi mīṇṭum,
"neṟippaṭat tōṉṟu meñcuporuṭ kiḷavi"	(tol. col. 430)
eṉak kuṟiyiṭutal eccaviyaluḷ vantatāyiṉum muṉṉar akkuṟiyīṭu vantatiṉmiyiṉ atu vārātateṉappaṭumākaliṉ aḵtu īṇṭuk kuṟiyiṭuta lāyiṟṟu. viṉiyiyaluṭ peyareccameṉṟu āḷutalum atu.
'vaṇṇacciṉiccol' 'muṟṟuccol' eṉpaṉavuṅ kuṟṟiya likarattip puḷḷiyeṉṟalum āṭciyuṅ kuṟiyīṭum oruṅku nikaḻntaṉa vākaliṉ aviyum iṉi vārāmiyāṉ vantuḻi vantuḻi avvāṟu āṇṭāṉeṉpatu.
iṉip, puḷḷiyeṉamēl āḷa vārātataṉip puḷḷiyeṉṟu āḷvaṉavaṟṟoṭu mayakkaṅ kūṟutaleṉpatu,
"avitāṅ,
kuṟṟiya likaraṅ kuṟṟiya lukara
māyta meṉṟa
muppāṟ puḷḷiyu meḻuttō raṉṉa"	(tol. eḻut. 2)
eṉpuḻik kuṟṟiyalikaram puḷḷiyeṉṟu yāṇṭum āḷa vārāmiyāṉum atutāṉ avvaḻi varavēṇṭutalāṉum aṅṅaṉam  puḷḷiyeṉṟu āḷavaruṅ kuṟṟukarattōṭum āytattōṭum uṭaṉkūṟutalāyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam uṭaṉkūṟākkāṟ 'puḷḷiyuṅ kuṟṟikaramu'meṉac cūttiram peṟutal vēṇṭuvatāvāṉ cellumeṉpatu. iṉi,
"ā ē ō ammūṉṟum viṉāa"	(tol. eḻut. 32)
eṉavum,
"māṟuko ḷeccamum viṉāvu miyamum"	(tol. eḻut. 290)
eṉavuṅ kūṟuvaṉavum avi; eṉṉi? iṭiccollōttiṉuḷ viṉāveṉ ṟōtāta ākāram vārātatuṭaṉ āṇṭu viṉāveṉṟu ōtiya ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ,
"ā ē ō ammūṉṟum viṉāa"
eṉṟu uṭaṉ kūṟiṉamiyāṉum, avvāṟu iṭiccollōttiṉuḷ eṭuttōtāta māṟukoḷeccattōṭum aiyattōṭum āṇṭōtiya viṉāviṉiyum eṇṇiṉiyum eḻuttōttiṉuḷ vitantuṭaṉ kūṟiṉamiyāṉumeṉpatu. itu nōkkip pōlum,
"īṟṟaci yivvin tēkāram"	(tol. col. 259)
eṉa iṭiccolliṉi eḻuttuccāriyi peytōtiya karuttāṉē ipporuṇmikoḷḷa vippāṉāyiṟṟumeṉpatu.
maṟṟu ivi etiratu pōṟṟalākāvōveṉiṉ,atu poruṭ paṭik kaṇṇateṉavum, ivi āṭciyuṅ kuṟiyīṭum paṟṟiyatoru pakuti yeṉavuṅ kūṟi viṭukka. aḵtēṟ, kuṟiyīṭṭāṉ īṇṭārāyāṉōveṉiṉ, -  ivi uttivakiyākalāṉum, atutāṉē uttiyeṉap paṭumātalāṉum, ataṉi īṇṭu ārāyāṉeṉpatu.
(7) vantatukoṇṭu vārātatuṇarttal: piṉṉoruvaḻi vantatu koṇṭu muṉvārātatoru poruḷ aṟiyavittal; atu,
"ñāṅkark kiḷanta valleḻuttu mikum"	(tol. eḻut. 204)
eṉpatu.
"eñciya mūṉṟum mēlvantu muṭikku
meñcuporuṭ kiḷavi yilaveṉa moḻipa"	(tol. col. 439)
eṉa, vantatu koṇṭu mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa ēḻeccattiṟkum mēl vantu muṭikkuñ col vārātataṉi varumeṉṟuṇarntu koḷḷavittami yiṉ ituvumatuvēyāyiṟṟu.
"āyiru tiṇiyi ṉicikkumaṉa collē"	(tol. col. 1)
eṉṟavaḻit tiṇiyeṉṉum peyar epporuṭkum eytuvittal oru cūttirattuḷḷē kōṭaliṉ ataṉi ataṟku iṉameṉṟu koḷḷap paṭum; piṟavum aṉṉa.
(8) muntu moḻintataṉ ṟalitaṭumāṟṟu:muṉṉorukāṟ kūṟiya muṟiyaṉṟip piṉṉorukāl tali taṭumāṟākak kūṟutal; atu,
"paṉṉī ruyiru moḻimuta lākum"	(tol. eḻut. 49)
eṉavum,
"uyirmey yallaṉa moḻimuta lākā"	(tol. eḻut. 50)
eṉavum, uyirum meyyum niṟuttamuṟiyāṉaṉṟik,
"katana pamaveṉu māvin teḻuttu
mellāvuyiroṭuñ cellumār mutalē"	(tol. eḻut. 51)
eṉavum meypaṟṟi variyaṟi kūṟutalum,.
"ellā moḻikku miṟutiyu mutalu
meyyē yuyireṉ ṟāyī riyala"	(tol. eḻut. 103)
eṉa muṟkūṟiya muṟipiṟaḻak kūṟutalumāyiṉa.
meyyum uyirum paṟṟi vitantu variyaṟuppiṉum aḵtu iraṇṭaṟ kuñ cellumeṉṟu kōṭaṟkum, iṉi iyalpuvakiyāṉ īṟātal orutaliyāka uṭiyaṉa meyyeṉṟaṟkum avvāṟu kūṟiṉāṉeṉ ṉāmōveṉiṉ, aṅṅaṉamē akkaruttiṉāṉaṉṟē 'ottakāṭci'yeṉak kuṟṟattōṭu oppumikūṟi maṟṟup poruḷ payattaliṉ amiyumeṉṟu koḷvāmāyiṟṟeṉpatu.
iṉi,
"mūṉṟutali yiṭṭa muppatiṟ ṟeḻuttiṉ."	(tol. eḻut. 103)
eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa iṉameṉappaṭum.
"puḷḷi yillā vellā meyyum.;'	(tol. eḻut. 17)
eṉpatu mey muṟkūṟiṉamiyiṉ ituvum itaṟku utāraṇameṉappaṭum.
maṟṟu, mayaṅkakkūṟalōṭu itaṉiṭi vēṟṟumi yeṉṉi yeṉiṉ, - aḵtu,
" iṉṉē vaṟṟē,"	(tol. eḻut. puṇa. 17)
eṉa niṟuttamuṟiyāṟ kūṟātu maṟṟatuvē paṟṟāka maṟṟoru poruḷ koḷḷap paṭum. iḵtu aṉṉataṉṟi aṅṅaṉam mayaṅkakkūṟalvēṇṭum poruṇmittāki varumeṉpatu; aḵtēl, itu māṟukoḷakkūṟa leṉṉuṅ kuṟṟamākātō veṉiṉ, ākātu; eṉṉi? itu muṟkūṟiya poruḷiṉi māṟupaṭāmiyāṉum niṟuttamuṟi talitaṭumāṟa vikkun tuṇiyākalāṉumeṉpatu.
(9) oppakkūṟal :oṉṟu kūṟuṅkāl iruporuṭkuṟittateṉṟu iraṭṭuṟacceytal. atu,
"iṉṉi ṉikara māvi ṉiṟuti
muṉṉark keṭuta lurittu mākum."	(tol. eḻut. 120)
eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa.
"viṉiyeñcu kiḷaviyu muvamak kiḷaviyum"	(tol. eḻut. 204)
eṉavum,
" aṉṉa veṉṉu muvamak kiḷaviyum."	(tol. eḻut. 204)
eṉavum iṉamallaṉavaṟṟi uṭaṉeṇṇutalum,
" māmarak kiḷaviyu māvu māvum."	(tol. eḻut. 231)
eṉa māṭṭeṟiyuṅkāl vēṟuvēṟu vitiyuṭiyaṉavaṟṟi oruṅku māṭṭeṟitalum, āṇṭu nāṉku valleḻuttiṉiyum uṭaṉkōṭalum, nilimoḻittoḻiloṭu varumoḻit toḻilum oppakkoṇṭu māṭṭeṟitalum pōlvaṉa oppakkūṟaleṉpataṉ pakutiyāy aṭaṅkumeṉpatu.
(10) orutali moḻital: ōr atikārattiṟ collaṟpālataṉi vēṟu atikārattuc colli avvilakkaṇamē āṇṭuṅ koḷḷa vittal ; atu,
" a i uam mūṉṟum cuṭṭu."	(tol. eḻut. 31)
eṉavum,
" āṇum peṇṇu. maḵṟiṇi yiyaṟki."	(tol. eḻut. 303)
eṉavum ivi eḻuttatikārattuk kūṟiyavāṟṟāṉē collatikārat tuḷḷum avvilakkaṇaṅ koḷḷa vittamiyiṉ appeyarttāyiṟṟu.
uṭampoṭupuṇarttuc colluvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum. eṉṉi? vitiyallātatu vitipōla maṟṟoruvaḻic cēṟaliṉ.
(11) taṉkōṭkūṟal:collātaṉa piṟavuḷavāyiṉum annūṟku vēṇṭuvatē koḷvaleṉṟal; atu.
" aḵtiva ṇuvalā teḻuntupuṟat ticikku
meyteri vaḷiyici yaḷavunuvaṉ ṟiciṉē."	(tol. eḻut. 102)
eṉavum,
" cakarak kiḷaviyu mavaṟṟō raṟṟē
aai auveṉu mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē."	(tol. eḻut. 61)
eṉavum,
" kuṟṟeḻut tintu moḻiniṟi pilavē."	(tol. eḻut. 44)
eṉavum,
" pāṭaluṭ payiṉṟavi nāṭuṅ kāli."	(tol. akat.  3)
eṉavum varum.
ivvāṟṟāṉē,
" aḷapiṟ kōṭa lantaṇar maṟittu."	(tol. eḻut. 102)
āyiṉum atu kūṟilēṉeṉavum, iyaṟcolliṟkallatu nilimoḻi yākkaṅ kūṟēṉeṉavum,
" vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷum ārāyval"
eṉṟu pukuntāṉ pāṭaluṭ payiṉṟa vaḻakkē kūṟavaleṉavuṅ kūṟutaliṉ avi taṉkōṭkūṟutalām.
iṉi, oṉpatumayakkattuṇ meymmayakkaṅkūṟi oḻintaṉa kūṟāmiyum, viṉittokiyum paṇputtokiyum eṭuttōti muṭiyāmiyum,
" nāṭaka vaḻakkiṉu mulakiyal vaḻakkiṉum
pāṭal cāṉṟa pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam."	(tol. akat. 53)
paṟṟip poruḷilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuvaleṉṟalum, perumpāṉmiya ilakkaṇa vaḻakkeṉpa eṉṟalum, ataṉāṉē ciṟupāṉmiya mayakkameṉṟalum, am ām em ēm eṉpaṉa mutalāyavaṟṟi aṅṅaṉam pakuttōtutaṟ payaṉum, avi viṉiyiṉṟi avviṉiceytāṉmēl nikaḻkiṉṟa kūṟātalē paṟṟi,
" viṉiceyaṉ maruṅkiṟ kālamoṭu varunavum."	(tol. col. 252)
eṉṟu iṭiccollōṭu ōtutalum pōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉap paṭum; eṉṉi? ivi tāṉ kūṟuvaleṉṟu pukuntavaṟṟuḷḷum oru poruḷāṉavaṟṟi varintukoṇṭamiyiṉ avaṟṟu iṉameṉap paṭṭaṉa.
(12) muṟipiṟaḻāmi:kāraṇamiṉṟit tāṉ cila poruḷ eṇṇi niṟuttiyapiṉṉar ammuṟi piṟaḻntāluṅ kuṟṟamilvaḻiyum am muṟiyiṉiyē ilakkaṇamākac collutal ;atu,
" peyar ai oṭu ku
iṉ atu kaṇviḷi yeṉṉu mīṟṟa."	(tol. col. vēṟ. 3)
eṉa niṟutta muṟi paṟṟi eḻuvāyvēṟṟumi iraṇṭāvatu mūṉṟāva teṉap peyarkoṭuttal.
" akaramuta ṉakara viṟuvāy."	(tol. eḻut. 1)
eṉavuṅ,
" kikkiḷi mutalāp peruntiṇi yiṟuvāy"	(tol. akat. 1)
eṉavum, vaḻakkiyalāṉum ilakkaṇavakiyāṉum uḷporuḷi vitantē eṇṇi niṟuttātavaḻiyum,
"avaṟṟuḷ,
a i u e o"
eṉavum,
" avaṟṟuḷ,
naṭuva ṇintiṇi naṭuvaṇa toḻiya."	(tol. akat. 2)
eṉavum muṟipiṟaḻāmaṟkōṭal ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum.
(13) piṟaṉuṭampaṭṭatu tāṉuṭampaṭutal:uḷporuḷ aṉṟāyi ṉum vaḻakkiyalāṟ koḷ poruḷ ituveṉak kūṟutal ; atu,
" paṇṭiyaṉ maruṅkiṉ marīiya marapu " 	(tol. col. 91)
eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉavaṟṟāṉ aṟika.
iṉi,
" mīyeṉa marīiya viṭamvari kiḷaviyum"	(tol. eḻut. 250)
eṉpuḻi mēleṉpatu ilakkaṇameṉṟu eṭuttōtiyataṉi uṭkoṇṭu mīeṉṟataṉi marūuveṉṟamiyiṉ aḵtu iṉam eṉappaṭum; eṉṉi? mutaṉūluṭ koṇṭavāṟaṟintu maṟṟu ataṉit tāṉ iccol iṉṉavāṟāyiṟṟeṉṟu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟātu uṭampaṭutaliṉ. maṟṟu ataṉi iṉameṉpa teṟṟukku? itutāṉē piṟaṉuṭampaṭṭatu tāṉuṭampaṭṭatākātō, mutaṉūlāciriyaṉ uṭampaṭṭa tākaliṉeṉiṉ, - aṟṟaṉṟu; mutaṉūlāciriyaṉip piṟaṉeṉṉāmiyāṉum, mutaṉūliṉ vaḻittākiya nūluḷ avaṉ uṭampaṭṭatoṉṟu uṭampaṭuvaleṉṟu uttivakiyāṟ koḷḷātu muḻuvatūuṅ koḷvaṉākalāṉum avvāypāṭu kūṟalākāteṉpatu.
iṉi, mutaṉūluḷmēleṉpatu mīyeṉa marīiyiṟṟeṉṟu vitantōtappaṭṭataṉi aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟātu mīyeṉa marīiyiṟṟeṉṟu vāḷātu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, ataṉi iṉameṉṟu koṇṭāmeṉpatu. eṉavē,  īṇṭup piṟaṉeṉṟatu vaḻakkiṉuḷḷōri nōkkiyāyiṟṟu; eṉṟāṟku, oḻinta vaḻinūlāciriyarip piṟaṉeṉṟāṉeṉṉāmō veṉiṉ, avaruṭampaṭṭatu uṭampaṭṭataṉāṟ payantateṉṉi? mutaṉūliṟ piṟaḻāmi nūlceyyumāyiṉeṉa maṟukka.
allatūum icinūluṅ kūttanūlum paṟṟip piṟaṉkōṭkūṟa leṉpataṉāṟ piṟaṉeṉṉiṉaṉṟi, iyaṟṟamiḻk kaṇṇē mutaṉūlāciri yaṉip piṟaṉeṉṉāṉeṉpatu. aḵtēl, vaḻakku nūl ceyvāṉ avvaḻakkiṉi vaḻaṅkuvārip piṟaṉeṉṉumō veṉiṉ,-ilakkaṇa mum vaḻakkumeṉa iraṇṭaṉuḷ iḵtilakkaṇamātaliṉ avvaḻakkiṉuḷ vaḻaṅkuvārip piṟaṉeṉṟal amiyumaṉṟōveṉpatu; eṉṟāṟku, avaruṭam paṭṭatu uṭampaṭutal uttivakiyeṉpa teṟṟukku? aḵtu iyalpēyaṉṟō veṉiṉ. aṅṅaṉam marīiyiṉum ilakkaṇameṉpatu tiripillātākaliṟ, ṟiripupaṭum vaḻakkiṉi uṭampaṭutal ilakkaṇamaṉ ṟāyiṉum, atu vaḻakkiṉuḷḷār vēṇṭumāṟṟāṉ ilakkaṇamēyā meṉpatu karuttu ; allākkāl, eḷḷēpōla eṭkuppiyun taṉtaṉmiyāṉ uḷporuḷākalum vēṇṭumaṉṟōveṉpatu.
(14) iṟantatu kāttaleṉpatu: muṟkūṟiyavoru cūttirapporuṇ miyip piṉṉoru cūttirattāṉ vilakkutal ; atu,
" pālkeḻu kiḷavi nālvarkku murittē"	(tol. poru. 5)
eṉak kūṟiya piṉṉar.
" naṭpi ṉaṭakki yāṅkalaṅ kaṭiyē"	(tol. poru. 6)
eṉṟāṟpōla vilakkutal.
nūṟpuṟaṉaṭiyum ottuppuṟaṉaṭiyum atikārappuṟaṉaṭiyum  pōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum. avi,
" īṟiyaṉ maruṅki ṉiviyivaṟ ṟiyalpeṉak
kūṟiya kiḷavip pallā ṟellām
meyttalip paṭṭa vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi
ottavi yuriya puṇarmoḻi niliyē"	(tol. eḻut. 171)
eṉavum,
" puḷḷi yiṟutiyu muyiriṟu kiḷaviyuñ
colliya valla vēṉiya vellām"	(tol. eḻut. 202)
eṉavum varuvaṉa pōlvaṉa.
(15) etiratupōṟṟal:varukiṉṟa cūttirap poruṇmikkēṟpa vēṟoruporuḷ muṟkūṟutal ; atu,
" īṟāku puḷḷi akaramoṭu niliyum"	(tol. eḻut. 161)
eṉa varukiṉṟataṉi nōkki,
" āṟa ṉurupi ṉakarak kiḷavi
yīṟā kakaramuṉik keṭutal vēṇṭum"	(tol. eḻut. 115)
eṉak kūṟutalum,
" pallōr paṭarkki muṉṉili taṉmi"	(tol. col. 222)
eṉa varukiṉṟataṉi nōkkit,
"taṉmic collē yaḵṟiṇik kiḷaviyeṉṟu
eṇṇuvaḻi maruṅkiṉ viravutal variyār"	(tol. col. 43)
eṉa vaḻuvamittalum pōlvaṉa.
iṉi, oru cūttirattuḷḷē,
" īṟāku puḷḷi yakaramoṭu niliyum "	(tol. eḻut. 161)
eṉa varuvataṉi nōkkik kūṟiya,
" kuṟṟoṟ ṟiraṭṭa lilli	(tol. eḻut. toki. 19)
eṉṟal ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum.
'(16) moḻivāmeṉṟal:orupayaṉōkki muṟkūṟuva leṉṟal; avi,
" uṇarak kūṟiṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟum"	(tol. eḻut. 35)
eṉavum,
" kaṭappā ṭaṟinta puṇariya lāṉa"	(tol. eḻut. 37)
eṉavum,
"avvē,
ivveṉa vaṟitaṟku meypeṟak kiḷappa"	(tol. col. 121)
eṉavum varum.
iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiyataṉāṟ payaṉ : kuṟṟukaravīṟṟukkaṇaṉṟip puṇar moḻik kuṟṟikara miṉṟeṉpatūum, iṉip puṇarmoḻik kuṟṟikaram peruvaraviṟṟeṉpatūum aṟivittalāyiṟṟu.
" avvē,
