ivveṉa vaṟitaṟku meyppeṟak kiḷappa"	(tol. col. 121)
eṉpatūum,
'tām viḷi koḷḷā' (co. 126) eṉṟatūum avviḷiyilakkaṇa meṉṟaṟ payam paṭa vantaṉa; īṟṟup potuviṉāṉ viḷiyēṟkumeṉṟu ōtappaṭṭa peyaruḷ ivi viḷiyēlāveṉa viḷivilakkal vēṇṭuta lāṉum, nī vārāyeṉpatu iyalpuviḷiyaṉṟeṉa vilakkutalum viḷiyilakkaṇamē yātalāṉu meṉpatu.
iṉi,
" meyppeṟak kiḷantu poruḷvarin ticikkum
aikāra vēṟṟumit tiripeṉa moḻipa"	(tol. eḻut. 157)
eṉavum,
" melleḻut tiyaṟki colliya muṟiyāṉ
ṅañanama veṉṉu moṟṟā kummē"	(tol. eḻut. 143)
eṉavum varuvaṉa iṉameṉappaṭum. eṉṉi? aviyum muṉṉarp pōy moḻivaṉavaṟṟi avāvi niṉṟamiyi ṉeṉpatu.
(17) kūṟiṟṟeṉṟal:muṟkūṟiyatōr ilakkaṇattiṉi maṟṟoru poruṭkum vitikkavēṇṭiyavaḻi, avvilakkaṇattiṉi mīṭṭuṅkūṟātu mēṟkūṟiyavāṟṟāṉē koḷkaveṉpāṉ avi kūṟiṉēṉeṉṟu nekiḻntu pōtal; avi,
" kikkiḷi mutalā veḻuperun tiṇiyu
muṟkiḷan taṉavē muṟineṟi vakiyiṉ"	(tol. cey. 185)
eṉavum,
"eṇvaki yiyaṉeṟi piḻiyā tāki
muṉṉuṟak kiḷanta muṭiviṉa tatuvē"	(tol. cey. 205)
eṉavum varum. ivi akapporuṭkum puṟapporuṭkum ōtiya ilakkaṇañ ceyyuḷuḷḷum avvāṟeytuvittavāṟāyiṟṟu.
"mutaleṉap paṭuva tāyiru vakittē"	(tol. akat. 17)
eṉavum,
"meypeṟu vakiyē kikōḷ vakiyē"	(tol. cey. 188)
eṉavum,
"māttiri yaḷavu meḻuttiyal vakiyu
mēṟkiḷan taṉṉa veṉmaṉār pulavar"	(tol. cey. 2)
eṉavum varuvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum ; eṉṉi? kikkiḷi peruntiṇikkum iviyē mutaleṉṟalāṉun, tiṇiyuṅ kikōḷum pōlvaṉa puṟapporuṭkuṅ kōṭaṟpayaṉpaṭa vittamiyāṉum, muṟkūṟiya māttiriyum eḻuttum piṟavāṟṟāṟ ceyyuṭkup payaṉpaṭu māṟṟāṉ vēṟupaṭṭavallatu avi mēṟkūṟiya māttiriyum eḻuttumē eṉṟamiyāṉumeṉpatu.
(18) tāṉ kuṟiyiṭutal:ulakukuṟiyiṉṟit taṉṉūluḷḷē vēṟu kuṟiyiṭṭāḷal ; avi,
uyartiṇi aḵṟiṇiyeṉavum, kikkiḷi peruntiṇi yeṉavum, colliṟkum poruḷiṟkum vaḻakkiyalāṉaṉṟi āciriyaṉ tāṉē kuṟiyiṭutal. vaṇṇac ciṉiccoṉ, muṟṟuviṉiccol leṉa āṭciyuṅ kuṟiyīṭum oruṅku nikaḻntaṉavum, viṉiyeñcukiḷaviyum peyareñcukiḷaviyumeṉṟu āṇṭu,
"... āyī rintum
neṟippaṭat tōṉṟu meñcuporuṭ kiḷavi"	(tol. col. 430)
eṉak kuṟiyiṭutalum atu.
"iraṇṭākuvatē
aiyeṉap peyariya vēṟṟumik kiḷavi"	(tol. col. 72)
eṉavum,
" mūṉṟaṉu mintaṉun tōṉṟak kūṟiya
ākkamoṭu puṇarnta vētuk kiḷavi"	(tol. col. 93)
eṉavum peyar koṭuttal ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum.
(19) orutaliyaṉmi: uṟuti allāta nili.
"vēṟṟumi yāyiṉ valleḻuttu mikum"	(tol. eḻut. 225)
eṉa virittāṟ pōlātu, ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiyoḻital :
atu,
" kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉa rāytap puḷḷi
yuyiroṭu puṇarntaval lāṟaṉ micittē"	(tol. eḻut. 38)
eṉṟakkāl yāṇṭum orutaliyāka vārātu, varuñāṉṟu varuvatu āṇṭeṉṟu koḷḷavittal.
iṉic, collōttiṉuḷ vēṟṟumiyeṉṟu ōtappaṭṭa eṭṭaṉuḷ eḻuvāy vēṟṟumiyiṉiyum viḷivēṟṟumiyiṉiyum 'vēṟṟumi'yeṉṉātu eḻuttōttiṉuḷ 'alvaḻi'yeṉṟal pōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum.
(20) muṭintatu kāṭṭal:collukiṉṟa poruṭku vēṇṭuvaṉa vellāñ collātu tollāciriyar kūṟiṉāreṉṟu collutal.
aḵtu 'oṉṟaṟivatu uṟṟaṟiva'teṉṟaṟkum, ' iraṇṭaṟivatu uṟṟuñ cuvittum aṟivatu' eṉṟaṟkum muṟimiyāṟ cūttirañ ceyvāṉ avi avvāṟātaṟkuk kāraṇaṅ kūṟātu, 
" nēriti ṉuṇarntōr neṟippaṭut tiṉarē"	(tol. mara. 27)
eṉa muṭintatu kāṭṭal.
' nuṇṇiti ṉuṇarntōr kaṇṭa vāṟē'	(tol. eḻut. 7)
eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum.
(21) āṇi kūṟal:ivvāciriyaṉ karuttu ituveṉak koḷḷa vittal : atu,
" ammi ṉiṟuti kacatak kālit
taṉmey tirintu ṅañana vākum"	(tol. eḻut. 129)
eṉak karuviyōttiṉuṭ cāriyi makaram pakara varumoḻikkaṇ tiriyā teṉṟu pōyc ceykiyuḷ valleḻuttiṉatu iyaṟki melleḻut tātal aṟivittaṟku 'alvaḻi yellā melleḻuttāku'meṉac collutal pōlvaṉa. ṅañana ātaṟkum iḵtokkum.
" ompaṭi yāṇiyiṟ kiḷantavaṟ ṟiyalāṟ
pāṅkuṟa vuṇarta leṉmaṉār pulavar"	(tol. col. 396)
eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum.
maṟṟut taṉkōṭkūṟalōṭu itaṉiṭi vēṟṟumi yeṉṉi yeṉiṉ, atu tantirañ ceyyum pakutikkaṇṇatu ; iḵtu aṉṉataṉṟip puṇarccikkaṭ ciṟappuṭiya nilimoḻi varumoḻit tiripu pōlā teṉṟu karuviyākiya iṭiccoṟkāyiṉ ittuṇi yamiyumeṉṟu āṇi ceytaliṉ appeyarttāyiṟṟu.
(22) palporuṭkēṟpiṉ nallatu kōṭaloru cūttirattuṭ payanta coṟṟoṭar palaporuṭ kēṟṟatāyiṉum nallatu koḷkiṉṟāreṉak karuti avvāṟu ceytal : avi,
" oruva reṉṉum peyarnilik kiḷavi
yirupāṟku murittē teriyuṅ kāli"	(tol. col. 193)
eṉṟavaḻi, oruvareṉpatoru col taṉkaṇṇē irupālāriyun taḻīi niṟkumeṉavum, atu karuviyāka irupālāriyuñ collappaṭumeṉavum kavarttavaḻi,
" taṉmi cuṭṭiṟ paṉmik kēṟkum"	(tol. col. 194)
eṉpataṉoṭu paṭuttunōkka irupālāriyum orucol taṉkaṭ ṭaḻīi niṟṟalē nallateṉṟu koḷḷavittal.
" numme ṉiṟuti yiyaṟki yākum"	(tol. eḻut. 187)
eṉpuḻi, eḻuttu vikāramuṭiyataṉik kaḷintu cāriyikkaṇṇē iyaṟki kōṭal ataṉpāṟpaṭum. itaṉi ēṟpuḻik kōṭaleṉavum orupuṭic cēṟaleṉavuñ collupa.
(23) tokuttamoḻiyāṉ vakuttaṉar kōṭal:oru vāypāṭu eṭuttōtap palavāypāṭu ataṟku vantu pūṇumeṉṟu vakuttuk koḷḷavittal ; atu,
"ceytu ceyyūc ceypu ceyteṉac
ceyyiyar ceyyiya ceyiṉceyac ceyaṟkeṉa
avvaki yoṉpatum viṉiyeñcu kiḷavi"	(tol. col. 230)
eṉavum,
"kāppi ṉoppi ṉūrtiyi ṉiḻiyiṉ"	(tol. col. 73)
eṉavum oru vāypāṭu tokuttu ōtiyavāṟṟāṉē pala vāypāṭu vakuttuk koḷḷavippateṉpatu, ceyteṉpataṉi nakku vantu kaṇṭu niṉṟu pāṭip pōy eṉap palavākkutalum cey eṉṉum mutaṉiliyāṉ muṟṟuvāypāṭu palavumākkutalum, iṉik 'kāppi' ṉeṉṟavaḻip
"purattal puṟantara lōmputal pōṟṟal"
eṉap palavāka vakuttaluṅ kaṇṭukoḷka.
" uruveṉa moḻiyiṉum"	(tol. col. 24)
eṉṟalum,
" itaṉa tituviṟṟu"	(tol. col. 111)
eṉṟalum pōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum.
(24) maṟutalicitittut taṉṟuṇipurittal:oru poruḷiṉi oruvaṉ vēṟupaṭakkoḷvatōr uṇarvutōṉṟiyakkāl av vēṟupāṭṭiṉi māṟṟit tāṉ tuṇintavāṟu avaṟkum aṟivuṟuttal. itu maṟutali citittaluṭimiyiṉ vāḷātu taṉkōṭ kūṟaliṉ aṭaṅkātāyiṟṟu.
" mūvaḷa picitta lōreḻut tiṉṟē"	(tol. eḻut. 5)
eṉavum,
" nīṭṭam vēṇṭi ṉavvaḷa puṭiya"	(tol. eḻut. 6)
eṉavum,
" kuṉṟici moḻivayi ṉiṉṟici niṟikkum" 	(tol. eḻut. 41)
eṉavum mūṉṟu māttiriyāṉ ōreḻuttu uṇṭeṉpāri vilakki vaḻakkiyalāṉ illiyeṉṟu taṉṟuṇipu urittavāṟu. uyirmey vēṟeḻuttaṉṟeṉpāṉ,
" puḷḷi yillā vellā meyyu
uruvuru vāki yakaramō ṭuyirttalum"	(tol. eḻut. 17)
eṉavum,
"meyyiṉ vaḻiya tuyirtōṉṟu niliyē"	(tol. eḻut. 18)
eṉavum vēṟupaṭātu kūṟutalum atu.
vaḻakkiṉuḷ aṟiyātārmāṭṭu vēṟupōl icippaṉavāyiṉum aṉṟeṉṟu kūṭṭamuṇarttiṉamiyiṉ,
" moḻippaṭut ticippiṉun terintuvē ṟicippiṉum
eḻuttiya ṟiriyā"	(tol. eḻut. 53)
eṉṟalum iṉameṉa ataṉpāṟ cārtti uṇarappaṭum. piṟavum aṉṉa.
(25) piṟaṉkōṭkūṟal:taṉṉūlē paṟṟākap piṟanūṟku varuvatōr ilakkaṇaṅ koḷḷumāṟu kūṟutal ; atu,
"ariyaḷapu kuṟukaṉ makara muṭittē
iciyiṭa ṉarukun teriyuṅ kāli"	(tol. eḻut. 13)
eṉavum,
"aḷapiṟan tuyirttalu moṟṟici nīṭalum
. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . 
narampiṉ maṟiya veṉmaṉār pulavar"	(tol. eḻut. 33)
eṉavum,
" paṇṇit tōṉṟiya veṇṇāṉku poruḷuṅ
kaṇṇiya puṟaṉē nāṉāṉ keṉpa"	(tol. meyp. 1)
eṉavum, ivi avvan nūluṭ koḷḷumāṟṟāṉ amiyumeṉṟavā ṟāyiṉa.
" aḷapiṟ kōṭa lantaṇar maṟittu"	(tol. eḻut. 102)
eṉpatu ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum; eṉṉi? avar matampaṟṟi ivaṉ koḷvatoru payaṉiṉṟākaliṉeṉpatu.
(26) aṟiyātuṭampaṭal:tāṉōtiya ilakkaṇattiṉ vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉa tāṉ aṟintilaṉākak kūṟi ataṉpuṟattuc ceyvatoru puṟaṉaṭi; avi,
" kiḷanta valla vēṟupiṟa tōṉṟiṉuṅ
kiḷantavaṟ ṟiyalā ṉuṇarntaṉar koḷalē"	(tol. col. 119)
eṉavum,
" varuva vuḷaveṉiṉum vantavaṟ ṟiyalāṉ
tiriviṉṟi muṭitta ṟeḷḷiyōr kaṭaṉē"	(tol. cey. 243)
eṉavum varum.
iṟantatu kāttalōṭu itaṉiṭi vēṟṟumiyeṉṉiyeṉiṉ, iṟantateṉpatu tāṉ tuṇintu collappaṭṭa poruḷākal vēṇṭum. iḵtu aṉṉataṉṟic collappaṭāta poruṇmēṟṟāki atuvun tāṉ tuṇiyappaṭāta poruḷākit tāṉ nūlceyta kālattē uḷḷavaṟṟuḷ oḻiyappōyiṉa uḷavāyiṉuṅ koḷka veṉpāṉ, 'vēṟupiṟa tōṉṟi ṉum' eṉavum, 'varupavuḷaveṉiṉum' eṉavun tēṟātu ataṉ aiyappāṭu tōṉṟac collutaliṉ itu vēṟeṉka. muḻutuṇarntāṟkallatu paḻutaṟac collalākāmiyiṉ aḵtu aviyaṭakkiyal pōlvatōr uttiyeṉakkoḷka.
" kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉart taṉṉuru iraṭṭalum
aṟiyat tōṉṟiya neṟiyiya leṉpa"	(tol. eḻut. 160)
eṉavum,
" celvaḻi yaṟital vaḻakkat tāṉa"	(tol. eḻut. 312)
eṉavum varuvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉa vuṇarka.
(27) poruḷiṭiyiṭutal:vēṟṟumip poruḷiṉic colkiṉṟa poruṇmikkiṭiyē peytu collutalum, colkiṉṟa poruṭku iyipuṭiya taṉi āṇṭuc collātu iṭiyiṭṭupōyp piṟitoruvaḻic collutalum pōlvaṉa; avi,
" muṟippeyark kiḷavi yēyoṭu varumē"	(tol. col. 138)
eṉṟāṟpōlac colkiṉṟa uyartiṇip peyariṭi viravup peyar peyturittalum, vaḻuvamikkiṉṟa kiḷaviyākkattuḷ ōtātu eccaviyaluṭ pōkki,
" aviyal kiḷavi maṟittaṉar kiḷattal"	(tol. col. 443)
eṉac collutalum pōlvaṉa.
"tāṉeṉ peyaruñ cuṭṭumutaṟ peyarum
yāṉeṉ peyarum viṉāviṉ peyaru
maṉṟi yaṉittum viḷikō ḷilavē"	(tol. col. 139)
eṉṟavaḻit, tāṉeṉṉum viravup peyariṉi iṭippeytu vilakkutalum, vēṟṟumik kiḷaviyōttiṉuṭ kūṟātu eñci niṉṟa vēṟṟumit toki mutaliya tokikaḷi eccaviyaluṭ collutalum pōlvaṉavum iṉam eṉa ataṉpāṟ cārtti yuṇarka.
(28) etirporuḷuṇarttaltāṉ kūṟiya ilakkaṇattiṟ cila piṟkālattut tiripupaṭiṉum paṭumeṉpatu, mutaṟkāla mutaṉūluṅ koṇṭuṇarnta āciriyaṉ, etirkālattu varuvatu nōkki ataṟkēṟṟatōr ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟippōtal :
" makarat toṭarmoḻi mayaṅkutal varinta
ṉakarat toṭarmoḻi yoṉpaḵ teṉpa
pukaraṟak kiḷanta vaḵṟiṇi mēṉa"	(tol. eḻut. 82)
eṉṟu ōtiya ilakkaṇattuc cila piṟkālattuk kuṟiya varutal etirporu ḷeṉappaṭum. ataṉit tāṉ uṇarntu,
" kaṭico lillik kālattup paṭiṉē"	(tol. col. 453)
eṉṟu kūṟavē muṟkūṟiya poruḷiṉi vaṟpuṟuttalām atuveṉpatu. iṅṅaṉan tiripupaṭutal aṟintē kūṟutalāṉum, etirporuḷākalāṉum, iḵtaṟiyātu uṭampaṭutaliṉaṭaṅkā tāyiṟṟu.
" poyyum vaḻuvun tōṉṟiya piṉṉar
aiyar yāttaṉar karaṇam"	(tol. kaṟ. 4)
eṉpatūum ataṉpāṟ paṭum.
(29) colliṉeccañ colliyāṅ kuṇarttalcolli ṉāṟṟalāṟ peṟappaṭum poruḷiṉiyum eṭuttōtiyāṅkuk koḷḷa vittal; aḵtu,
" eñciya vellā meñcuta lila"	(tol. eḻut. 77)
eṉpuḻi, ellā meṉpataṉi eccappaṭuttu ataṟkākātaṉa irupattāṟu koṇṭavaḻiyum ataṉi eṭuttōtiṟ ciṟappiṉṟeṉṟu koḷḷaṟka eṉpatu itaṉ karuttu.
eccaviyaluṭ kūṟiya poruḷ akattōttuk kūṟiya poruḷōṭu oppak kūṟutalum iṉameṉa ataṉpāṟpaṭum.
'moḻinta poruḷōṭoṉṟa vavvayiṉ moḻiyātataṉi muṭṭiṉṟu muṭitta'lōṭu itaṉiṭi vēṟṟumiyeṉṉiyeṉiṉ, aḵtu eṭuttōtappaṭṭa poruṭkaṇṇateṉavum, iḵtu eṭuttōtti ṉōṭu oppa eccappaṭa vittukkoḷḷum ilakkaṇam eṉavum ataṉō ṭitaṉiṭi vēṟṟumiyuṇarka.
(30) tantu puṇarnturittal: uḷporu ḷallataṉi uḷatu pōlat tantu kūṭa uṇarttal ; avi,
" aḷapeṭi yacinili yākalu murittē"	(tol. cey. 16)
eṉavum,
" kuṟiliṇi yukara malvaḻi yāṉa"	(tol. cey. 4)
eṉavuṅ kūṟuṅkāl aḷapeṭiyiṉi eḻuttup pōlavuṅ kuṟṟukarattiṉik kuṟṟeḻuttup pōlavum ōtutal pōlvaṉa.
" meyyī ṟellām puḷḷiyoṭu niliyal"	(tol. eḻut. 104)
eṉavum,
" meyyuyir nīṅkiṟ ṟaṉṉuru vākum "	(tol. eḻut. 139)
eṉavum mēṟkūṟiya puḷḷiyiṉiyē orupayaṉōkki mīṭṭum puḷḷi peṟumeṉak kūṟutal ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum.
" kuṟumiyu neṭumiyu maḷaviṟ kōṭaliṟ
ṟoṭarmoḻi yellā neṭṭeḻut tiyala"	(tol. eḻut. 50)
eṉpatēpaṟṟi,
" neṭiyataṉ muṉṉa roṟṟumey keṭutalum"	(tol. eḻut. 160)
eṉpuḻit toṭarmoḻiyi neṭṭeḻuttākkik kōṭalum atu.
(31) ñāpakaṅkūṟalcūttirañ ceyyuṅkāl ataṟku ōtiya ilakkaṇa vakiyāṉē cilvakiyeḻuttiṉ ceyyuṭṭākavum nāṭutaliṉṟip poruṇaṉi viḷaṅkavuñ ceyyātu aritum peritumāka nalintu ceytu maṟṟum ataṉāṉē vēṟupala poruḷuṇarttal : atu,
" meypeṟu marapiṟ ṟoṭivaki tāmē
. . . uṇarntici ṉōrē"	(tol. cey. 101)
eṉa varutal. itaṉāṟkoṇṭa poruṇmiyellāñ ceyyuḷiyaluṭ kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.
" eppeyar muṉṉarum valleḻuttu varuvaḻi
yakki ṉiṟutimeym miciyoṭuṅ keṭumē"	(tol. eḻut. 128)
eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum. piṟavum aṉṉa.
(32) uyttukkoṇṭuṇartaloruvaḻi oru poruḷ col liyakkāl ataṉkaṇṇē maṟṟoru poruḷiṉiyuṅ koṇarntu koṇṭaṟiyumāṟu tōṉṟac ceytal : avi,
" neṭṭeḻut timparun toṭarmoḻi yīṟṟuṅ
kuṟṟiya lukaram vallā ṟūrntē"	(tol. eḻut. 36)
eṉa iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum kūṟi, ataṉāṉ īṟu ākkaṅkōṭaluṅ,
" kuṟṟiya lukara muṟippeyar maruṅki
ṉoṟṟiya nakaramici nakaramoṭu mutalum"	(tol. eḻut. 67)
eṉa mutalākkaṅ kūṟi, ataṉāṉē iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum atuvēyeṉak kōṭalum pōlvaṉa.
peyarviṉikku ōtiya ilakkaṇam oḻinta coṟkuñ cevvaṉañ cellumeṉṟu kōṭal ataṟku iṉameṉappaṭum; piṟavum aṉṉa.
maṟṟu nutaliyataṟitalōṭu itaṉiṭi vēṟṟumiyeṉṉi yeṉiṉ,- atu vāḷātu payamila kūṟiyatu pōlak kūṟiyavaḻi ivvāṟu kūṟiyatu iṉṉa karuttup pōlumeṉṟu aṟiya vittalum, urivaki yāṉum nutaliyataṟiyac collutalumām; iḵtu aṉṉataṉṟi, accūttiran taṉṉāṉ oruporuḷ payantataṉṟaliyum piṉṉoruporuḷ peṟa varutaliṉ itu vēṟeṉpatu.
maṟṟu ñāpakaṅ kūṟalōṭu itaṉiṭi vēṟṟumi yeṉṉi yeṉiṉ, - payamillatu pōlavum aritum peritumākavum iyaṟṟi eḷituñ ciṟitumāka iyaṟṟātu cūttirañ ceytal vēṟupāṭē nimitta mākat tōṟṟik koḷvatoru poruḷpeṟa vittaliṉ ituvum vēṟeṉappaṭumeṉpatu.
meyppaṭa nāṭic colliya alla piṟa avaṇ variṉum - uyttuk koṇṭuṇartaloṭu mēṟkūṟiya muppattiraṇṭum iccūttirattuḷ eṭuttōtiya poruḷvakiyāṉ ārāyntu collappaṭṭaṉavaṉṟē? aṅṅaṉañ collātaṉa piṟavum innūluḷ variṉum; colliya vakiyāṟ curuṅka nāṭi - uttivakiyeṉa vakuttukkoṇṭu ōtiya muppattiraṇṭu pakutiyāṉ aṭaṅkumāṟu ārāyntu; maṉattiṉ eṇṇi mācaṟat terintu koṇṭu- ōtappaṭṭa utti palavum oruṅku variṉum uḷḷattāṉ teḷḷitiṉ ārāyntu mayakkan tīra vēṟu vēṟu terintu vāṅkik koṇṭu;  iṉattil cērtti uṇarttal vēṇṭum- muppattiraṇṭākum ēṟṟa vakiyāṉ iṉañ cārtti maṟṟavaṟṟi iṉṉatituveṉap peyar kūṟalvēṇṭum, aṅṅaṉan tokaniṉṟa vaḻiyum vēṟuvēṟu koṇṭu; nuṉittaku pulavar kūṟiya nūlē - talimi cāṉṟa āciriyarāṟ kūṟappaṭṭa nūl e-ṟu.
eṇṇiya muppattiraṇṭu mallaṉa tōṉṟiṉum avaṟṟuḷ aṭakki, avitām oruṅku variṉum vēṟu terintu iṉantōṟuñ cērttutali avāvi niṟkum īṇṭu ōtiyanūleṉpatu karuttu.
colliya alla piṟa avaṇ varumāṟum, avi colliyavaki yāṟ curuṅka nāṭi iṉattiṟ cērttumāṟum mēṟ kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.
iṉi, oruṅku palavutti vantavaḻi uḷḷattāṉ teḷḷiti ṉeṇṇit terintu koṇṭu iṉattiṟ cērttutal varumāṟu :
" aṉṉa piṟavuṅ kiḷanta valla
paṉmuṟi yāṉum parantaṉa varūum
uricco lellām poruṭkuṟi kūṭṭa
iyaṉṟa maruṅki ṉiṉitteṉa vaṟiyum
varamputamak kiṉmiyiṉ vaḻinaṉi kaṭippiṭittu
ōmpaṭi yāṇiyiṟ kiḷantavaṟ ṟiyalāṟ
pāṅkuṟa vuṇarta leṉmaṉār pulavar"	(tol. col. 396)
eṉṟavaḻi,
" aṉṉa piṟavuṅ kiḷanta valla
paṉmuṟi yāṉum parantaṉa varūu 
muriccol"
eṉpatu, iṟantatu kāttalām; eṉṉi? iciyuṅ kuṟippum paṇpumē yaṉṟic cīrttiyum puṉiṟum pōlvaṉa vēṟum uḷa eṭuttōtap paṭṭaṉa eṉavum, eṭuttōtātu iciyuṅ kuṟippum paṇpumaṉṟic cēṇeṉṟun toṟuveṉṟum varuvaṉa uḷaveṉavuṅ kūṟiṉamiyiṉ,
" varamputamak kiṉmiyiṉ"
eṉpatu etirporu ḷuṇarttalum aṟiyātuṭaṉpaṭalumām.
" ōmpaṭi yāṇi"
eṉpatu āṇi kūṟalām. iṅṅaṉam oru cūttirattuṭ pala vantavaḻi,  oṉṟē uttiyeṉṟuṇarātu, maṉattiṉeṇṇi mācaṟat terintukoṇṭu ivvāṟṟāṉ iṉattiṟ cērttuka eṉṟāṉ āciriyaṉeṉpatu.
" marapunili tiriyā māṭciya vāki
yuripaṭu nūṟā miruvaki iyala"	(tol. mara. 93)
eṉpatu mutalāka ittuṇiyum vaḻakkunūlilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiṉāṉ, eḻunilatteḻunta ceyyuḷiṉ (tol. cey. 476) ituvum oṉṟākaliṉ itaṉic ceyyuḷiyaliṟ kūṟiṉāṉ. īṇṭu marapu kūṟumvaḻi oḻinta ceyyuṭkup pōlātu, nūliṟkup poruṭpaṭiyākiya yāppuk kuṟṟaṅka ḷum uttivakiyuṅ kūṟal vēṇṭutaliṟ kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu. aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟākkāṟ ' pāṭṭiṉ marapu' kaṭṭuri pōlavuṅ,
"kaṭṭaḷi araṅkiṉṟi vaṭṭā ṭiyatu" 
pōlavum, varampiṉṟi vēṇṭiyavāṟu nūlceytal vilakkiṉṟāvāṉ cellum ; cellavē, eḻucīrāṉākiya muṭukiyalaṭiyāṉum ettuṇiyum nīṇṭatoru vañcippāṭṭāṉum piṟavum vēṇṭiyavāṟuñ cūttirañ ceytalum, oruvaṉ peyariṉi avvac cūttirac ceyyuḷuṭ cārttu vakiyāṟ peytu kūṟalum, uṇarttappaṭum poruḷiṉi mutaṉūluṭ kiṭantavāṟu pōlātu marapunili tiriyac ceytalum, nālvaki yāppoṭu māṟupaṭac ceytalum, vēṇṭiyavar vēṇṭiyavāṟṟāṟ cila poruḷkaḷi vēṟu tōṟṟikkoṇṭu nūlceytalum vilakkiṉṟākal paṭumeṉpatu.
iṉi uttivakiyum avvāṟē iṉṟiyamiyātaṉa veṉappaṭum. eṉṉi? uṇarttappaṭum poruḷ ituveṉṟu aṟivittalum, eḻuttuc coṟporuḷeṉap pakuttuk koṇṭu atikārañ ceytalum, uṇarvu pulaṅkoḷḷumāṟṟāṉ tokuttuk kāṭṭalum, maṟṟu avaṟṟi vakuttuk kāṭṭiyavaḻip payamila kūṟaleṉṟu karutāmal atutaṉṉā ṉoru payampaṭac ceytalum, mutaṉūlāyiṉa vellām nūṟporu ḷuṇartaṟkuk karuviyākaluñ cūttiraccurukkattuk kētuvākalum uṭimiyiṉ aviyum vēṇṭappaṭṭaṉa veṉpatu.
iṉi, avaṟṟi ittuṇiyeṉa variyaṟākkāl, ettuṇiyum palavāki ikantōṭutalum, vaḻinūṉ mutaṉūl vaḻittaṉṟākalum paṭum; muppattumūṉṟeḻut teṉṟāṉāyiṉum, appeṟṟittaṉṟi aṟupattā ṟākak koḷḷavittāṉeṉṟu utti kūṟalum uṭampaṭuvāṉātal cellumeṉa maṟukka.
iṉi,
" nuṉittaku pulavar kūṟiya nūl"
eṉṟataṉāṉē, pāyiracceyyuḷuñ cūttiracceyyuḷum āciriyappāvum veṇpāvum peṟṟu varutalum, avvirupāvum peṟṟu varutaṟkaṇ, pāṭṭuppōla  ellāvuṟuppum peṟutaṟkuc cellā veṉpatūum, māttiri mutalākap pāvīṟāka vanta patiṉoṉṟum vaṇṇaṅkaḷuḷ ēṟpaṉa koḷḷiṉuṅ koḷḷumeṉavum, yāppuṟuppuk koḷḷuṅkāl īṇṭōtiya marapuṅ koḷḷappaṭumeṉavuṅ koḷka. urikkuṅ kāṇṭikikkum ivaṟṟuḷḷum ēṟpaṉa aṟintu koḷka.
iṉṉum ' nuṉittaku pulavar' eṉṟataṉāṉē, tantiramuñ cūttiramum viruttiyumeṉa mūṉṟum oruvarēyaṉṟi oṉṟu oruvar ceytalum, iraṇṭu ceytalum peṟappaṭumeṉṟalum, orucāli māṇākkarun tammāciriyarun tammiṭi nūl kēṭṭa māṇākkarum pāyirañ ceyyappeṟupa veṉṟalum, potuppāyiramuñ ciṟappuppāyiramumeṉa appāyirantām iraṇṭāmeṉṟalum, īvōṉṟaṉmiyum ītaliyaṟki yuṅ koḷvōṉṟaṉmiyuṅ kōṭaṉmarapumeṉṟa nāṉkuṟuppuṭiyatu potup pāyirameṉṟalum, ataṉvaḻiyē kūṟappaṭuñ ciṟappup pāyirantāṉ eṭṭilakkaṇa muṭitteṉṟalum, avi ākkiyōṉ peyarum vaḻiyum elliyum nūṟpeyarum yāppum nutaliya poruḷuṅ kēṭpōrum payaṉu (naṉṉūl 47) meṉappaṭumeṉṟalum, avitām nūṟkiṉṟiyamiyāveṉak koḷḷappaṭutaluṅ kūṟi muṭikka.
ciṟappuppāyirattāṉē nūlilakkaṇam oruvakiyāṉ uṇarap paṭum; potuppāyirattāṉē āciriyarum māṇākkarum nūlurittalum nūlkēṭṭalum mācaṟavaṟintu uri naṭāttuvārāka. ataṉāṉē ivi nūṉmukattiṉiṉṟu nilāvumeṉpatu.	(110)
" nuṇmā ṇuḻipula millā ṉeḻiṉalam
maṇmāṇ puṉipāvi yaṟṟu"	(kuṟaḷ . 407)
" olkāp perumpukaḻt tolkāp piyaṉaṭi
palkāṟ paravutu meḻuttoṭu
colkā maruporuṭ ṭokitikaḻ poruṭṭē"
marapiyal muṟṟiṟṟu. marapiyaṟkuppērāciriyaruri muṟṟiṟṟu.
